0097 Character creation
0098 Use standard array to distribute ability scores: 15,14,13,12,10, 8.
0099 Choose a class.
0100 Select a race from dwarf, elf (three different), halflings, and humans (four different).
0101 Select a cultural Feat from the appropriate list, and a general feat.
0102 Background: choose two skills in which you gain proficiency.
0103 Choose a second language in addition to (imperial) common.
0104 Replace non-class level progression table with table 1a (or 1b).
0105 Remove any ability increase or feats gained at 4th, 8th 12th, 16th levels.

0231 Races
0232 Dark elves
0233 Ability modifiers: +1 to dexterity and strength
0234 Proficiencies: Athletics and Perception
0235 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in compete darkness.
0236 Wraith sight: Elves can perceive creatures that dwell in the wraith-world, the ethereal world, be they spirits or ghosts, even when they are normally Invisible to the eyes of the living. Additionally, Elves are immune to the Frightened condition when the source of that condition is an undead creature.
0237 Quickness: When you move on your turn in combat, you can double your speed until the end of the turn. Once you use this trait, you can’t use it again until you move 0 feet on one of your turns.
0238 Ambush: If you surprise a creature and hit it with an attack on your first turn in combat, the attack deals an extra 2d6 damage to it.
0239 Dwarves
0240 Ability modifiers: +1 to wisdom and constitution
0241 Proficiencies: athletics.
0242 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in compete darkness.
0243 Hardy: You have advantage on saving throws against poison, and you have resistance against poison damage.
0244 Stonecunning: Whenever you make an Intelligence (History) check related to the origin⁠ of stonework, you are considered proficient in the History skill and add double your proficiency bonus to the check, instead of your normal proficiency bonus.
0245 Tough: Your hit point maximum increases by 1, and it increases by 1 every time you gain a level.
0246 Resilient: You can focus yourself to occasionally shrug off injury. When you take damage, you can use your reaction to roll a d8. Add your Constitution modifier to the number rolled, and reduce the damage by that total. After you use this trait, you can’t use it again until you finish a long rest.
0247 Forest elves
0248 Ability modifiers: +1 to wisdom and constitution
0249 Proficiencies: Stealth and Perception
0250 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in compete darkness.
0251 Wraith sight: Elves can perceive creatures that dwell in the wraith-world, the ethereal world, be they spirits or ghosts, even when they are normally Invisible to the eyes of the living. Additionally, Elves are immune to the Frightened condition when the source of that condition is an undead creature.
0252 Quick: Your base walking speed increases to 35 feet.
0253 Shadow: You can attempt to hide even when you are only lightly obscured by foliage, heavy rain, falling snow, mist, and other natural phenomena.
0254 Light footed: You can move across difficult terrain made of earth and stone without expending extra movement and without leaving any trace of your passing. You are able to grant yourself a +10 bonus to your Stealth check for up to one hour. Once you have used this ability you must finish a Long rest before doing so again. Any creature following directly in your footsteps also gain the same bonus to Stealth for the duration.
0255 Halflings
0256 Ability modifiers: +1 to dexterity and charisma
0257 Proficiencies: one of either Nature, Animal handling, Medicine, Performance or Persuasion
0258 Halflings luck: When you roll a 1 on the d20⁠ for an attack roll, ability check, or saving throw, you can reroll the die and must use the new roll.
0259 Brave: You have advantage on saving throws against being frightened.
0260 Tiny: You can move through the space of any creature that is of larger size than yours.
0261 Fury: When you score a critical hit with a melee attack, you can roll one of the weapon's damage dice one additional time and add it to the extra damage of the critical hit.
0262 Full of life: You have resistance to necrotic damage and radiant damage.
0263 Vengeance: When you damage a creature with an attack and the creature’s size is larger than yours, you can cause the attack to deal extra damage to the creature. The extra damage equals your level. Once you use this trait, you can’t use it again until you finish a short or long rest.
0264 Star elves
0265 Ability modifiers: +1 to charisma and intelligence
0266 Proficiencies: Insight and Perception
0267 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in compete darkness.
0268 Wraith sight: Elves can perceive creatures that dwell in the wraith-world, the ethereal world, be they spirits or ghosts, even when they are normally Invisible to the eyes of the living. Additionally, Elves are immune to the Frightened condition when the source of that condition is an undead creature.
0269 Inner Light: You can use your action to unleash your Inner Light, causing a searing light to radiate from your skin, pouring out of your eyes and mouth. During it, you shed bright light in a 10-foot radius and dim light for an additional 10 feet. Your transformation lasts for 1 minute or until you end it as a bonus action.
0270 Starting at 3rd level, when using your Inner Light, at the end of each of your turns, you and each creature within 10 feet of you take radiant damage equal to half your level (rounded up). In addition, once on each of your turns, you can deal extra radiant damage to one target when you deal damage to it with an attack or a spell. The damage is equal to half your level (rounded up).
0271 Humans:
0272 Human Urban: +1 charisma and 2 other ability scores. Proficient in Persuasion and 1 other skill. One general feat
0273 Human Wilderness: +1 wisdom and 2 other ability scores. Proficient in Survival and 1 other skill. One general feat
0274 Human Nomad: +1 constitution and 2 other ability scores. Proficient in Animal handling and 1 other skill. One general feat
0275 Human Rural: +1 intelligence and 2 other ability scores. Proficient in Nature and 1 other skill. One general feat
0277 Athasian:
0278 Athasian Elf
0279 Ability modifiers: +1 to dexterity and constitution
0280 Proficiencies: Athletics and Perception
0281 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in complete darkness.
0282 Quick: Your base walking speed increases to 35 feet.
0283 Elven endurance: Elves have a natural resistance to extreme temperatures and aren't adversely affected by the heat of the day or the chill of the night. They treat extreme heat or cold as if it were only very hot or cold, (see temperature effects) but suffer normally from abysmal heat, or from magical supernatural heat and cold.
0284 Elven run: As long as an elf does not have any level of Exhaustion it can potentially run for days on end. Each day that an elf continues to run, it must make a Constitution save to maintain their run: The DC is 8, +2 per day after the first. On failed save, they suffer a level of Exhaustion.
0285 Weapon Familiarity: Elves are proficient with longbows (including composite longbows), longswords, and shortbows (including composite shortbows), and treat any weapon with the word “elven” in its name as a martial weapon.
0286 Athasian Mule
0287 Ability modifiers: +1 Strength and +2 Constitution.
0288 Proficiencies: Athletics
0289 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in complete darkness.
0290 Damage reduction; Any damage from bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing nonmagical attacks is reduced by 1
0291 Endurance: Muls gain Advantage on any saving throw made to avoid Exhaustion caused by extreme weather or strenuous activity.
0292 Heritage: at character creation the Mule must choose whether it has a human or dwarven heritage. The choice determines which list of feats are available. If choosing human, the Mule must also choose which type of Human (nomad etc.).
0293 Athasian Half Giant
0294 Ability modifiers: +2 Strength, +1 Constitution, -2 Dexterity, -2 to one of Intelligence, Wisdom or Charisma. A half giant can increase its Strength score to 24 (instead of 20).
0295 Large size: You are considered Large, and occupy a space equal to 10 by 10 ft. You count as Large when determining your carrying capacity and the weight you can push, drag, or lift. You do not fit regular clothes or armour and must pay four times the amount for such items. You consume four times as much food and water as humans. You are twice the height of an ordinary human and weigh eight times as much.
0296 Overrun: When you move, you can attempt to enter the space of a creature that is size medium or smaller. When you do so, that creature must succeed on a Strength saving throw or be knocked prone as you enter their space. The DC for this saving throw equals 8 + your Strength modifier + your proficiency bonus. You can't force a creature to make this saving throw more than once per turn.
0297 Thick skin: While you are not wearing armour, your Armour Class equals 10 + your Constitution modifier.
0298 Athasian Half Elf
0299 Ability modifiers: +1 Dexterity, +1 Charisma
0300 Proficiencies: Survival and deception
0301 Adaptable: After finishing a long rest a half elf can select either Survival or Deception. Until they finish another long rest they gain Expertise in that skill.
0302 Heritage: At character creation you choose a list of feats available to one of the human subraces. You can select one feat from that or the Elf list at character creation. Your selection will also determine which list you will be able to pick feats from, when you later gain new levels.
0304 Athasian Thri-Kreen
0305 Ability modifiers: +1 to Strength and +2 to Dexterity.
0306 Proficiencies: Athletics and Survival
0307 Low light vision: You can see in dim light within 60 feet of you as if it were bright light, and in darkness as if it were dim light. You cannot see in complete darkness.
0308 Leap: You triple Your strength score when determining jumping distance and can do a long jump without a 10’ start.
0309 Chameleon Carapace: Your Armour Class equals 13 + your Dexterity modifier. As an action, you can change the color of your carapace to match the color of your surroundings, giving you advantage on Dexterity (Stealth) checks made to hide in those surroundings. You can never wear armour.
0310 Secondary Arms: You have two slightly smaller secondary arms below your primary pair of arms. The secondary arms can manipulate an object, open or close a door or container, pick up or set down a Tiny Object. They cannot wield weapons or use Magic items.
0311 Telepathy: you can’t speak the non-thri-kreen languages you know. Instead you use telepathy to convey your thoughts. You transmit your thoughts mentally to all willing creatures within 120 feet of yourself. A contacted creature who you share a known language with, can understand your words. Your telepathic link to a creature is broken if you and the creature move more than 120 feet apart, if either of you is incapacitated, or if either of you mentally breaks the contact (no action required). No matter which language you communicate in, any creature contacted will perceive your accent, intonation and choice of words as strange and alien, disclosing that the voice in their head comes from a thri-kreen.
0312 Language: Kreen can use their own language when communicating with each other, composed of buzzes, clicks, glottal stops, pops, rasps, rushes of air from the lungs accompanied by the gnashing of jaws, snaps, and whistles. Only Kreen can speak their own language, but the noises are made at such frequency that their voices carry twice the distance of a normal voice.
