0641 Human cultural feats:
0642 A Hunter’s Resolve
0643 Prerequisite: Rural
0644 Benefit: As a Reaction you may expend a Hit Dice for hit point recovery immediately, including during combat. Once used, this ability cannot be used again until you’ve taken a short or long rest.
0645 Birthright
0646 Prerequisite: Rural
0647 Benefit: You receive a +1 bonus on all Dexterity saving throws. If you are reduced to 0 hit points, but not killed outright, you are considered to have already successfully passed 1 death saving throw.
0648 Born to the Saddle
0649 Prerequisite: Nomad
0650 Benefit: You have advantage in Wisdom (Animal Handling) ability checks whenever dealing with horses. In addition, you add double your proficiency bonus to the check, instead of your normal proficiency bonus.
0651 Bowman of the Guild
0652 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0653 Benefit: All of your ranged attacks with bows have a +1 damage bonus. Additionally, after you make a successful ranged attack with a bow, you may re-roll the weapon’s damage dice and use either total. You must take a short rest or a long rest before you can use the damage re-roll ability again.
0654 Brothers to Bears
0655 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0656 Benefit: Add +1 bonus to your Constitution score. You have resistance to cold damage. Moving through difficult terrain made of ice and snow costs you no extra movement. You automatically succeed on Dexterity saving throws to avoid falling prone on slippery ice, and you are considered to weigh half as much when accounting for the weight tolerance of thin ice.
0657 Bully
0658 Prerequisite: any human
0659 Benefit: When attacking small or smaller targets you gain the effects of the cleave and power attacker feats
0660 Captain of the Guard
0661 Prerequisite: Urban
0662 Benefit: You may use your Reaction to have a nearby ally automatically hit on what was a missed attack. Once you use this ability you have to finish a short or long rest before using it again.
0663 Dark sight
0664 Prerequisite: any human
0665 Benefit: You gain the ability to see 10' in any darkness, including magical darkness.
0666 Dauntless Guardians
0667 Prerequisite: Rural
0668 Benefit: You can select this feat three times. The first time you learn "See the Unseen"; You are automatically aware of the presence or proximity of ghosts, spectres and wraiths or other types of incorporeal creatures (i.e. creatures with incorporeal movement, or etheralness. While you do not identify the type of creature, a successful DC 15 Intelligence (Religion) ability check yields additional information on its nature. The second time you learn "Strength of Will"; When an Undead creature forces you to make a Wisdom saving throw against any effect that may cause the Frightened condition, add twice your proficiency bonus to the check. If you roll either a 19 or 20 to pass the check, you can allow a number of companions equal to your Wisdom modifier to pass their own checks automatically. The third time you learn "Wraith Bane"; All of your attacks cause +2 damage to Undead creatures that can be affected by your weapons.
0672 Desperate Courage
0673 Prerequisite: Nomad
0674 Benefit: If you stand alone against an adversary with Hit Dice greater than your level or are part of a group fighting against a single adversary with Hit Dice greater than your level +3, you gain a +1 bonus to your Armour Class. You may consult the Loremaster on when this bonus may be used.
0675 Determined
0676 Prerequisite: any human
0677 Benefit: When you make an attack roll, an ability check, or a saving throw, you can do so with advantage. Once you use this ability, you can’t use it again until you finish a short or long rest.
0678 Endurance of the first men
0679 Prerequisite: Rural
0680 Benefit: When a blow reduces you to 0 hit points, but does not kill you outright, you may spend a Hit die to remain conscious and continue to take actions at the expense of a failed Death save. Any failed Death saves from the use of this Feat are removed after taking a long rest.
0681 Fierce Shot
0682 Prerequisite: Nomad
0683 Benefit: When you use a long bow, your damage is increased by both your Dexterity modifier and half your Strength modifier, rounded down.
0684 Grapevine
0685 Prerequisite: any human
0686 Special: You can choose this feat more times than once. The first time, you learn to tap the grapevine. The second (and third) time you learn either argue or inflame.
0687 Tap the grapevine: in any settled area you can spend 1 hour, while picking up news and rumours. For the next day you gain advantage on a single Intelligence ability check you wish to make within the area. After having used this ability, you can spend another 1 hour to grant yourself the same benefit.
0688 Argue: You affect a number of targets within 30' up to your Proficiency bonus + your charisma modifier. As long as none of your targets are currently threatened by you or your allies, and they are able to hear and understand what you are saying, you can roll a Persuasion check DC 12. If you succeed targets gain a disadvantage on all wisdom related skill checks and initiative checks as long as you continue speaking. You can take no other actions while speaking.
0689 Inflame: When speaking to a crowd of people for the first time, you can inflame them with passion granting them advantage on a single d20 roll until their next long rest. You affect a number of targets within 30' up to your Proficiency bonus + your charisma modifier.  Targets benefiting from this effect cannot be of equal to or higher character level than you.
0690 Great Strength
0691 Prerequisite: Nomad
0692 Benefit: Increase your Strength score by 1. You’re lifting and carrying capacities increase as if you were one size category larger. So long as you are carrying less than half of your full capacity (or are unencumbered if using the variant Encumbrance rules) you have a +1 bonus to your AC.
0693 Guard of the Tower
0694 Prerequisite: Urban
0695 Benefit: You can select this feat more than once. You learn "Shield-fence"; As long as there is at least one ally fighting by your side, you may increase the AC bonus from your shield by +1. The second time you take this feat you can choose between the following: "Long Reach"; As long as you are fighting with a martial melee weapon that is not light, you can use a Bonus action to gain reach until the end of your turn.  You can use this ability a number of times equal to half your Proficiency bonus, rounding up, before finishing a long rest. "Many Foes"; When targeted by a melee attack, you may use a Reaction to add your proficiency bonus to your Armour Class, perhaps making the attack miss. You can use this ability a number of times equal to half your Proficiency bonus, rounding up, before finishing a Long rest.
0696 Herbal Remedies
0697 Prerequisite: Rural
0698 Benefit: You can select this feat twice, each time you can choose between the following abilities:  "Fragrant Weeds"; Expend a Hit Dice and ignore the effects of one level of exhaustion – the level doesn’t go away, but you don’t suffer the penalty. For example, if you have three levels of exhaustion, you are only penalised as if you had two. "Poison Remedies"; As an Action you can expend a Hit Dice and make a DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) ability check to neutralise the effects of a single poison currently affecting a creature. During a Long rest, you can create a number of concoctions equal to your Proficiency bonus. These concoctions grant advantage on all saving throws against poison for the next hour after imbibing.
0699 Hound of deep woods
0700 Prerequisite: Rural
0701 Benefit: You have chosen a wolfhound of the deep woods to accompany you in your wanderings. The creature has the following stat modifiers: STR +1, DEX +2, CON +1, INT -4, WIS +1, CHA -2, AC 12 + PB, HP 4 + 4 times your level, Speed 40', Perception +3, Scent, Attack: Bite. Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5ft, Hit: 4 (1d6 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a small or medium creature, it must succeed at a DC 11 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on its next attack. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the Hound. If the Hound is reduced to 0 HP it is killed, and you have to spend a week in game time to find a new hound.
0702 Household Esquire
0703 Prerequisite: Nomad
0704 Benefit: At the start of a journey, you may pick one squire to accompany you. These servants are loyal to your house, and have served your family for generations, which is why they are willing to accompany you into danger. The squire has the following stat modifiers: STR +1, DEX +1, CON +1, INT +0, WIS +0, CHA +0, AC 14 (mail shirt), HP 4 + 4 times your level, Speed 30', Athletics +3, Animal handling +2, Insight +2, Perception +2. Weapon attack: +3 to hit, by weapon. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the Squire. If the squire is reduced to 0 HP it is killed, and you have to travel to a town or city, to find a new squire.
0705 Improvisation
0706 Prerequisite: any human
0707 Benefit: You gain a +1 bonus to one of either Intelligence, Wisdom og Charisma. Select one skill in which you are not proficient, which uses either Intelligence, Wisdom or Charisma as its ability modifier. You’re now considered proficient in that skill. You can change the skill after finishing a long rest.
0708 Inquisitive
0709 Prerequisite: any human
0710 Benefit: Increase one ability score of your choice by one, to a maximum of 20. You gain proficiency in one skill of your choice. Choose one skill in which you have proficiency. You gain expertise with that skill, which means your proficiency bonus is doubled for any ability check you make with it. The skill you choose must be one that isn't already benefiting from a feature, such as Expertise, that double your proficiency bonus.
0711 Inspire
0712 Prerequisite: any human
0713 Benefit: You can choose this feat more times than once. The first time you gain the ability to grant all lies within 15´ a +1 bonus to hit, damage, saving throws or AC. You must choose the same effect for all allies, and the benefit last for a number of rounds equal to your charisma modifier, minimum one. You must be able to speak, allies must be able to hear you, and you cannot cast spells while this feat is in effect. After using this ability, you have to finish a long rest before doing so again. The second and subsequent times you can choose from the following abilities: "Final stand"; all allies within 15´ gains +1d10 temporary HP. The temporary HP lasts for a number of rounds equal to your charisma modifier, minimum one, after which recipients receive one level of Exhaustion. "Inspire health"; you are able spend a move action to grant an ally a healing die. The ally must be conscious to gain this benefit and has to roll the healing die immediately. You can use this a number of times per short rest equal to your charisma modifier (minimum 1). "Inspire surge"; choose one ally within 15´. This ally can ignore the effect of Exhaustion for a number of rounds equal to your charisma modifier, minimum one. Once the effect of inspire surge runs out, the target gains one more level of Exhaustion.
0714 King’s Guard
0715 Prerequisite: Nomad
0716 Benefit: You learn one of the listed abilities when you first select this virtue. You may take this feat again to learn a different ability: "Horse-archery"; You are allowed to use a bow from horseback without provoking opportunity attacks, regardless of how close your mount is to your foes. Additionally, you gain a +1 bonus to ranged combat attack rolls while mounted on a horse. "Shield-raising"; When you are using a shield while mounted, your shield increases your Armour Class by +3. "Sword and Spear"; When you are using a long sword or a spear while mounted on a horse, you gain a +2 bonus to damage rolls.
0717 King’s Men
0718 Prerequisite: Urban
0719 Benefit: Choose two weapons from the following list: short swords, long swords, spears, and long bows. You gain proficiency in your two chosen weapons and a +1 bonus to attack rolls made with them.
0720 Magnetic personality
0721 Prerequisite: any human
0722 Benefit: You gain proficiency in the Deception or the Persuasion skills. If you are already proficient in that skill, your proficiency bonus is doubled for any check you make with that skill. You are able to secure free basic lodging for yourself and a number of companions equal to your Proficiency bonus in any civilized area.
0723 Master of Doom
0724 Prerequisite: Nomad
0725 Benefit: At the start of a session, you may choose to temporarily reduce your Wisdom by a number of points up to the total of half your Proficiency Bonus, rounded down. For each point that you reduce your Wisdom you add a number of temporary Hit Points equal to 1d8 + your Constitution modifier to a pool that lasts until the end of the session, unless reduced by damage.
0726 Merchant Prince
0727 Prerequisite: Urban
0728 Benefit:  At the start of a journey, you may pick one servant to accompany you. These servants are loyal to your house, and have served your family for generations, which is why they are willing to accompany you into danger. The servant has the following stat modifiers: STR +0, DEX +1, CON +1, INT +2, WIS +2, CHA +2, AC 11, HP 4 + 4 times your level, Speed 30', Perception +4, Insight +4, and one of either Arcana, History, Nature or Religion +4. Weapon attack: +2 to hit (+3 if light or ranged weapon), damage by weapon. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the servant. If the servant is reduced to 0 HP it is killed, you have to travel your household, or a household closely allied to yours, to replace them.
0729 Natural Watchfulness
0730 Prerequisite: Rural
0731 Benefit: While in or near (within a few miles) a forest, you have advantage on all Wisdom (Perception) ability checks. You also gain Advantage to initiative rolls.
0732 Night-goer
0733 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0734 Benefit: At night you can choose to slip into a dream-like state and leave your body in spirit form to swiftly travel along the tracks made by animals across the length and breadth of the wilderness. When using this ability, you enter a trance which can only be broken if someone else shakes you awake or you suffer damage. In this state, you can swiftly explore an area within three days of travel making you aware of settlements, rivers, and any other natural feature in the terrain. You also gain Advantage on any check to avoid being surprised while in the area. There is a chance that your Spirit form is attacked while using this ability. After waking up from your trance, roll a Wisdom saving throw DC 12. If you fail the save you suffer 1d3 levels of Exhaustion. You have no recollection about the exact encounter but are left with a feeling of having encountered something dark and vile. Once you’ve used this ability, it cannot be used again until a week has passed.
0735 Old Songs and Children’s Tales
0736 Prerequisite: Nomad
0737 Benefit: By drawing on old snippets of lore and fragments of stories you can grant yourself advantage on ability checks using one of the following skills: Arcana, History, Insight, Investigation, Medicine, Nature, Performance and Survival. You can select a new Skill each time you finish a long rest.
0738 Onward into Battle
0739 Prerequisite: Urban
0740 Benefit: If you succeed on a saving throw against any effect that causes the Frightened condition, you can spend a Reaction to have any ally within 20 feet which fails their saving throw ignore the effects of the Frightened condition.
0741 Out of Memory and Old Tales
0742 Prerequisite: any human
0743 Benefit: You may re-roll a failed skill check. You can use this ability once per PB before finishing a long rest.
0744 Over Dangerous Leagues
0745 Prerequisite: Nomad
0746 Benefit: You may ignore the effects of first two levels of exhaustion you gain from travel or extreme weather conditions. You still suffer from exhaustion but ignore the effects.
0747 Piercing Sight
0748 Prerequisite: Urban
0749 Benefit: The first time you are introduced to another Human as part of a social encounter, instead of making an ability check using a skill, you may instead fix your opponent with your steely gaze. Your target must make a Wisdom saving throw against a DC equal to 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Wisdom modifier. If it fails, you gain insight into its reasoning (if any), its emotional state and something that looms large in its thoughts, such as whatever it worries over, loves or hates. If it succeeds, its thoughts are veiled to you. Whether the target succeeds or fails, it knows that something is amiss, and feels discomforted by your gaze. Further social ability checks against the target have disadvantage.
0750 Prodigy
0751 Prerequisite: any human
0752 Benefit: You have a knack for learning new things. You gain one skill proficiency of your choice, and fluency in one language of your choice. You can select this feat more than once
0753 Royalty Revealed
0754 Prerequisite: Urban
0755 Benefit: You can spend an Action and reveal your heritage to your enemies. Any enemy within 10 feet must make a Wisdom saving throw against a DC equal to 8 + your Charisma modifier + your Proficiency bonus. Failure means they are Frightened of you for 1 minute. Any enemy Frightened in this manner thereafter will seek to attack you and your kindred whenever given the opportunity. An enemy can only ever be affected by this ability once. If any creature fails their saving throw, their despair invigorates your allies making them immediately recover 1d6 + your Charisma modifier in hit points as long as they are within sight of you.
0757 Shared manipulation
0758 Prerequisite: any human
0759 Benefit: As a move action, you can grant all friendly creatures within 30 feet who can see or hear you a bonus equal to half your proficiency bonus (rounded up) to any Intimidate or Deception checks they make.
0760 Shield-fighting
0761 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0762 Benefit: If you are fighting in melee with a shield, you can use a bonus action to make a melee combat attack with your shield. You add your Strength ability modifier to the attack roll but not to a successful damage roll. Shields cause 1d4 bludgeoning damage, buckler shields cause 1d6 bludgeoning damage.
0763 Skin-coat
0764 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0765 Benefit: You can select this feat more than once. The first time you take the feat you have resistance to non-magical piercing damage. The second time you gain resistance to non-magical bludgeoning damage. The third time you gain resistance to non-magical slashing damage. You lose the effect of this feat if you wear armour of any kind. You can only select this feat once per level (meaning humans can only select this feat once at first level).
0766 Staunching Song of the Woodmen
0767 Prerequisite: Rural
0768 Benefit: After you finish a short rest, you may sing this song to recover a Hit Die + your Constitution modifier’s worth of damage without expending one of your Hit Dice. By spending a Hit Die, you may do the same for one member of your company. Your ally roll to recover hit points based on their Hit Dice and Constitution modifier, not yours.
0771 Sterner than Steel
0772 Prerequisite: Rural
0773 Benefit: Raise your Wisdom score by 1 point. You have advantage on Wisdom saving throws against effects that would give you the Frightened condition.
0774 Strength of honour
0775 Prerequisite: any human
0776 Benefit: You can select this feat more than once. The first time you choose it you can give yourself a +2 bonus to strength for a number of rounds equal to your charisma bonus, minimum 1. After using this ability, you have to finish a long rest before doing so again. The second and subsequent times you choose from amongst the following abilities:  "Honourable will"; you can choose to add either your wisdom bonus or your charisma bonus to any Wisdom saving throw you roll. "Knightly courage"; you add half your Wisdom bonus rounding down (minimum 1) to your initiative rolls. "Sense evil"; As an Action you can detect the presence of any aberration, celestial, elemental, fey, fiend, or undead within 30 feet of you, as well as where the creature is located. Similarly, you know if there is a place or object within 30 feet of you that has been magically consecrated or desecrated. This effect lasts for as long as you maintain Concentration and do not perform any other actions. Once you use this ability you have to finish a long rest before using it again.
0777 Swordmaster
0778 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0779 Benefit: When fighting with either a short or long sword, you gain a +1 bonus to your AC.
0780 The Big and the Little
0781 Prerequisite: any human
0782 Benefit: During either a short or long rest you may expend a Hit Die healing others instead of yourself. Doing so uses their Hit Die type and constitution modifier.
0784 Trader’s Savvy
0785 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0786 Benefit: You have advantage on all Charisma ability checks when involved in direct negotiation against one other person.
0787 Traveller’s Blessing
0788 Prerequisite: Urban
0789 Benefit: You have advantage on ability checks made when making a journey to a Home in which you are welcome.
0790 Twice-baked Honey-cakes
0791 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0792 Benefit: When taking a short rest, you can serve up a number of delicious honey cakes equal to your Proficiency bonus +1. Eating your cakes removes a level of Exhaustion. Only one such cake can be eaten during a short rest, lest you gain a new level of exhaustion from stomach cramps.
0793 Warhorse
0794 Prerequisite: any human
0795 Benefit: At the start of a journey, you may have a warhorse accompany you. This warhorse is only loyal to you. Anyone besides you attempting to use the horse have Disadvantage on all Animal handling checks. The warhorse has the following stat modifiers: STR +4, DEX +1, CON +1, INT -4, WIS +1, CHA -2, AC 11, HP 5 + 5 times your level, Speed 60'. Weapon attack: +6 to hit, reach 5ft, Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage. If the horse moves at least 20 ft. straight toward a creature and then hits it with it hoof attack on the same turn, that target must succeed on a DC 14 Strength saving throw or be knocked prone. If the target is prone, the horse can make another attack with its hooves against it as a bonus action. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the warhorse. If the warhorse is reduced to 0 HP it is killed, you have to purchase a new “Combat Trained Horse” (see table 4 below) in order to replace it (the characteristics described above replaces those of a normal warhorse).
0796 Water Legs
0797 Prerequisite: Wilderness
0798 Benefit: You always have advantage on Dexterity (Acrobatics) ability checks and any saving throws involving keeping your footing, such as when fighting on a boat, a raft or other precarious or risky surfaces (for example, an Elven tree platform, a rope bridge or on an icy lake). In addition, you can always choose to substitute Dexterity for Strength when making Athletics checks involving swimming.
0799 Woeful Foresight
0800 Prerequisite: Urban
0801 Benefit: Raise your Wisdom score by 1 point. You can elect to use your wisdom modifier instead of you dexterity modifier to determine your initiative bonus.
0803 Dwarven cultural feats:
0804 Aggression
0805 Prerequisite: dwarf
0806 Benefit: As a bonus action, on the first round of any encounter you can move up to your speed toward an enemy of your choice that you can see or hear. You must end this move closer to the enemy than you started.
0807 Born alone
0808 Prerequisite: dwarf
0809 Benefit: Gain 1d8 temporary hit points when you kill or knock out an opponent. These temporary HP does not stack
0810 Dwarf Fortitude
0811 Prerequisite: dwarf
0812 Benefit: Increase your Constitution score by 1, to a maximum of 20. Whenever you take the Dodge action in combat, you can spend one Hit Die to heal yourself. Roll the die, add your Constitution modifier, and regain a number of hit points equal to the total (minimum of 1).
0813 Earth glide
0814 Prerequisite: dwarf, Murmurs of the earth
0815 Benefit: You gain the earth glide universal monster ability, allowing you to easily move through solid stone. You can pass through stone, dirt, or almost any other sort of rock, traveling at a speed of 5 feet. Part of your skin have turned to stone, giving you a disconcerting appearance. While other dwarves are not affected by this, any Charisma based Ability or Skill check made towards other humanoids are done with Disadvantage.
0816 Fury
0817 Prerequisite: dwarf
0818 Benefit: Gain a +2 bonus to attack and damage on a creature that has damaged you. This bonus last until the end of your first round after taking the damage.
0819 Grudge
0820 Prerequisite: dwarf
0821 Benefit: If you have taken any damage during your last turn, and you are wielding a simple or one-handed martial melee weapon, you can roll one of the weapon’s damage dice an additional time and add it as extra damage of the weapon’s damage type on the first successful attack you make after being damaged.
0822 Murmurs of the earth
0823 Prerequisite: dwarf, Stony step
0824 Benefit: As a move action, you grant yourself a limited form of Tremorsense. As long as you remain stationary and do not perform any action you become aware of all creatures within 30 feet that are in contact with the ground.
0825 Old hatred
0826 Prerequisite: dwarf
0827 Benefit: When you are fighting Orcs, Goblins and their kind using a melee weapon, whenever you roll for damage, you add your Proficiency Bonus to the damage.
0829 Pass of Diabas
0830 Prerequisite: dwarf
0831 Benefit: When fighting underground, you gain a +1 bonus to your Armour Class.
0832 Raven
0833 Prerequisite: dwarf
0834 Benefit: you have befriended an Intelligent raven, used by dwarves to convey messages and perform simple tasks. The raven can deliver messages anywhere within one day’s flight (around 60 miles). The Raven can either carry a written message of no more than 25 words. Alternatively, it can caw its message to any other Raven along its path or to another Dwarf. The recipient is able to convey an equally long message back, through the raven, or have the raven return with a written message. While the raven is with you, you gain a +2 bonus to your Passive perception.
0835 Roar
0836 Prerequisite: dwarf
0837 Benefit: As an Action, you can give out a thunderous roar forcing each creature of your choice within 30 feet of you to make a Wisdom saving throw (DC 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Charisma modifier). A target automatically succeeds if it can’t hear or see you. A target is unaffected by this ability if it has a higher Proficiency bonus than you, or if its HD is higher than your level. On a failed save, a target becomes frightened for 1 minute.  If the frightened target takes any damage, it can repeat the saving throw, ending the effect on itself on a success. You can use this ability a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus before having to take a long rest.
0838 Squat Nimbleness
0839 Prerequisite: dwarf or halfling
0840 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Dexterity score by 1, to a maximum of 20. Increase your walking speed by 5 feet. You gain proficiency in the Acrobatics or Athletics skill (your choice). You have advantage on any Strength (Athletics) or Dexterity (Acrobatics) check you make to escape from being grappled.
0841 Stony step
0842 Prerequisite: dwarf
0843 Benefit: Ignore all difficult terrain
0844 Tenacity
0845 Prerequisite: dwarf
0846 Benefit: Select a single creature type or ancestry. During the first round of any combat against your chosen foes, your attack rolls against any of them have advantage. When any of your chosen foes makes an opportunity attack against you, it makes the attack roll with disadvantage. Whenever you make an Intelligence (Arcana, History, Nature, or Religion) check to recall information about your chosen foes, you add double your proficiency bonus to the check, even if you’re not normally proficient.
0847 The Stiff Neck of Dwarves
0848 Prerequisite: dwarf
0849 Benefit: you get a +1 bonus to all Wisdom based Skill checks.
0850 Voice of the Mountains
0851 Prerequisite: dwarf
0852 Benefit: When underground and surrounded by solid rock, you gain advantage on Investigation checks to detect secret doors. In addition, for up to 1 minute per day, you can detect any movement underground in a one mile radius. You know the general direction of any creatures underground, and the direction in which they are moving. You will know roughly the size and number of creatures. While using this ability you enter a deep trance during which all your other senses are shut off. You cannot perform any other Actions during the trance. You have to finish a long rest before using this ability again
0855 Halfling cultural feats:
0856 Art of Disappearing
0857 Prerequisite: halfling
0858 Benefit: when surprised or at the start of the first round of combat you can slip into shadow, using your Stealth to hide. Roll a Stealth check, if you succeed you are considered Invisible until the start of your first turn.
0859 Bountiful Luck
0860 Prerequisite: halfling
0861 Benefit: When an ally you can see within 30 feet of you rolls a 1 on the d20 for an attack roll, an ability check, or a saving throw, you can use your reaction to let the ally reroll the die. The ally must use the new roll.
0862 Brave in a pinch
0863 Prerequisite: halfling
0864 Benefit: You are immune to the Frightened condition.
0865 Drivel
0866 Prerequisite: halfling
0867 Benefit: As a bonus action make a deception check DC 12. If you succeed the target is confused causing it to gain Disadvantage on its next d20 roll. You can only perform this action once per target. Finishing a Long rest lets you use this ability against the same target again.
0868 Fair shot
0869 Prerequisite: halfling
0870 Benefit: You gain a +1 bonus to attack rolls made with simple ranged weapons.
0871 Horde charge
0872 Prerequisite: halfling
0873 Benefit: If an ally moves into melee range with an enemy, you gain advantage on attack rolls against that same enemy until your ally takes its next turn.
0875 Inexplicable luck
0876 Prerequisite: halfling
0877 Benefit: Once per long rest, gain a +8 bonus to any one d20 roll before the roll is made, or a +4 bonus after the roll is made
0878 Low blow
0879 Prerequisite: halfling
0880 Benefit: When attacking a larger creature than yourself you cause and extra 1d4 extra damage per proficiency bonus on any critical hit.
0881 Resilience
0882 Prerequisite: halfling
0883 Benefit: Whenever you take the Dodge action in combat, you can spend one Hit Die to heal yourself. Roll the die, add your Constitution modifier, and regain a number of hit points equal to the total (minimum 1).
0884 Reverse feint
0885 Prerequisite: halfling
0886 Benefit: Until the beginning of your next turn all creatures have Advantage on melee attack rolls against you. During this time, you can spend a reaction whenever attacked to immediately retaliate with an attack of your own, gaining Advantage on the attack roll.
0887 Second Chance
0888 Prerequisite: halfling
0889 Benefit: Fortune favours you when someone tries to strike you. You gain the following benefits When a creature you can see hits you with an attack roll, you can use your reaction to force that creature to reroll. You can only use this ability once per creature per encounter.
0890 Squat Nimbleness
0891 Prerequisite: dwarf or halfling
0892 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Dexterity score by 1, to a maximum of 20. Increase your walking speed by 5 feet. You gain proficiency in the Acrobatics or Athletics skill (your choice). You have advantage on any Strength (Athletics) or Dexterity (Acrobatics) check you make to escape from being grappled.
0893 Stoic pose
0894 Prerequisite: halfling
0895 Benefit: By spending a full round finding a suitable location, you can hold yourself so still that you appear to be a small object such as a pile of rocks. This allows you to make a Stealth check with Advantage, without cover or concealment. You remain hidden as long as you do not move or take any other actions.
0896 Tough in the fibre
0897 Prerequisite: halfling
0898 Benefit: Your small statue belies your toughness. When regaining hit points, you may roll any Hit Die twice and add the results together.
0900 Elven cultural feats:
0901 Attuned to the wild
0902 Prerequisite: elf
0903 Benefit: Select one type of terrain from the ranger class’s favoured terrain class feature (except urban). While you are in your selected terrain type, you gain bonus healing dice equal to your proficiency bonus.
0904 Deadly Archery
0905 Prerequisite: elf
0906 Benefit: When making ranged attacks with a short bow, you do not suffer disadvantage for long range, nor for attacking within 5 feet.
0907 Dreams of the Ancient Days
0908 Prerequisite: elf
0909 Benefit: Increase your Wisdom score by +1. You can choose to gain Advantage on a single d20 roll. You have to complete a short or long rest before using this ability again.
0911 Elf Tricks
0912 Prerequisite: elf
0913 Benefit: When you first select this Feat, you learn "Elf-lights": You know how to make a torch, or a lamp burn with a peculiar flame that attracts all mortals who see it. Any living humanoid creature who sees its flame must make a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or try to get near it by any means possible. You may snuff out the light at will, even from a distance. After you snuff out the light any affected creature has disadvantage on all they do for the following round. The second time you take this feat you learn "Enchanted Sleep": Having used an Elf-light to attract an unwary victim, you can enchant him into slumber. The closest living creature with a number of Hit Dice equal to or lower than your level immediately drops into a slumber when you snuff out the light. Once you have used Elf lights you have to finish a long rest before doing so again.
0914 Elven Accuracy
0915 Prerequisite: elf
0916 Benefit: Increase your Dexterity, Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma score by 1, to a maximum of 20. Whenever you have advantage on an attack roll using Dexterity, Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma, you can reroll one of the dice once.
0917 Engulf
0918 Prerequisite: elf
0919 Benefit: Whenever you are in close vicinity of open flame (within 5'), you can cause the flames to wreathe you until the end of your next turn. The flames don’t harm you or your possessions, and they shed bright light out to 30 feet and dim light for an additional 30 feet. The flames will even light up magical darkness for its duration. While the flames are present, any creature within 5 feet of you take 1d8 fire damage.
0920 Fade away
0921 Prerequisite: elf
0922 Benefit: When you take damage, you can use a reaction to magically become invisible until the end of your next turn or until you attack, deal damage, or force someone to make a saving throw. Once you use this ability, you can’t do so again until you finish a short or long rest.
0923 Life’s blood
0924 Prerequisite: elf
0925 Benefit: As an Action, you may elect to take damage equal to one Hit Die, and healing another creature you touch the same amount. A creature cannot be healed by this ability more than once before they have to finish a long rest.
0926 Resolute
0927 Prerequisite: elf
0928 Benefit: As a bonus action you can make an additional saving throw against a fear effect currently affecting you. If you succeed on the check, you remove the Frightened condition, and can do the same with an ally within 30’, also currently under the effect of the Frightened condition. You can continue to do this until an ally fails the saving throw.
0929 Shadow Bane
0930 Prerequisite: elf
0931 Benefit:  When you are fighting in melee combat against Aberrations, Undead and Fiends you have a +2 bonus to your attack rolls. Conditionally you gain this bonus against all creatures which are under the control of an Aberration, Undead or Fiend.
0932 Shadowy dash
0933 Prerequisite: elf
0934 Benefit: While using the Stealth to hide you can move at such speed that you remain hidden even while passing through open areas as long as you end up either in cover or in an area where you can use your Stealth skill to hide at the end of your move.
0938 Teleportation
0939 Prerequisite: elf, shadowy dash
0940 Benefit: As an action you can teleport to any location within your movement rate if there is nothing physically blocking your way (such as bars, walls and doors). You must take a short or long rest before doing so again
0941 Tenacity
0942 Prerequisite: elf
0943 Benefit: As a Reaction, when taking damage that would normally drop you to 0 HP, you can immediately expend a Healing die to regain 1d8 Temporary hit points and perform a move Action that does not provoke attacks of opportunity.
0944 Weightless
0945 Prerequisite: elf
0946 Benefit: You are able to levitate to the extent that you become almost weightless, allowing you to cross any surface more solid than air and water. You gain Advantage on any Athletics checks for climbing, and any Acrobatics checks used for balancing.
0948 Half Giant cultural feats:
0949 Aggression
0950 Prerequisite: half giant
0951 As a bonus action, you can move up to your speed toward an enemy of your choice that you can see or hear. You must end this move closer to the enemy than you started.
0952 Booming Bellow
0953 Prerequisite: half giant
0954 Your Strength or Constitution score increases by 1. You can use your action to cause all creatures adjacent to you to make a Constitution saving throw, with a DC equal to 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Constitution modifier. On a failure they are deafened for 1 minute and are stunned until the start of your next turn. Once you use this feature you can't do so again until you complete a short or a long rest.
0955 Crushing Strike
0956 Prerequisite: half giant
0957 When you take this feat, once per turn after you hit a target with a melee attack, you deal extra damage equal to your Proficiency bonus. If the target fails a Dexterity save DC 8 + your proficiency bonus + Strength or Constitution it falls prone. You can use this feat a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus per long rest.
0958 Crushing Throw
0959 Prerequisite: half giant
0960 Your Strength score increases by 1. You double the range for any thrown item. Once per turn, when you make a successful ranged attack with a thrown weapon using Strength, you can add your Proficiency bonus to the attack’s damage roll.
0961 Destructive Bulk
0962 Prerequisite: half giant
0963 Your Strength or Constitution score increases by 1. When you move at least 30 feet straight on your turn before you reach an opponent you gain the following benefits; when you damage a creature with a melee attack you may add your Constitution modifier to the damage you deal, and you have advantage on attempts made to shove a creature.
0964 Endurance of the Depths
0965 Prerequisite: half giant
0966 Your Strength or Wisdom score increases by 1. You gain resistance to Cold and Thunder damage.
0967 Fling
0968 Prerequisite: half giant
0969 As a bonus action, you can choose one medium or smaller creature within your reach and move it to an unoccupied space you can see within 30 feet of yourself. An unwilling creature must succeed on a Strength saving throw (DC equals 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Strength modifier) to avoid the effect.
0970 Giant Stature
0971 Prerequisite: half giant
0972 Your reach increases by 5 feet
0973 Massive hands
0974 Prerequisite: half giant
0975 You have adapted to wield weapons that are more appropriate to your size. Such a weapon costs twice the listed price, and deals an extra 1d4 damage. From now on, if you wield weapons that have not been made especially for you, you attack with disadvantage.
0976 Trampling Charge
0977 Prerequisite: half giant
0978 Your Strength or Constitution score increases by 1. You can use your action to move in a straight line and attempt to push each creature, of your size or smaller, on that path. The first time you enter a creature's space during your move it must make a Strength saving throw, with a DC equal to 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Strength modifier. On a success you end your move 5 feet from the target, on a failure they are pushed ahead of you for the rest of your move. If a creature fails by 5 or more, they are also knocked prone.
0979 Vigor of the Giants
0980 Prerequisite: half giant
0981 Increase your Strength, Constitution, or Wisdom score by 1. When you are subjected to an effect that would move you at least 5 feet or give you the prone condition, you can use your reaction to steady yourself. You aren’t moved and don’t have the prone condition. Whenever you eat food as part of a short rest and spend one or more Hit Dice to regain hit points, you regain additional hit points equal to your proficiency bonus.
0985 Thri-Kreen cultural feats:
0986 Active Glands:
0987 Prerequisite: Poisonous beak.
0988 Benefit: Prerequisite: Poisonous mandibles. Benefits: You can use your poisonous bite two additional times per day. In addition, on a failed save and creature attack by Your poisonous beak gains the Poisoned condition. The creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turn, removing the Poisoned condition on a successful save.
0989 Ancestral memories:
0990 Prerequisite: -.
0991 Benefit: You gain Access to your racial memory and become more entuned with Your surroundings. This grants you access to natural abilities inured in most wild Thri-kreen. You only need half the amount of food and water to sustain yourself. In addition, you are able to burrow into sand, allowing you to escape most sandstorms and gain shelter during nighttime when in the desert. Most Thri Kreen living outside civilization learn this at an early age.
0992 Forceful Telepathy.
0993 Prerequisite: -.
0994 Benefit: You can telepathically contact any creature within range of Your Telepathy ability, even Creatures who would otherwise reject Your telepathic projections.
0995 Guardian:
0996 Prerequisite: -.
0997 Benefit: Once per round you can choose to place yourself in harm’s way and become the target of an attack originally targeted at an ally within 5’ feet. This must be declared prior to any attack roll being made.
1001 Gyth'sa:
1002 Prerequisite: -.
1003 Benefit: You add +1 to Your constitution score. You do not require sleep and can choose to remain conscious during a long rest, though you must still refrain from strenuous activity to gain the benefit of the rest.
1004 Improved Gyth'sa:
1005 Prerequisite: Gyth-sa.
1006 Benefit: You regain all spent Healing Dice during a long rest. In addition, at the end of any Long Rest you can select one ability score. You are proficient in Saving Throws using that ability.
1007 Minor Telepathy.
1008 Prerequisite: Forceful Telepathy.
1009 Benefit: You can telepathically contact any creature within sight, which you can otherwise contact with Your thri-kreen Telepathy ability.
1010 Pheromone Presence:
1011 Prerequisite: -.
1012 Benefit: You add +1 to either your wisdom or charisma scores. You gain advantage on any charisma and wisdom related skill check made against Creatures which are not humanoid.
1013 Poisonous mandibles:
1014 Prerequisite: -.
1015 Benefit: Your mandibles harden making them count as a natural weapon, which you can use to make unarmed strikes dealing 1d4 damage. Additionally when you hit with your mandibles, you can excrete poison dealing an additional 2d6 poison damage to the target. You can do this a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus and regain all expended uses upon finishing a short or long rest.
1019 Powerful limbs:
1020 Prerequisite: Stronger limbs.
1021 Benefit: Your secondary limbs can now wield weapons which lack the two-handed, heavy or special properties.
1022 Shield of the Mind.
1023 Prerequisite: -.
1024 Benefit: You add +1 to Your wisdom score. You have advantage on saving throws against the charmed and frightened conditions. Thri-keen minds are alien and psionic thri-kreen even more so.
1025 Stronger limbs:
1026 Prerequisite: -.
1027 Benefit: You are able to use your secondary limbs to wield weapons that has the light property. When you use an action to make a melee weapon attack using your main limbs, you may make one extra melee weapon attack with your secondary limbs, as part of the same action. You do not add any bonus damage from having a high strength or dexterity score. If you use your action to make a ranged weapon attack, you can use your bonus action to make one attack with a different melee or ranged weapon in a different hand.
1039 General feats:
1040 Actor.
1041 Prerequisite: -.
1042 Benefit: Increase your Charisma score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You have advantage on Charisma (Deception) and Charisma (Performance) checks when trying to pass yourself off as a different person. You can mimic the speech of another person, or the sounds made by other creatures. You must have heard the person speaking, or heard the creature make the sound, for at least 1 minute. A successful Wisdom (Insight) check contested by your Charisma (Deception) check allows a listener to determine that the effect is faked.
1043 Alert.
1044 Prerequisite: -.
1045 Benefit: You gain a +2 bonus to initiative. You can’t be surprised while you are conscious. Other creatures don’t gain advantage on attack rolls against you as a result of being hidden from you
1046 Athlete.
1047 Prerequisite: -.
1048 Benefit: When you are prone, standing up uses only 5 feet of your movement. Climbing doesn’t halve your speed. You can make a running long jump or a running high jump after moving only 5 feet on foot, rather than 10 feet.
1049 Charger.
1050 Prerequisite: -.
1051 Benefit: When you use your action to Dash, you can use a bonus action to make one melee weapon attack or to shove a creature. If you move at least 10 feet in a straight line immediately before taking this bonus action, you either gain a +5 bonus to the attack’s damage roll (if you chose to make a melee attack and hit) or push the target up to 10 feet away from you (if you chose to shove and you succeed).
1053 Chef.
1054 Prerequisite: -.
1055 Benefit: Increase your Constitution or Wisdom score by 1, to a maximum of 20. As part of a short rest, you can cook special food, provided you have ingredients and cook's utensils on hand. You can prepare enough of this food for a number of creatures equal to 4 + your proficiency bonus. At the end of the short rest, any creature who eats the food and spends one or more Hit Dice to regain hit points regains an extra 1d8 hit points. With one hour of work or when you finish a long rest, you can cook a number of treats equal to your proficiency bonus. These special treats last 8 hours after being made. A creature can use a bonus action to eat one of those treats to gain temporary hit points equal to your proficiency bonus.
1056 Crossbow Expert.
1057 Prerequisite: -.
1058 Benefit: You ignore the loading quality of crossbows with which you are proficient. Being within 5 feet of a hostile creature doesn’t impose disadvantage on your ranged attack rolls. When you use the Attack action and attack with a one-handed weapon, you can use a bonus action to attack with a hand crossbow you are holding.
1059 Crusher.
1060 Prerequisite: -.
1061 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Constitution by 1, to a maximum of 20. Once per turn, when you hit a creature with an attack that deals bludgeoning damage, you can move it 5 feet to an unoccupied space, provided the target is no more than one size larger than you. When you score a critical hit that deals bludgeoning damage to a creature, attack rolls against that creature are made with advantage until the start of your next turn.
1065 Defensive Duellist.
1066 Prerequisite: Dexterity 13 or higher, or Strength 13 or higher.
1067 Benefit: When you are wielding a melee weapon with which you are proficient and another creature hits you with a melee attack, you can use your reaction to add your proficiency bonus to your AC for that attack, potentially causing the attack to miss you.
1068 Dour-handed
1069 Prerequisite: 6th level or higher.
1070 Benefit: Raise the damage of all successful ranged attacks by half your proficiency bonus, rounded down.
1071 Dual Wielder.
1072 Prerequisite: -.
1073 Benefit: You can use two-weapon fighting even when the one-handed melee weapons you are wielding aren’t light. You can draw or stow two one-handed weapons when you would normally be able to draw or stow only one.
1074 Dungeon Delver.
1075 Prerequisite: -.
1076 Benefit: When you roll a Hit Die to regain hit points, the minimum number of hit points you regain from the roll equals twice your Constitution modifier (minimum of 2). You have advantage on Wisdom (Perception) and Intelligence (Investigation) checks made to detect the presence of secret doors. You have advantage on saving throws made to avoid or resist traps. You have resistance to the damage dealt by traps. You can search for traps while travelling at a normal pace, instead of only at a slow pace.
1077 Durable.
1078 Prerequisite: -.
1079 Benefit: When you roll a Hit Die to regain hit points, the minimum number of hit points you regain from the roll equals twice your Constitution modifier (minimum of 2).
1080 Expertise
1081 Prerequisite: -.
1082 Benefit: Choose a skill. Double your proficiency bonus on skill checks made with the chosen skill.
1083 Fell-handed
1084 Prerequisite: 6th level or higher
1085 Benefit: Raise the damage of all of your successful melee attacks by half your proficiency bonus, rounded down.
1086 Fighting Initiate.
1087 Prerequisite: Proficiency with a martial weapon.
1088 Benefit: You learn one Fighting Style option of your choice from the fighter class. If you already have a style, the one you choose must be different. Whenever you reach a level that grants the Ability Score Improvement feature, you can replace this feat's fighting style with another one from the fighter class that you don't have.
1089 Grappler.
1090 Prerequisite: Strength 13 or higher.
1091 Benefit: You have advantage on attack rolls against a creature you are grappling. You can use your action to try to pin a creature grappled by you. To do so, make another grapple check. If you succeed, you and the creature are both restrained until the grapple ends. Creatures that are one size larger than you don’t automatically succeed on checks to escape your grapple.
1092 Great Weapon Master.
1093 Prerequisite: -.
1094 Benefit: On your turn, when you score a critical hit with a melee weapon that is not a light or finesse weapon or reduce a creature to 0 hit points with one, you can make one melee weapon attack as a bonus action.
1095 Healer.
1096 Prerequisite: -.
1097 Benefit: When you use a healer’s kit to stabilize a dying creature, that creature also regains 1 hit point. As an action, you can spend one use of a healer’s kit to tend to a creature and restore 1d6 + 4 hit points to it, plus additional hit points equal to the creature’s maximum number of Hit Dice. The creature can’t regain hit points from this feat again until it finishes a short or long rest.
1098 Heavily Armoured.
1099 Prerequisite: Proficiency with medium armour.
1100 Benefit: You gain proficiency with heavy armour.
1101 Heavy Armor Master.
1102 Prerequisite: Proficiency with heavy armour.
1103 Benefit: Increase your Strength score by 1, to a maximum of 20. While you are wearing heavy armour, bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing damage that you take from non-magical weapons is reduced by 3.
1104 Hardy
1105 Prerequisite: -.
1106 Benefit: You may re-roll any 1's or 2's when rolling Hit Dice for healing during a long or short rest.
1107 Inspiring Leader.
1108 Prerequisite: Charisma 13 or higher.
1109 Benefit: You can spend 1 minute inspiring your companions, shoring up their resolve to fight. When you do so, choose up to six friendly creatures (which can include yourself) within 30 feet of you who can see or hear you and who can understand you. Each creature can gain temporary hit points equal to your level + your Charisma modifier. A creature can’t gain temporary hit points from this feat again until it has finished a short or long rest.
1110 Keen Mind.
1111 Prerequisite: -.
1112 Benefit: Increase your Intelligence score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You always know which way is north. You always know the number of hours left before the next sunrise or sunset. You can accurately recall anything you have seen or heard within the past month.
1113 Lightly Armoured.
1114 Prerequisite: -.
1115 Benefit: Increase your dexterity or strength by +1. You gain proficiency with light armour.
1116 Linguist.
1117 Prerequisite: -.
1118 Benefit: Increase your Intelligence score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You learn three languages of your choice. You can ably create written ciphers. Others can’t decipher a code you create unless you teach them, they succeed on an Intelligence check (DC equal to your Intelligence score + your proficiency bonus), or they use magic to decipher it.
1119 Lucky.
1120 Prerequisite: -.
1121 Benefit: You have a single luck point. Whenever you make an attack roll, an ability check, or a saving throw, you can spend one luck point to roll an additional d20. You can choose to spend one of your luck points after you roll the die, but before the outcome is determined. You choose which of the d20s is used for the attack roll, ability check, or saving throw. You can also spend one luck point when an attack roll is made against you. Roll a d20, and then choose whether the attack uses the attacker’s roll or yours. If more than one creature spends a luck point to influence the outcome of a roll, the points cancel each other out; no additional dice are rolled. You regain your expended luck point when you finish a long rest.
1122 Martial Adept.
1123 Prerequisite: -.
1124 Benefit: You learn two manoeuvres of your choice from among those available to the Battle Master archetype in the fighter class. If a manoeuvre you use requires your target to make a saving throw to resist the manoeuvre’s effects, the saving throw DC equals 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Strength or Dexterity modifier (your choice). If you already have superiority dice, you gain one more; otherwise, you have one superiority die, which is a d6. This die is used to fuel your manoeuvres. A superiority die is expended when you use it. You regain your expended superiority dice when you finish a short or long rest. You can select this feat multiple times.  Each time you do so, you must choose two different manoeuvres.
1125 Medium Armor Master.
1126 Prerequisite: Proficiency with medium armour.
1127 Benefit: Wearing medium armour doesn’t impose disadvantage on your Dexterity (Stealth) checks. When you wear medium armour, you can add 3, rather than 2, to your AC if you have a Dexterity of 16 or higher.
1128 Mobile.
1129 Prerequisite: -.
1130 Benefit: Your speed increases by 10 feet. When you use the Dash action, difficult terrain doesn’t cost you extra movement on that turn.
1131 Moderately Armoured.
1132 Prerequisite: Proficiency with light armour.
1133 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Dexterity score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You gain proficiency with medium armour and shields.
1134 Mounted Combatant.
1135 Prerequisite: -.
1136 Benefit: While you are mounted and aren’t incapacitated, you have advantage on melee attack rolls against any unmounted creature that is smaller than your mount. You can force an attack targeted at your mount to target you instead. If your mount is subjected to an effect that allows it to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, it instead takes no damage if it succeeds on the saving throw, and only half damage if it fails.
1137 Observant.
1138 Prerequisite: -.
1139 Benefit: Increase your Intelligence or Wisdom score by 1, to a maximum of 20. If you can see a creature’s mouth while it is speaking a language you understand, you can interpret what it’s saying. You have a +5 bonus to your passive Wisdom (Perception) and passive Intelligence (Investigation) scores Polearm Adept. When you take the Attack action and attack with only a glaive, halberd, or quarterstaff, you can use a bonus action to make a melee attack with the opposite end of the weapon. The weapon’s damage die for this attack is a d4 (bludgeoning).
1140 Piercer.
1141 Prerequisite: -.
1142 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Dexterity by 1, to a maximum of 20. Once per turn, when you hit a creature with an attack that deals piercing damage, you can re-roll one of the attack’s damage dice, and you must use the new roll. When you score a critical hit that deals piercing damage to a creature, you can roll one additional damage die when determining the extra piercing damage the target takes.
1143 Poisoner.
1144 Prerequisite: -.
1145 Benefit: You can coat a weapon in poison as a bonus action, instead of an action. You gain proficiency with the Craft (Poison) skill if you don't already have it. You double your Proficiency Bonus when calculating how much Poison you can craft. In addition, after finishing a long rest your Poisoners kit will always have enough ingredients to craft 1 poison
1146 Polearm Master.
1147 Prerequisite: -.
1148 When you take the attack action with a glaive, halberd, or quarterstaff, you can use a bonus action to make a melee attack with the other end of the weapon. The damage die is a d4 (add attribute modifiers as normal) and deals bludgeoning damage. Otherwise, this attack functions just as if you attacked with the weapon in question.
1150 While wielding a glaive, halberd, pike, or quarterstaff, other creatures provoke an opportunity attack from you when they enter your reach.
1151 Resilient.
1152 Prerequisite: -.
1153 Benefit: Increase one ability score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You gain proficiency in saving throws using the chosen ability.
1154 Savage Attacker.
1155 Prerequisite: -.
1156 Benefit: Once per turn when you roll damage for a melee weapon attack, you can reroll the weapon’s damage dice and use either total.
1157 Sentinel.
1158 Prerequisite: -.
1159 Benefit: When you hit a creature with an opportunity attack, the creature’s speed becomes 0 for the rest of the turn. Creatures within 5 feet of you provoke opportunity attacks from you even if they take the Disengage action
1160 Sharpshooter.
1161 Prerequisite: -.
1162 Benefit: Attacking at long range doesn't impose disadvantage on your ranged weapon attack rolls. Your ranged weapon attacks ignore half cover and three-quarters cover.
1163 Shield Master.
1164 Prerequisite: -.
1165 Benefit: You gain the following benefits while you are wielding a shield If you aren’t incapacitated, you can add your shield’s AC bonus to any Dexterity saving throw you make against a spell or other harmful effect that targets only you. If you are subjected to an effect that allows you to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, you can use your reaction to take no damage if you succeed on the saving throw, interposing your shield between yourself and the effect.
1166 Skill Expert.
1167 Prerequisite: -.
1168 Benefit: Increase one ability score of your choice by 1, to a maximum of 20. You gain proficiency in one skill of your choice. Choose one skill in which you have proficiency. You gain expertise with that skill, which means your proficiency bonus is doubled for any ability check you make with it. The skill you choose must be one that isn't already benefiting from a feature, such as Expertise, that doubles your proficiency bonus.
1169 Skilled.
1170 Prerequisite: -.
1171 Benefit: You gain proficiency in any combination of two skills of your choice. You can select this feat more than once
1172 Skulker.
1173 Prerequisite: Dexterity 13 or higher.
1174 Benefit: You can try to hide when you are lightly obscured from the creature from which you are hiding. When you are hidden from a creature and miss it with a ranged weapon attack, making the attack doesn't reveal your position. Dim light doesn’t impose disadvantage on your Wisdom (Perception) checks relying on sight.
1175 Slasher.
1176 Prerequisite: -.
1177 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Dexterity by 1, to a maximum of 20. Once per turn when you hit a creature with an attack that deals slashing damage, you can reduce the speed of the target by 10 feet until the start of your next turn. When you score a critical hit that deals slashing damage to a creature, you grievously wound it. Until the start of your next turn, the target has disadvantage on all attack rolls.
1178 Tavern Brawler.
1179 Prerequisite: -.
1180 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Constitution score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You are proficient with improvised weapons and unarmed strikes. Your unarmed strike uses a d4 for damage. When you hit a creature with an unarmed strike or an improvised weapon on your turn, you can use a bonus action to attempt to grapple the target.
1181 Tough.
1182 Prerequisite: -.
1183 Benefit: Your hit point maximum increases by an amount equal to your level when you gain this feat. Whenever you gain a level thereafter, your hit point maximum increases by 1 additional hit point.
1184 Weapon Master.
1185 Prerequisite: -.
1186 Benefit: Increase your Strength or Dexterity score by 1, to a maximum of 20. You gain proficiency with four weapons of your choice.
1187 Wild Talent.
1188 Prerequisite: -.
1189 Benefit: When you must select one of the powers from the list below.  You can use this power once per Proficiency Bonus, before finishing a Long Rest to replenish its uses.
1190 Aura reading: As an action, choose a creature you can see within 30 feet of you, then expend one Psionic Energy die. For a minute you can read their auras, showing you their current mood.
1191 Call to Mind: If you fail an Intelligence ability check, you can expend and roll your Psionic Energy die and add the number rolled to the check, potentially turning failure into success.
1192 Psionic sense: As an action you can expend a psionic die to sense all psionic practitioners and psionic influences in a 30 ft radius for 10 minutes.
1193 Telekinetic Manipulation: You can manipulate an object, open an unlocked door or container, stow or retrieve an item from an open container, or pour the contents out of a vial, with your mind within 15 ft. Using this ability you can’t attack, activate magic items, or carry more than 10 pounds.
1194 Telepathy: You can telepathically speak to any creature you can see within 30 feet of you. You don’t need to share a language with the creature for it to understand your telepathic utterances, but the creature must be able to understand at least one language.
1195 You regain all your uses when you finish a long rest. You can select this feat more than one time. Each time you gain 1 more use and can select a new power from the list.
1196 Special: any Athasian character gains this feat automatically at character creation. Instead of choosing from the list above, they roll randomly on table X: Athasian Wild Talents (see Additional House Rules).
1216 Spellcasting feats:
1217 Magecraft (general).
1218 Prerequisite: Charisma, Intelligence or Wisdom of 15 or higher
1219 Benefit: You have the gift (or curse) of true magic and possess the rudimentary knowledge of how to control it, enabling you to wield sorcerous power. You gain a number of spell points equal to your Proficiency bonus. As long as you have more than 0 spell points you can use your intelligence Arcana (skill) check, to create minor magical effects, akin to illusions (see below). If the check fails you lose 1 spell point and do not cast the spell. Using any such minor magic counts as an Action (and is otherwise considered as casting a spell). The effect lasts for 1 minute, unless otherwise stated.
1220 DC
1221 EFFECT
1222 Detect magic
1223 You sense the presence of magical auras (8), number of auras (10), strength and location (12), type of magic (14), creature type of spellcaster (16) or whether spellcaster has more HD than you (18). The base range for this is 15 feet. The final effects of this cantrip are decided retroactively, after rolling your Arcana skill check.
1224 Ghost sound
1225 You create weak background noise (8), the noise of someone walking (10), talking (12), shouting (14), roaring animal (16), roaring beast (18) or thunder (20). The base duration for this effect is immediate to 1 round.
1226 Palming
1227 You make an item weighing up to 1 pound, which you carry on your person appear in your hand (8). Add (+2) per additional pound that the item weighs. This effect is immediate, and if you succeed on your check by more than 5 you can cast this spell by spending a Bonus Action instead.
1228 Prestidigitation
1229 You chill, warm, clean or change the colour of up to 1 foot cube of matter (8). Add (+2) per additional 1 foot cube. This effect is immediate.
1230 Light
1231 You can generate light equal to candlelight (8). Add (+2) per 5 foot radius of additional light. If you roll higher than 8, you can elect to reduce the light emitted.
1232 Minor illusion
1233 You create a visible illusion up to 1 foot cube (8). Add (+2) per additional 1 foot cube.
1234 Message
1235 You telepathically whisper a 25-word message to a creature within 15 feet (8). Add (+2) per additional 15 feet. Assuming the message isn’t blocked by any barrier the target creature hears the message and can whisper a reply that you hear. Add +10 to the DC to message any creature you are familiar with. The effect ends after 1 round, or after the target replies.
1240 Spellcraft (general).
1241 Prerequisite: magecraft
1242 Benefit: Choose one School of the First Magnitude. You are able to cast all Incantations from that School. When taking this Feat you also gain 1 spell point. You always have to know at least one more spell school of a lesser magnitude than of a higher. This means you cannot learn a second magnitude spell school before you’ve learned at least two of the first magnitude. Every time you take the Spellcraft feat, you can also opt to change a single School you’ve already mastered. You can only select a new School of the same Magnitude.
1243 Special: You can take this Feat a number of times equal to your intelligence bonus +1 (minimum 1).
1244 Example: after first having taken the Magecraft Feat Yocto takes the Spellcraft Feat allowing him access to a single School. Yocto chooses Afflict. This allows him to cast mild curse, fling curse, aura of doom, blinding rays and cloud of pestilence incantations.
1245 Arcane focus (general).
1246 Requirements: magecraft.
1247 Benefit: you can choose to replace Intelligence with either Wisdom or Charisma for determining the effects of your incantations. You have to choose which ability score to replace intelligence with when you first take this Feat. Any roll related to spellcasting is replaced by the new score.
1248 Spellfocus (general).
1249 Requirements: magecraft, spellcraft.
1250 Benefit: Choose one spell school that you know. Any Arcana check made to cast these spell gain advantage.
1257 Agonizing Raze (defiling)
1258 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement
1259 Benefits: Any creature within range of your defiling radius have disadvantage on any ability check or attack roll they make until the end of their next turn.
1260 Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1
1261 Controlled Raze (defiling)
1262 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement
1263 Benefits: Your defiling radius doubles. You can specify one 5 ft. square per 5 ft. radius of your defiling circle per proficiency bonus that is unaffected by your energy gathering. Creatures in unaffected squares do not suffer the adverse effects of being caught in the defiling circle, nor is vegetation in that square turned to ash.
1264 Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1
1265 Distant Raze (defiling)
1266 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement, controlled raze
1267 Benefits: You can move the centre of your defiling circle (on the ground) up to 10 feet per proficiency bonus, within sight, in effect moving the entire circle of defiling.
1268 Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1
1269 Efficient Raze (defiling)
1270 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement
1271 Benefits: You reduce the area which is needed to fuel your spells by half.
1272 Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1
1275 Exterminating Raze (defiling)
1276 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement
1277 Benefits: Any creature within the range of your defiling take 1d4 damage per spell point used. Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1. You can select this feat more than once. each time the damage increase by +1d4 per spell point used
1278 Fast Raze (defiling)
1279 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement
1280 Benefits: When defiling, you can add +5 to the arcana skill check, by expanding the radius of your defiling aura by +10 feet.
1281 Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1
1282 Lure of power (defiling)
1283 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft, uncompromising defilement
1284 Benefits: You select the Spellcraft feat as a cultural feat
1285 Normal: the spellcraft feat is a general feat.
1286 Special: Reduce you constitution or charisma score by 1
1287 Uncompromising Raze (defiling)
1288 Prerequisite: magecraft, spellcraft
1289 Benefit: You no long suffer exhaustion when defiling.
1290 Special: Reduce your constitution and charisma score by 2. After having taken this feat you can choose to not defile when casting a spell, but doing so will cause you a level of Exhaustion. The GM should allow you to replace this feat (and other defiling feats) with another, but doing so should take considerable time and effort, perhaps requiring the spellcaster to seek out some special purifying being, place or object.
1292 Spellcasting
1293 Some creatures possess the gift (or curse) of true magic and some have even acquired the rudimentary knowledge of how to control it, enabling you to wield sorcerous power. Spells are divided into schools and incantations. Schools represent a broader category of magic, while incantations are the specific spells available under each school. Those that possess the Magecraft Feat can use their Arcana skill to create minor magical effects, akin to illusions (see below), while those that also possess the Spellcasting Feat gain access to a specific Spell School and can use their knowledge to create more potent effects through incantations.
1294 Spellcasting DC: Arcana (skill) check against a DC depending on the potency of the spell.
1295 Spell Save DC = 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Intelligence modifier
1296 Learning spells
1297 When taking the Spellcasting (General) Feat you learn a single School, and you can manipulate it’s effects enough to create all the Incantations listed under that School. For example, if you learn the First Magnitude Spell School “Beast”, you can cast both the Sense animals and Speak with animals Incantations. Any later School you wish to cast Incantations from are learned through taking the Spellcasting feat again (see description).
1298 The Gamemaster may require your character to have access to a spellbook, the direct supervision of a teacher, or complete substantial research before taking the Spellcasting feat.
1299 Casting and augmenting incantations
1300 All incantations have a visible Somatic and an observable Verbal component. You can make the use of these components undiscernible, but you take Disadvantage on any Arcana spell checks when doing so. All Spell Schools include a Base result and a number of Enhance effects which you can use to augment the Base effect. Most Spell Schools have a basic description which only affects the caster, or has no effect unless you augment the Base result by increasing range, area of effect, duration etc. In addition, all Schools have unique Enhance effects which you can add to the Base result or the Incantations (in some cases an augmented Effect will replace the Base result). By combining the Base and Enhance effects you can create Incantations, which can be augmented with increased duration, range, area of effect etc. (see below for full list and cost). Most forms of augmentation will increase the difficulty to cast the Incantations.
1301 Some Schools will give illogical Base results, such as Air which causes a gust of wind. Using Incantations from these Schools without augmentation will only result in the Incantation affecting the caster. For instance, using Air without augmentation might be described as the casters hair and clothes shifting and moving, or perhaps the casters abdomen inflates, while Afflict without augmentation only curses the spellcaster. Some spells must therefore be augmented in order to have any discernible effect at all or prevent them from having adverse effects. Other examples are spells such as Charm and Spirit.
1302 Having learned a School you are able to cast all Incantations by spending a spell point per Magnitude of the School and then succeeding on an Intelligence (Arcana) ability check against the spellcasting DC.
1303 Most Incantations take an action to cast, but if you roll lower than the Casting DC you don’t automatically fail. Instead, you can choose to keep the value from that roll and add to it by spending an Action the following round and rolling another Spellcrafting check. Even if you have to spend more than one Action to cast an Incantation you only spend one spell point per magnitude of the spell. In addition, you only add any bonus to the check once and you can never spend more rounds rolling than you have proficiency bonus.
1304 If you ever roll a natural 1 on your Arcana skill check you gain 1 level of Exhaustion and automatically fail casting the Incantation.
1305 Example: Thorak has a Proficiency Bonus of +2, and he is attempting to cast the Cloud of Pestilence Incantation, from the Frist Magnitude Afflict Spell School. During the first round he rolls 15 on his Intelligence (Arcana) Skill check, adding +3 for his intelligence, and +2 from his Proficiency Bonus, for a total of 20. Not enough but close, so Thorak decides to keep casting during the next round, this time rolling 8, adding it to the 20 from the previous round, for a total of 28. Success! Had he not succeeded, he would not be able to continue for a Third round until his Proficiency Bonus increases by +1.Example: Thorak has a Proficiency Bonus of +2, and he is attempting to cast the Cloud of Pestilence Incantation, from the Frist Magnitude Afflict Spell School. During the first round he rolls 15 on his Intelligence (Arcana) Skill check, adding +3 for his intelligence, and +2 from his Proficiency Bonus, for a total of 20. Not enough but close, so Thorak decides to keep casting during the next round, this time rolling 8, adding it to the 20 from the previous round, for a total of 28. Success! Had he not succeeded, he would not be able to continue for a Third round until his Proficiency Bonus increases by +1.Used Spell Points are regained when taking a Long Rest
1306 Example: Thorak has a Proficiency Bonus of +2, and he is attempting to cast the Cloud of Pestilence Incantation, from the Frist Magnitude Afflict Spell School. During the first round he rolls 15 on his Intelligence (Arcana) Skill check, adding +3 for his intelligence, and +2 from his Proficiency Bonus, for a total of 20. Not enough but close, so Thorak decides to keep casting during the next round, this time rolling 8, adding it to the 20 from the previous round, for a total of 28. Success! Had he not succeeded, he would not be able to continue for a Third round until his Proficiency Bonus increases by +1.
1308 Example: Thorak has a Proficiency Bonus of +2, and he is attempting to cast the Cloud of Pestilence Incantation, from the Frist Magnitude Afflict Spell School. During the first round he rolls 15 on his Intelligence (Arcana) Skill check, adding +3 for his intelligence, and +2 from his Proficiency Bonus, for a total of 20. Not enough but close, so Thorak decides to keep casting during the next round, this time rolling 8, adding it to the 20 from the previous round, for a total of 28. Success! Had he not succeeded, he would not be able to continue for a Third round until his Proficiency Bonus increases by +1.
1310 The Word of Magic
1311 The Magecraft feat allows you to recognize writing and speech as “magical”. The Spellcraft feat allows you to speak and read magic. Knowing this language allows you to decipher magical inscriptions on books, scrolls, weapons, and the like written in magical script. This deciphering doesn’t normally invoke any magic contained in the writing. While reading archaic runes or symbols on magic items, you can attempt an Arcana check with a DC set by the GM, to gain a rudimentary understanding of how the item works.
1312 Defiling
1313 You can elect to defile an area, destroying any non-sentient life within a radius 10 feet per spell point used for casting the Incantation. Doing so grants you a +10 bonus to the Arcana Spellcrafting check, but you also gain 1 level of exhaustion. The effect of your defiling can be increased through feats. Though the immediate effect of defiling is to turn life into dead ash, in time it also defiles the magic user. All defiling feats therefore comes with a cost. The lure of defiling is so potent that such feats can be taken both as a cultural and a general feat, allowing you to select them more often.
1314 Creating new Incantations
1315 By combining the Base and Enhance effects for a spell school, casters can create new Incantations. All Incantations have a base DC of 4, to which the cost of each augmentation must be added. In theory an Incantation could be combined with another Incantation, even from a different school. In such cases both Incantations must have the same general Augmentation effects added and the same targets or area of effect.
1316 Creating such Incantation takes time at the table and should preferably be done by players familiar with the spellcasting rules. When learning the system it is advised you follow the progression in the examples on the next page, and only progress to the next step after mastering the previous.
