3080 By 17th level, you have become one with the night you serve. While in dim light or darkness, you gain the following benefits:
3081 When you use your Night Strike feature to grant yourself advantage on an attack roll, you deal additional damage equal to half your Priest level if the attack hits.
3082 Creatures that do not have blindsight, true sight, or Tremorsense have disadvantage on attack rolls against you if you are not incapacitated, grappled, or restrained. If a creature hits you with an attack, it is immune to this effect until the end of its turn. If you roll initiative and have no uses of your Night Strike ability remaining, you regain 1d4 uses. If you roll a number that is higher than your Wisdom modifier, you regain a number of uses equal to your Wisdom modifier instead (minimum of 1).
3084 Tempest Domain
3085 Sky Speaker
3086 At 1st level, you know when storms are brewing; you have a general idea of what the weather in your area will be for the next 24 hours as long as you can see the sky. This includes whether it will rain or snow and how severely.
3087 Storm's Wrath
3088 Also at 1st level, you learn to punish enemies with the fury of your deity. When a creature within 30 feet of you deals damage to you, you can use your reaction to make a weapon attack against them. You can use this ability a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (a minimum of once)and regain all uses when you finish a long rest.
3089 Thunderous Strikes
3090 By 6th level, your strikes are filled with the ardor of your deity. Once per turn when you hit a Large or smaller creature with a weapon attack, you can push it up to 10 feet away from you if it was a melee attack or 5 feet away from you if it was a ranged attack. If a creature pushed by this feature strikes a solid surface, the target takes 1d6 bludgeoning damage. If the target is pushed into another creature, that creature must succeed on a Dexterity saving throw (DC = 8 + your Wisdom modifier +your proficiency bonus) or both creatures take 1d6bludgeoning damage.
3091 In addition, if you have expended any uses of your Storm's Wrath feature, you regain one use when you score a critical hit with your symbolic weapon.
3092 Channel the Storm
3093 By 10th level, you have endured storms and learned to resist and redirect their power. You gain resistance to lightning and thunder damage. When an ally within 30 feet of you takes lightning or thunder damage, you can use your reaction to take that damage in their place if you are wearing metal armour, wielding a metal weapon, or wielding your symbolic weapon.
3094 When you take lightning or thunder damage, the first weapon attack you hit with before the end of your next turn deals an additional 2d6 damage of the type you took.
3095 Tempest Blade
3096 At 14th level, you master techniques inspired by the storm and empowered by your faith. If an ability forces a creature to make a saving throw, the DC is 8 + your Wisdom modifier +your proficiency bonus. You gain the following abilities:
3097 Deluge: As an action, you batter a single creature with a barrage of inescapable blows from your symbolic weapon. You must be wielding your symbolic weapon, and the creature must be within its range. If it is a ranged weapon, you must expend 4 pieces of ammunition. The creature must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take 10d6 damage of your symbolic weapon's type and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and aren't knocked prone.
3098 Hurricane Strike: As an action, you sweep your weapon in a wide arc. Each creature within range of a melee weapon you are wielding must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, a creature takes 6d6 damage of your weapon's type and is pushed as if affected by your Thunderous Strikes ability. On a success, they take half as much damage and aren't pushed.
3099 You can perform these techniques a total of 3 times in any combination and regain all uses when you finish a short or long rest.
3100 Rising Storm
3101 By 17th level, your attacks grow more powerful as your temper rises. When you are hit by an attack or fail a saving throw, the first weapon attack you hit with or Tempest Blade ability you use before the end of your next turn deals an additional 1d6 damage of the weapon's type. The additional damage increases by 1d6 with each consecutive hit or failed saving throw, to a maximum number of d6s equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1d6).
3103 Trickery Domain
3104 Divine Trickery
3105 At 1st level, you have proven your devotion to your deity by learning a few choice skills. You gain proficiency in one of the following skills of your choice: Deception, Sleight of Hand, or Stealth.
3106 Sacred Poison
3107 Also at 1st level, you gain proficiency in Craft (Poison). Over the course of a long rest, you can prepare a number of vials of poison equal to your proficiency bonus. A creature can use the poison in each vial to coat one slashing or piercing weapon or up to five pieces of ammunition. Applying the poison takes a bonus action. Once applied, the poison retains potency for 1 minute before drying.
3108 The first time on a turn that a creature is hit by the poisoned weapon or ammunition, it takes poison damage equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1 damage).
3109 Poisons created with this feature are unstable. They can’t be sold and lose potency at the end of your next long rest.
3110 Trickster's Reward
3111 At 6th level, your faith and experience have improved your skills. You can add double your proficiency bonus to checks made  with one skill of your choice that is listed in the Divine Trickery feature and you are proficient in, or Craft (Poison).
3112 In addition, poison made through your Sacred Poison feature now ignores resistance to poison damage and deals poison damage equal to 1d6 plus your Wisdom modifier. If itis applied to your symbolic weapon or ammunition for that weapon, it deals poison damage equal to 1d8 plus your Wisdom modifier instead.
3113 Subtle Strike
3114 By 10th level, you have learned that taking your enemies by surprise is the favoured strategy of the trickster. You gain proficiency in Stealth, and any hit you score against a creature that is surprised is a critical hit.
3115 In addition, poison made through your Sacred Poison feature now deals poison damage equal to 1d8 plus your Wisdom modifier. If it is applied to your symbolic weapon or ammunition for that weapon, it deals poison damage equal to1d10 plus your Wisdom modifier instead.
3116 Trickster's Takedown
3117 At 14th level, you learn to add debilitating ingredients to your poison. When a creature takes poison damage from your Sacred Poison feature, it must make a Constitution saving throw (DC = 8 + your Wisdom modifier + your proficiency bonus). A surprised creature makes this saving throw with disadvantage. On a failure, the creature is poisoned until the end of its next turn. Creatures that fail their saving throw by 5 or more fall prone unless they are immune to the poisoned condition. A creature that succeeds on its saving throw is immune to this effect for 1 hour.
3118 Miasma
3119 By 17th level, you have learned to refine your poisons through ritual, experimentation, and luck. The poison created through your Sacred Poison feature now deals half damage to creatures that are normally immune to poison damage and deals poison damage equal to 2d6 plus your Wisdom modifier. If it is applied to your symbolic weapon or ammunition for that weapon, it deals poison damage equal to 2d8 plus your Wisdom modifier.
3120 When you hit a creature with a weapon coated with your Sacred Poison and deal poison damage to it, you can force each creature within 10 feet of the target, other than yourself and the original target, to make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, a creature takes the same poison damage as the original target and is poisoned until the end of your next turn. If it fails by 5 or more, it falls prone unless it is immune to the poisoned condition. Once you have used this ability, you must finish a short or long rest before you can use it again.
3122 War Domain
3123 Acolyte of War
3124 At 1st level, your devotion to your deity has given you a greater propensity for battle. When you take the Attack action or use your action to perform an Act of Faith, you can make one weapon attack as a bonus action.
3125 You can do this a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (a minimum of once) and regain all uses when you finish a long rest.
3126 Battle Tactics
3127 Also at 1st level, you and your allies are more prepared to act in the face of danger. When you roll initiative, you or a creature of your choice within 30 feet of you that can see or hear gain a bonus to their initiative equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of +1).
3128 Divine Warrior
3129 At 6th level, your faith allows you moments of perfect clarity and unyielding strength on and off the battlefield. When you make an attack roll or Strength check, you can choose to gain a +10 bonus to the roll. You make the choice after seeing the roll, but before knowing whether it is a hit or miss for an attack or success or failure for an ability check.
3130 You can use this ability twice and regain all uses when you finish a short or long rest.
3131 In addition, if you have expended any uses of your Acolyte of War feature, you regain one use when you score a critical hit with your symbolic weapon.
3132 War Leader
3133 By 10th level, you have learned to share your conviction with your allies. When an ally within 30 feet of you makes an attack roll or Strength check, you can use your reaction and expend a use of your Divine Warrior ability to focus them with a gesture, prayer, or war cry. They gain a +10 bonus to the roll if they can see or hear you. You make the choice after seeing the roll, but before knowing whether it is a hit or miss for an attack or success or failure for an ability check. In addition, you can now use your Divine Warrior ability three times between rests, instead of two.
3134 Divine Roar
3135 At 14th level, you learn to rally allies and suppress enemies with a mighty roar. You can use your Castigate Act of Faith as a bonus action. When you do, each creature of your choice within 30 feet that can see or hear you has advantage on the first attack roll they make before the start of your next turn. When you use Castigate through this feature, a creature that fails it saving throw has its movement speed reduced to0 for the duration. If a creature fails its saving throw by 5 or more, it is stunned for as long as it is frightened. Once you have used Castigate as a bonus action, you cannot do so again until you finish a short or long rest.
3136 Avatar of Battle
3137 At 17th level, your unbreakable faith allows you to fight through otherwise crushing blows. You gain resistance to bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing damage from nonmagical weapons.
3139 Divine Revelations
3140 Apostate's Bane
3141 Prerequisite: 5th level, Faith's Foe Revelation
3142 When a creature of the type you chose as your Faith's Foe fails its saving throw against your Castigate Act of Faith, it must take the Dash action and move away from you by the safest available route on each of its turns, unless there is nowhere to move, for as long as it is frightened of you. It has disadvantage on its saving throws to end Castigates fear effect if you are in its line of sight.
3143 Blessed Water
3144 Over the course of a long rest, you can create a number of vials of holy water equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1) without expending silver or spell slots. This holy water cannot be sold and loses potency the next time you finish along rest.
3145 Divine Tongues
3146 You learn to read, write, and speak Celestial, Infernal, and Abyssal.
3147 Divine Water
3148 Prerequisite: 5th level, Blessed Water Revelation
3149 You are proficient with attacks made with holy water and can add your Wisdom modifier to the damage rolls of such attacks. Holy water made with your Blessed Water feature deals a number of d6s of damage equal to your proficiency bonus, instead of the usual 2d6.
3150 Divine Wisdom
3151 Prerequisite: 12th level
3152 You recognize any aberration, celestial, elemental, fey, fiend, or undead for what it is as long as you can see it and it is within 30 feet of you, even if its form is concealed by disguise or magic. If its true form is concealed, you recognize its true creature type but do not know its original form.
3153 Faith's Foe
3154 Choose one of the following creature types: aberrations, celestials, elementals, fey, fiends, or undead. This is usually a type of creature that serves as an enemy to your deity or religious order. You learn a language associated with such creatures and have advantage on ability checks made to recall information about them.
3155 Mentor
3156 When you use the Help action to grant an ally advantage on an ability check, they can roll 1d4 and add it to the check if you are proficient in the skill you are helping them with.
3157 Missionary
3158 You have advantage on Charisma checks involving explaining aspects of your faith and converting others to join it.
3159 Pilgrim
3160 Prerequisite: 5th level
3161 Your walking speed increases by 10 feet if you are wearing light or no armour, or 5 feet if you are wearing medium or heavy armour.
3162 Prayer of Endurance
3163 By offering a prayer to your deity, you can grant yourself temporary hit points equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1) as an action at will.
3164 Religious Scholar
3165 You gain proficiency in Religion. If you are already proficient, you instead add double your proficiency bonus whenever you make an Intelligence (Religion) check.
3166 Symbol of Protection
3167 Prerequisite: 5th level
3168 When you are hit with a melee attack while you are wielding your symbolic weapon, you can use your reaction to add your Wisdom modifier to your armour class against that attack, possibly causing it to miss.
3169 Ascetic Revelations
3170 The Divine Revelations listed below can only be taken by a Priest with the Ascetic Divine Calling.
3171 Cloud Step
3172 Prerequisite: 9th level, Ascetic Calling
3173 As an action, you can focus on your physical training. For the next minute, your jump distance is tripled as if you were under the jump spell. Because this ability does not rely on magic, it does not require concentration and is not affected by dispel magic or an antimagic field
3174 Disrupt Spirit
3175 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3176 Once on each of your turns when you hit a creature with your symbolic weapon, you can reduce the target's movement speed by 10 feet until the end of your next turn.
3177 Light Step
3178 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3179 When you take the Dash, Disengage, or Dodge action, your movement speed is increased by 10 feet until the end of your turn.
3180 Living Symbol
3181 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3182 Your damage with unarmed strikes increases to 1d4 and you can use your Wisdom modifier for attack and damage rolls with unarmed strikes. You can use any Priest, Divine Domain, or Divine Revelation feature that requires your symbolic weapon with your unarmed strikes. Your unarmed strike damage increases to 1d6 when you reach level 5 in this class and 1d8 when you reach level 11.
3183 Mind Over Muscle
3184 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3185 You can use your Wisdom modifier in place of your Strength modifier for Strength (Athletics) checks made to grapple a creature, shove a creature, or escape a grapple.
3187 One with the Weapon
3188 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3189 You focus on your training with your symbolic weapon as a bonus action. Until the end of your turn, your attacks with it deal an additional 1d4 damage.
3190 Perfect Stance
3191 Prerequisite: 12th level, Ascetic Calling
3192 When you suffer a critical hit, you can use your reaction and expend a use of your Acts of Faith to turn it into a regular hit.
3193 Physical Training
3194 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3195 You gain proficiency in Athletics and Acrobatics.
3196 Precision
3197 Prerequisite: 12th level, Ascetic Calling
3198 Once on each of your turns when you miss with an attack with your symbolic weapon, you can reroll the attack roll. If you had advantage or disadvantage on the initial roll, it does not apply when you reroll.
3199 Sound Body
3200 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3201 When you fail a saving throw against poison, you can expend a use of your Acts of Faith ability to reroll the saving throw. You must use the new result.
3202 Sound Mind
3203 Prerequisite: 7th level, Ascetic Calling
3204 When you fail a saving throw that causes you to become charmed or frightened, you can expend a use of your Acts of Faith ability to reroll the saving throw. You must use the new result.
3205 Sound Soul
3206 Prerequisite: 15th level, Sound Body and Sound Mind Revelations
3207 When you fail any saving throw, you can expend a use of your Acts of Faith ability to reroll the saving throw. You must use the new result.
3208 Stoicism
3209 Prerequisite: 9th level, Temperance Revelation
3210 You require food and water only once every 10 days and can hold your breath for up to one hour. You can gain the benefits of a long rest even if you spend seven of the eight hours awake and aware in quiet meditation or performing light activities.
3211 Symbol of Enlightenment
3212 Prerequisite: 15th level, Ascetic Calling
3213 Your damage dice with your symbolic weapon increase by one step. From 1d8 to 1d10, 1d10 to 1d12, or from 1d12 or2d6 to 2d8.
3214 Temperance
3215 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3216 You require only half as much food and water as an ordinary person, and can gain the benefits of a long rest even if you spend four of the eight hours awake and aware in quiet meditation or performing light activities.
3217 Weighted Weapon Training
3218 Prerequisite: Ascetic Calling
3219 You can use your Wisdom modifier for attack and damage rolls with your symbolic weapon even if it has the heavy property.
3220 Crusader Revelations
3221 The Divine Revelations listed below can only be taken by a Priest with the Crusader Divine Calling.
3222 Ardent Strikes
3223 Prerequisite: 7th level, Crusader Calling
3224 When you score a critical hit with your symbolic weapon, you can roll one of the weapon's damage dice one additional time and add it to the extra damage of the critical hit.
3225 Bastion of Faith
3226 Prerequisite: 15th level, Protector Revelation
3227 When you use your reaction to guard another creature with your Protector Revelation, you have resistance to the damage of the attack you take in their place.
3228 Campaigner
3229 Prerequisite: 5th level, Crusader Calling
3230 Your carrying capacity (including maximum load and maximum lift) is doubled, and you have advantage on Strength checks made to push, pull, lift, or break objects.
3231 Crusader's Charge
3232 Prerequisite: 7th level, Crusader Calling
3233 When you take the Dash action, you can make one attack with your symbolic weapon as part of the same action if you end the movement within 5 feet of a hostile creature.
3234 Crusader's Mettle
3235 Prerequisite: Crusader Calling
3236 Your hit point maximum increases by an amount equal to your Priest level when you gain this revelation. Whenever you gain a Priest level thereafter, your hit point maximum increases by an additional hit point.
3237 Fervent Strike
3238 Prerequisite: 5th level, Crusader Calling
3239 Before you make an attack with your symbolic weapon, you can choose to take a -5 penalty to the attack roll. If the attack hits, you add +10 to the attack's damage. This cannot be used in conjunction with the Sharpshooter or Great Weapon Master feats.
3240 Guardian
3241 Prerequisite: 9th level, Protector Revelation
3242 When you use your reaction to guard another creature with your Protector Revelation, you can force any attack that targets that creature to target you instead until the creature is more than 5 feet away from you or your next turn begins.
3243 Holy Soldier
3244 Prerequisite: Crusader Calling
3245 You gain proficiency in Athletics and Intimidation.
3248 Protector
3249 Prerequisite: Crusader Calling
3250 When a creature within 5 feet of you is hit by an attack, you can use your reaction to intervene. The attack hits you instead of the creature you protected.
3251 Raise Ardor
3252 Prerequisite: 5th level, Crusader Calling
3253 You gain the following Act of Faith option:
3254 Raise Ardor: As an action, you raise your symbolic weapon or let out a war cry to invigorate your allies. A number of creatures of your choice up to your Wisdom modifier(minimum of 1) within 30 feet of you that can see or hear you deal an additional 1d4 damage with their weapon attacks for the next minute.
3255 Symbol of Battle
3256 Prerequisite: Crusader Calling
3257 You can gain the benefits of any Priest, Divine Domain, or Divine Revelation feature that normally requires the use of your symbolic weapon with any weapon with which you are proficient.
3258 Veteran Crusader
3259 Prerequisite: 15th level, Crusader Calling
3260 You learn a second fighting style from those listed under the Crusader Divine Calling.
3261 Zealot's Endurance
3262 Prerequisite: Crusader Calling
3263 When you are reduced to 0 hit points but not killed outright, you can drop to 1 hit point instead. You can’t use this feature again until you finish a long rest.
3264 Zealous Strikes
3265 Prerequisite: 12th level, Crusader Calling
3266 Your attacks with your symbolic weapon deal additional damage equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1).
3267 Inquisitor Revelations
3268 The Divine Revelations listed below can only be taken by a Priest with the Inquisitor Divine Calling.
3269 Armoured Hunter
3270 Prerequisite: Inquisitor Calling
3271 Wearing medium armour doesn't impose disadvantage on your Dexterity (Stealth) checks.
3272 Heretic Hunter
3273 Prerequisite: Inquisitor Calling
3274 You gain proficiency in Survival and have advantage on ability checks to track one creature type, such as aberrations or fey, of your choice.
3275 Interrogator
3276 Prerequisite: Stern Gaze Revelation
3277 You can add double your proficiency bonus to Charisma(Intimidation) or Wisdom (Insight) checks (your choice).
3278 Judgment Everlasting
3279 Prerequisite: 15th level, Inquisitor Calling
3280 When the target of your judgment dies, you immediately regain the use of your judgment ability. If you have the Second or Third Judgment revelations, you lose their benefits until you finish a short or long rest.
3281 Judgment of Challenge
3282 Prerequisite: 5th level, Inquisitor Calling
3283 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe. Instead, the target of your judgment has disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than you while it is within 5 feet of you.
3284 Judgment of Piercing
3285 Prerequisite: Inquisitor Calling
3286 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe. Instead, your weapon attacks against them score a critical hit on a roll of 19 or 20.
3287 Judgment of Protection
3288 Prerequisite: Inquisitor Calling
3289 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe to gain a bonus to your armour class against their attacks. This bonus is equal to half your proficiency bonus (round up).
3290 Judgment of Purity
3291 Prerequisite: 7th level, Inquisitor Calling
3292 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe to gain a bonus to your saving throws against their spells and abilities. This bonus is equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of +1).
3293 Judgment of Resistance
3294 Prerequisite: 5th level, Inquisitor Calling
3295 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe. You instead resist one damage type dealt by their attacks, spells, and abilities. You choose the damage type when you render judgment, but cannot choose bludgeoning, piercing, or slashing.
3296 Judgment of Slaying
3297 Prerequisite: 12th level, Judgment of Piercing Revelation
3298 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe. Instead, your weapon attacks against them score a critical hit on a roll of 18, 19, or 20.
3299 Judgment of Terror
3300 Prerequisite: 12th level, Inquisitor Calling
3301 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe. Instead, the first time on each of your turns that you hit the target of your judgment with a weapon attack, it must make a Wisdom saving throw (DC = 8 + your Wisdom modifier + your proficiency bonus). On a failure, it is frightened of you until the start of your next turn.
3302 Judgment of Vengeance
3303 Prerequisite: 9th level, Inquisitor Calling
3304 When you render judgment, you can forgo the damage bonus to your judged foe. Instead, when the target of your judgment deals damage to you while within 5 feet of you, you can use your reaction to make a weapon attack against them.
3305 Second Judgment
3306 Prerequisite: 7th level, any Judgment Revelation
3307 When you render judgment, you can gain the benefits of one Judgment Revelation you know, such as the Judgment of Protection or Judgment of Purity, without forgoing the damage bonus of your judgment.
3308 Stern Gaze
3309 Prerequisite: Inquisitor Calling
3310 You gain proficiency in Intimidation and Insight.
3311 Swift Judgment
3312 Prerequisite: 9th level, Inquisitor Calling
3313 If you are surprised at the beginning of combat and aren’t incapacitated, you can act normally on your first turn, but only if you use your judgment ability before doing anything else on that turn.
3314 Third Judgment
3315 Prerequisite: 15th level, Second Judgment Revelation
3316 When you render judgment, you can gain the benefits of two Judgment Revelations you know, such as the Judgment of Protection or Judgment of Purity, without forgoing the damage bonus of your judgment.
3317 Preacher Revelations
3318 The Divine Revelations listed below can only be taken by a Priest with the Preacher Divine Calling.
3319 Benediction
3320 Prerequisite: 15th level, Preacher Calling
3321 When you use your Preacher ability to improve an attack roll, ability check, or saving throw and roll a 1 or 2 on the die, you can reroll the die and must use the new roll.
3322 Compelling Sermon
3323 Prerequisite: Preacher Calling
3324 You gain the following Act of Faith option:
3325 Sermon: If you spend at least 1 minute giving a sermon, lecture, or lesson, a number of humanoids of your choice up to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1) who are listening to you have disadvantage on Wisdom (Perception)checks to perceive creatures other than you for as long as they continue to listen to your sermon.
3326 Curse
3327 Prerequisite: Preacher Calling
3328 When a creature within 60 feet of you makes an attack roll or ability check, you can present your symbolic weapon or a holy symbol of your deity and expend a use of your Preacher ability as a reaction. If the creature can see or hear you and speaks at least one language, you can roll 1d6 and subtract it from their roll. You can use this ability after the creature makes its roll, but before knowing whether the attack roll or ability check succeeds or fails. When you reach levels 5, 10, and 15 in this class, you can subtract 1d8, 1d10, or 1d12 from the roll instead.
3329 Demagogue
3330 Prerequisite: Preacher Calling
3331 You gain proficiency in Persuasion and Deception.
3332 Enduring Blessing
3333 Prerequisite: 12th level, Preacher Calling
3334 When you use your Preacher ability to improve an ally’s ability check, attack roll, or saving throw, that ally can add1d6 to the next attack roll, ability check, or saving throw that they make in the next minute.
3335 Evangelist
3336 Prerequisite: Preacher Calling
3337 You gain the following Act of Faith option:
3338 Evangelize: If you spend at least 1 minute speaking of your religion, you can force a number of humanoids up to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1) who listened to you to make Wisdom saving throws. On a failure, a target is charmed by you for 1 hour. It idolizes your religion and hinder anyone who opposes your faith while charmed. It avoids violence unless it was already inclined to fight for you. The charm ends if it takes any damage, you attack it, or it witnesses you attacking or damaging its allies. A target that succeeds on its saving throw has no hint you tried to charm it and is immune to this ability for 1 day.
3339 Fire and Brimstone
3340 Prerequisite: Preacher Calling
3341 You gain the following Act of Faith option:
3342 Condemnation: If you spend at least 1 minute speaking of the consequences of defying your religion, you can force a number of humanoids up to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of 1) who listened to you to make Wisdom saving throws. On a failure, a target is frightened of you for1 hour. The effect ends if it takes any damage, you attack it, or it witnesses you attacking or damaging its allies. A target that succeeds on its saving throw has no hint you tried to frighten it and is immune to this ability for 1 day.
3343 Guide
3344 Prerequisite: 7th level, Preacher Calling
3345 When you roll initiative, allies within 30 feet of you can add+1 to their roll if they can see or hear you and you aren’t incapacitated.
3346 Lingering Curse
3347 Prerequisite: 12th level, Curse Revelation
3348 When you use your Curse ability to hinder a creature's ability check or attack roll, that creature must subtract 1d6 from the next attack roll or ability check that it makes in the next minute.
3349 Malediction
3350 Prerequisite: 15th level, Curse Revelation
3351 When you use your Curse ability to hinder a creature's attack roll or ability check and roll a 1 or 2 on the die, you can reroll the die and must use the new roll.
3352 Mouth of God
3353 Prerequisite: 7th level, Preacher Calling
3354 You can add your Wisdom modifier to Charisma checks.
3357 Pastor
3358 Prerequisite: 5th level, Preacher Calling
3359 You regain all uses of your Preacher ability when you finish a short or long rest.
3361 Elementalist (Air) Divine revelations:
3362 Conjure servant (Air)
3363 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Air domain)
3364 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3365 Conjure servant (small): As an action you can summon a servant made of nothing but the air surrounding you. The creature has the following stats: STR +2, DEX +2, CON +2, INT -4, WIS +1, CHA -2, AC 12, HP 5 (+ 5 times your level), Speed 60' (fly). Weapon attack: +4 to hit, reach 5ft, Hit: 7 (1d6 + 2) bludgeoning damage. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the servant. You need to spend a Bonus Action each round to direct your servant. The servant remains at your side for a number of rounds equal to your proficiency bonus.
3366 Conjure minor element (Air)
3367 Prerequisite: 5th level, conjure servant (Air) revelation.
3368 Your servant increases to size Medium. Its strength and dexterity modifier increases by +1. Its AC, attack and damage bonus increases by +1. It gains the following ability (Action):
3369 Windstorm: As an action the elemental creates a windstorm with a 30-foot radius up to 60 feet away. The storm lasts as long as long as the elemental spends and Action each round to maintain the Windstorm. Ranged attacks within the area are made with Disadvantage and anything within the area, or seen from within the area, are lightly obscured.
3370 Conjure element (Air)
3371 Prerequisite: 7th level, conjure minor element (Air) revelation.
3372 Your servant increases to size Large. Its strength and dexterity modifier increases by +1. Its AC, attack and damage bonus increases by +1. It gains the following ability (Action):
3373 Whirlwind (Recharge 4–6). Each creature in the elemental's space must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw. On a failure, a target takes 15 (3d8 + 2) bludgeoning damage and is flung up 20 feet away from the elemental in a random direction and knocked prone. If a thrown target strikes an object, such as a wall or floor, the target takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage for every 10 feet it was thrown. If the target is thrown at another creature, that creature must succeed on a DC 13 Dexterity saving throw or take the same damage and be knocked prone.
3374 Conjure elder elemental (Air)
3375 Prerequisite: 9th level, conjure element (Air) revelation.
3376 Your servant’s strength and dexterity modifier increases by +1. Its AC, attack and damage bonus increases by +1. It gains the following ability (Action):
3377 Lightning Strike (Recharge 6). The elemental makes one attack. On a hit, the target takes an extra 18 (4d8) lightning damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 13 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the elemental’s next turn.
3378 Ignore element (Air)
3379 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Air domain)
3380 You become one with the air around you, as your skin start to become transparent, and your clothes and hair constantly seems to be moved by slight breeze. You can ignore the strongest winds and no longer suffer any damage from falling.
3381 Become one with the elements (Air)
3382 Prerequisite: 5th level, ignore element (Air) revelation.
3383 Your skin become even more transparent as you slowly turn into your chosen element. You can effectively levitate, changing your altitude by up to 20 feet in either direction on your turn.
3384 Gate (Air)
3385 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Air domain)
3386 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3387 Gate: As an action you can create blowing winds from nothing. You are able to gate air in a 10 foot radius centred upon you. Targets within range must roll Dexterity or fall prone. Targets with size greater than large are unaffected.
3388 Call the storm (Air)
3389 Prerequisite: 7th level, gate (Air)
3390 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3391 Call the storm: A line of strong wind 60 feet long and 10 feet wide blasts from you in a direction you choose. Each creature that starts its turn in the line must succeed on a Strength saving throw or be pushed 15 feet away from you in a direction following the line. Any creature in the line must spend 2 feet of movement for every 1 foot it moves when moving closer to you. The gust disperses gas or vapor, and it extinguishes candles, torches, and similar unprotected flames in the area. It causes protected flames, such as those of lanterns, to dance wildly and has a 50 percent chance to extinguish them. As a bonus action on each of your turns before the spell ends, you can change the direction in which the line blasts from you. The storm remains for as long as your concentration is not broken by taking damage, up to one minute.
3392 Elementalist (Earth) Divine revelations:
3393 Conjure servant (Earth)
3394 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Earth domain)
3395 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3396 Conjure servant (small): As an action you can summon a servant made of nothing but the rock beneath your feet. The creature has the following stats: STR +2, DEX +2, CON +2, INT -4, WIS +1, CHA -2, AC 12, HP 5 (+ 5 times your level), Speed 20', 30’ (burrow). Weapon attack: +4 to hit, reach 5ft, Hit: 7 (1d6 + 2) bludgeoning damage. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the servant. You need to spend a Bonus Action each round to direct your servant. The servant remains at your side for a number of rounds equal to your proficiency bonus.
3397 Conjure minor element (Earth)
3398 Prerequisite: 5th level, conjure servant (Earth) revelation.
3399 Your servant increases to size Medium. Its strength and constitution modifier increases by +1. Its attack and damage bonus increases by +1, and its HP increase by 1 + your level. It gains the following ability (Action):
3400 Mud Breath (Recharge 6). The servant belches viscid mud onto one creature within 5 feet of it. If the target is Medium or smaller, it must succeed on a DC 11 Dexterity saving throw or be restrained for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
3401 Conjure element (Earth)
3402 Prerequisite: 7th level, conjure minor element (Earth) revelation.
3403 Your servant increases to size Large. Its strength and constitution modifier increases by +1. Its attack and damage bonus increases by +1, and its HP increase by 1 + your level. It gains the following abilities:
3404 The servant can burrow through nonmagical, unworked earth and stone. While doing so, it doesn't disturb the material it moves through.  The servant also deals double damage to objects and structures.
3405 Conjure elder elemental (Earth)
3406 Prerequisite: 9th level, conjure element (Earth) revelation.
3407 Your servant’s strength and constitution modifier increase by +1. Its attack and damage bonus increases by +1, and its HP increase by 1 + your level. It gains the following ability (Action):
3408 Thunderous Strike (Recharge 6). The servant makes one attack. If the attack hits, it deals an extra 16 (3d10) thunder damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 14 Strength saving throw or be knocked prone.
3409 Ignore element (Earth)
3410 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Earth domain)
3411 You become one with the dust and silt around you, as your skin start to become harder, and always seems to be shedding sand. As long as you are not wearing any armour, your armour class is 13. You can also sense the properties of any rock or metal you touch.
3412 Become one with the elements (Earth)
3413 Prerequisite: 5th level, ignore element (Earth) revelation.
3414 Part of your skin begins to crackle, as though it was dried dirt. You gain the ability to burrow in silt, sand and lose earth. Your movement rate when using this ability is half your ordinary move.
3415 Gate (Earth)
3416 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Earth domain)
3417 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3418 Gate: As an action you can gate earth somewhere within 15 feet of you. Each time you use this ability you gate enough earth to cover a 5´cube. If you gate this upon a target it causes 1d6 per proficiency bonus. The target can roll a reflex save (DC 8 + your proficiency bonus + your wisdom modifier) for half.
3419 Erupting earth
3420 Prerequisite: 7th level, gate Earth)
3421 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3422 Erupting earth: Choose a point you can see on the ground within range. A fountain of churned earth and stone erupts in a 20-foot cube centered on that point. Each creature in that area must make a Dexterity saving throw. A creature takes 3d6 bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Additionally, the ground in that area becomes difficult terrain until cleared. Each 5-foot-square portion of the area requires at least 1 minute to clear by hand.
3424 Elementalist (Fire) Divine revelations:
3425 Conjure servant (Fire)
3426 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Fire domain)
3427 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3428 Conjure servant (small): As an action you can summon a servant made of crackling fire. The creature has the following stats: STR +2, DEX +2, CON +2, INT -4, WIS +1, CHA -2, AC 12, HP 5 (+ 5 times your level), Speed 30' (fly). Weapon attack: +4 to hit, reach 5ft, Hit: 7 (1d6 + 2) fire damage. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the servant. You need to spend a Bonus Action each round to direct your servant. The servant remains at your side for a number of rounds equal to your proficiency bonus.
3429 Conjure minor element (Fire):
3430 Prerequisite: 5th level, conjure servant (Fire) revelation.
3431 Your servant increases to size Medium. Its strength and dexterity modifier increases by +1. Its AC, attack and damage bonus increases by +1. It gains the following ability (Action):
3432 Fire Breath (Recharge 6). The servant exhales a 15-foot cone of fire. Each creature in that area must make a DC 11 Dexterity saving throw, taking 7 (2d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
3433 Conjure element (Fire)
3434 Prerequisite: 7th level, conjure minor element (Earth) revelation.
3435 Your servant increases to size Large. Its strength and dexterity modifier increases by +1. Its AC, attack and damage bonus increases by +1. It gains the following abilities:
3436 Fire Form. The servant can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing. A creature that touches the servant or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 4 (1d8) fire damage. In addition, the servant can enter a hostile creature's space and stop there. The first time it enters a creature's space on a turn, that creature takes 4 (1d8) fire damage and catches fire; until someone takes an action to douse the fire, the creature takes 4 (1d8) fire damage at the start of each of its turns.
3437 Conjure elder elemental (Fire)
3438 Prerequisite: 9th level, conjure element (Fire) revelation.
3439 Your servants strength and dexterity modifier increases by +1. Its AC, attack and damage bonus increases by +1. It gains the following ability (Action):
3440 Fiery Strikes (Recharge 6). The servant blazes up with even more intensity, like a storm of fire. Each attack that hits deals an extra 5 (1d10) fire damage. The effect last until it misses on an attack roll.
3441 Ignore element (Fire)
3442 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Fire domain)
3443 You become one with the heat and fire, as your skin starts to darken, and ash always seems to shed from your body and hair. You gain Resistance to Fire damage.
3444 Become one with the elements (Fire)
3445 Prerequisite: 5th level, ignore element (fire) revelation.
3446 Part of your skin begins to char and parts of it becomes black. You are immune to Fire damage.
3447 Gate (Fire)
3448 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Fire domain)
3449 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3450 Gate: As an action you can gate fire anywhere within 30 feet. You can direct this as an attack causing 1d6 points of damage per proficiency bonus in a 15´ line.
3451 Wall of fire
3452 Prerequisite: 7th level, gate Fire
3453 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3454 Wall of fire. You create a wall of fire on a solid surface within range. You can make the wall up to 30 feet long, 10 feet high, and 1 foot thick, or a ringed wall up to 10 feet in diameter, 10 feet high, and 1 foot thick. When the wall appears, each creature within its area must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failed save, a creature takes 2d6 fire damage, or half as much damage on a successful save. One side of the wall, selected by you when you use this ability, deals 2d6 fire damage to each creature that ends its turn within 5 feet of that side or inside the wall. A creature takes the same damage when it enters the wall for the first time on a turn or ends its turn there. The other side of the wall deals no damage. The wall lasts for a number of rounds equal to your proficiency bonus.
3455 Elementalist (Water) Divine revelations:
3456 Conjure servant (Water)
3457 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Water domain)
3458 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3459 Conjure servant (small): As an action you can summon a servant made of whirling water. The creature has the following stats: STR +2, DEX +2, CON +2, INT -4, WIS +1, CHA -2, AC 12, HP 5 (+ 5 times your level), Speed 30'. Weapon attack: +4 to hit, reach 5ft, Hit: 7 (1d6 + 2) bludgeoning damage. You add your Proficiency Bonus to any Attack and Damage rolls from the servant. You need to spend a Bonus Action each round to direct your servant. The servant remains at your side for a number of rounds equal to your proficiency bonus.
3460 Conjure minor element (Water)
3461 Prerequisite: 5th level, conjure servant (Water) revelation.
3462 Your servant increases to size Medium. Its strength and constitution modifier increases by +1. Its attack and damage bonus increases by +1, and its HP increase by 1 + your level. It gains the following ability (Action):
3463 Water Breath (Recharge 6). The servant exhales a 15- foot cone of water. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 10 Dexterity saving throw, taking 4 (1d8) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
3465 Conjure element (Water)
3466 Prerequisite: 7th level, conjure minor element (Water) revelation.
3467 Your servant increases to size Large. Its strength and constitution modifier increases by +1. Its attack and damage bonus increases by +1, and its HP increase by 1 + your level. It gains the following abilities:
3468 Whelm (Recharge 4–6). Each creature in the servant’s space must make a DC 15 Strength saving throw. On a failure, a target takes 13 (2d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage. If it is Large or smaller, it is also grappled (escape DC 14). Until this grapple ends, the target is restrained and unable to breathe unless it can breathe water. If the saving throw is successful, the target is pushed out of the servant’s space. The servant can grapple one large creature or up to two medium or smaller creatures at one time. At the start of each of the servant’s turns, each target grappled by it takes 13 (2d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage. A creature within 5 feet of the servant can pull a creature or object out of it by taking an action to make a DC 14 Strength and succeeding.
3469 Conjure elder elemental (Water)
3470 Prerequisite: 9th level, conjure element (Water) revelation.
3471 Your servants strength and constitution modifier increases by +1. Its attack and damage bonus increases by +1, and its HP increase by 1 + your level. It gains the following ability (Action):
3472 Freezing Strikes (Recharge 6). The servant’s watery attacks chills their opponents bones. Each attack that hits deals an extra 5 (1d10) cold damage. A target that is hit by one or more of these attacks has its speed reduced by 10 feet until the end of the servant’s next turn.
3473 Ignore element (Water)
3474 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Water domain)
3475 Your appearance remain unchanged except that your skin takes on the slight hint of a hue which seems to resemble whatever colour the sky has above you, at any time. You become immune to suffocation and can subside on half the normal amount of water.
3476 Become one with the elements (Water)
3477 Prerequisite: 5th level, ignore element (water) revelation.
3478 Your skin seems to take on the same colour as the sky above you, at any time. You gain a swim speed equal to 30 feet. You may breathe underwater for a number of minutes equal to your Wisdom modifier, and regain minutes following a short rest
3479 Gate (Water)
3480 Prerequisite: Elementalist (Water domain)
3481 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3482 Gate: You create 1 galleon of water per proficiency bonus you have. Alternatively, you can create wet mist in a 10*10 area within 30 feet for every proficiency bonus you have. Alternatively, by touching a target, you can fill its mouth and lungs with water. The target must roll a Constitution saving throw (DC 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Wisdom bonus) or begin suffocating. At the end of each of its turn the target can repeat the saving throw. If it succeeds it is no longer suffocating.
3483 Tidal wave
3484 Prerequisite: 7th level, gate water
3485 You gain the following Act of Faith option
3486 Tidal wave. You conjure up a wave of water that crashes down on an area within range. The area can be up to 15 feet long, up to 5 feet wide, and up to 5 feet tall. Each creature in that area must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, a creature takes 2d8 bludgeoning damage and is knocked prone. On a success, a creature takes half as much damage and isn’t knocked prone. The water then spreads out across the ground in all directions, extinguishing unprotected flames in its area and within 15 feet of it, before dissipating entirely.

3601 10d6
3602 20
3603 6
3604 Stroke of Luck
3605 10d6
3607 Hit Dice: 1d8 per rogue level
3608 Proficiencies: Armor: Light armor, medium armor, shields
3609 Weapons: Simple weapons, hand crossbows, longswords, rapiers, shortswords
3610 Saving Throws: Dexterity, Intelligence
3611 Skills: Choose four from Acrobatics, Athletics, Craft (Poison), Deception, Insight, Intimidation, Investigation, Perception, Performance, Persuasion, Sleight of Hand, and Stealth
3612 Equipment
3613 (a) a rapier or (b) a shortsword
3614 (a) a shortbow and quiver of 20 arrows or (b) a shortsword
3615 Adventure pack, 50 feet of hempen rope, crowbar
3616 Leather armor, two daggers, and thieves tools
3617 Expertise
3618 At 1st level, choose two of your skill proficiencies. Your proficiency bonus is doubled for any ability check you make that uses either of the chosen proficiencies. At 6th level, you can choose two more of your proficiencies (in skills or with thieves' tools) to gain this benefit.
3619 Sneak Attack
3620 Beginning at 1st level, you know how to strike subtly and exploit a foe's distraction. Once per turn, you can deal an extra 1d6 damage to one creature you hit with an attack if you have advantage on the attack roll. The attack must use a finesse or a ranged weapon. You don't need advantage on the attack roll if another enemy of the target is within 5 feet of it, that enemy isn't incapacitated, and you don't have disadvantage on the attack roll. The amount of the extra damage increases as you gain levels in this class, as shown in the Sneak Attack column of the Rogue table.
3621 Thieves' Cant
3622 During your rogue training you learned thieves' cant, a secret mix of dialect, jargon, and code that allows you to hide messages in seemingly normal conversation. Only another creature that knows thieves' cant understands such messages. It takes four times longer to convey such a message than it does to speak the same idea plainly.
3623 In addition, you understand a set of secret signs and symbols used to convey short, simple messages, such as whether an area is dangerous or the territory of a thieves' guild, whether loot is nearby, or whether the people in an area are easy marks or will provide a safe house for thieves on the run.
3624 Cunning Action
3625 Starting at 2nd level, your quick thinking and agility allows you to move and act quickly. You can take a bonus action on each of your turns in combat. This action can be used only to take the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.
3626 Roguish Archetype
3627 At 3rd level, you choose an archetype that you emulate in the exercise of your rogue abilities, such as Thief. Your archetype choice grants you features at 3rd level and then again at 9th, 13th, and 17th level.
3628 Rogue Ability score Improvement
3629 10th level, you can increase one ability score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two ability scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can't increase an ability score above 20 using this feature.
3630 Ability Score Improvement
3631 When you reach 4th level, and again at 8th, 10th, 12th, 16th, and 19th level, you can increase one ability score of your choice by 2, or you can increase two ability scores of your choice by 1. As normal, you can't increase an ability score above 20 using this feature.
3632 Uncanny Dodge
3633 Beginning at 5th level, when an attacker that you can see hits you with an attack, you can use your reaction to halve the attack's damage against you.
3634 Evasion
3635 Starting at 7th level, you can nimbly dodge out of the way of certain area effects, such as a red dragon's fiery breath or an ice storm spell. When you are subjected to an effect that allows you to make a Dexterity saving throw to take only half damage, you instead take no damage if you succeed on the saving throw, and only half damage if you fail.
3636 Reliable Talent
3637 By 11th level, you have refined your chosen skills until they approach perfection. Whenever you make an ability check that lets you add your proficiency bonus, you can treat a d20 roll of 0 or lower as a 10.
3638 Blindsense
3639 Starting at 14th level, if you are able to hear, you are aware of the location of any hidden or invisible creature within 10 feet of you.
3640 Slippery Mind
3641 By 15th level, you have acquired greater mental strength. You gain proficiency in Wisdom saving throws.
3642 Elusive
3643 Beginning at 18th level, you are so evasive that attackers rarely gain the upper hand against you. No attack roll has advantage against you while you aren't incapacitated.
3644 Stroke of Luck
3645 At 20th level, you have an uncanny knack for succeeding when you need to. If your attack misses a target within range, you can turn the miss into a hit. Alternatively, if you fail an ability check, you can treat the d20 roll as a 20. Once you use this feature, you can't use it again until you finish a short rest.
3647 Roguish Archetypes
3648 Assassin
3649 Bonus Proficiencies
3650 When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you gain proficiency with Craft (Poison) and Deception.
3651 Assassinate⁠
3652 Starting at 3rd level, you are at your deadliest when you get the drop on your enemies. You have advantage on attack rolls against any creature that hasn’t taken a turn in the combat yet. In addition, any hit you score against a creature that is surprised is a critical hit.
3653 Infiltration Expertise⁠
3654 Starting at 9th level, you can unfailingly create false identities for yourself. You must spend seven days and 25 gp to establish the history, profession, and affiliations for an identity. You can’t establish an identity that belongs to someone else. For example, you might acquire appropriate clothing, letters of introduction⁠, and official-looking certification to establish yourself as a member of a trading house from a remote city so you can insinuate yourself into the company of other wealthy merchants.
3655 Thereafter, if you adopt the new identity as a disguise, other creatures believe you to be that person until given an obvious reason not to.
3656 Impostor⁠
3657 At 13th level, you gain the ability to unerringly mimic another person’s speech⁠, writing, and behavior. You must spend at least three hours studying these three components of the person’s behavior, listening to speech⁠, examining handwriting, and observing mannerisms.
3658 Your ruse is indiscernible to the casual observer. If a wary creature suspects something is amiss, you have advantage on any Charisma (Deception) check you make to avoid detection.
3659 Death Strike⁠
3660 Starting at 17th level, you become a master of instant death. When you attack and hit a creature that is surprised, it must make a Constitution saving throw (DC 8 + your Dexterity modifier + your proficiency bonus). On a failed save, double the damage of your attack against the creature.
3662 The Dagger Master
3663 Dagger Expert
3664 Starting at 3rd level, you are an expert with small blades. The damage die for daggers increases to 1d6 for you. When you reach level 11 in this class, it increases to 1d8.
3665 Twin Blade
3666 Also at 3rd level, when you engage in two-weapon fighting, you can add your ability modifier to the damage of the second attack. If you are wielding two weapons with the finesse and light properties, you gain one of the following benefits of your choice when you engage in two-weapon fighting:
3667 You gain a +1 bonus to your armor class until your next turn begins or you are no longer holding the weapons. When you reach level 11 in this class, the bonus to your armor class increases to +2.
3668 You can immediately make one additional attack with your offhand weapon as part of the same bonus action. You cannot add your ability modifier to the damage of this attack unless you are at least level 11 in this class.
3669 Hidden Blades
3670 By 9th level, you always have an extra dagger on your person, and can reveal it with a flourish to surprise enemies. You can hide a number of daggers about your person equal to your proficiency bonus. A creature is not aware you're armed unless it makes a successful Intelligence (Investigation) check with a DC equal to 8 + your Sleight of Hand modifier. If you do not appear to be armed at the start of your turn in combat, you can make a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) check with a DC equal to the passive perception of a creature within 20 feet of you that can see you (no action required). On a success, you draw one of your hidden daggers with a surprising flourish and have advantage on the first attack roll you make against that creature with it. Once you have used this ability, you cannot use it again until the next time you roll initiative.
3671 Swordbreaker
3672 Also at 9th level, your light blades can disarm your opponents with a quick flourish. When you use your Uncanny Dodge to half the damage of a melee weapon attack, you can force the attacker to make a Dexterity saving throw (DC = 8 + your Dexterity modifier + your proficiency bonus) as part of the same reaction. On a failure, you force the attacker to drop the weapon they attacked you with if the weapon is Medium or smaller. The weapon lands 10 feet away in a direction of your choice. You can do this a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus and regain all uses when you finish a long rest.
3673 Twin Feint
3674 By 13th level, you are an expert at using one weapon to distract an enemy while you attack with another. When you take the Attack action on your turn and miss a creature with an attack as part of that action, you have advantage on the first attack you make against that creature as part of the bonus action of two-weapon fighting. You can only use this ability if you are wielding two weapons with the light and finesse properties.
3675 Deadly Blades At 17th level, your every strike with a dagger is deadly. When you make a Sneak Attack with a weapon with the finesse and light properties, your Sneak Attack dice increase to d8s.
3677 The Brute
3678 Brute Force
3679 Starting at 3rd level, you gain proficiency with medium armor and Athletics. You can add double your proficiency bonus to any ability check you make in Athletics, and when you make a Charisma (Intimidation) check, you can use your Strength modifier instead of Charisma if your Strength is higher. In addition, you can make Sneak Attacks with any melee weapon with which you are proficient unless it has the heavy or special property.
3680 Shakedown
3681 Also at 3rd level, you can use your Cunning Action to attempt to grapple a creature, and you gain an additional way to use your Sneak Attack; you don't need advantage on the attack roll to use your Sneak Attack against a creature you are grappling if you don't have disadvantage on the attack roll. All the other rules for Sneak Attack still apply to you.
3682 Beatdown
3683 Beginning at 9th level, when you deal Sneak Attack damage to a creature you are not grappling with a melee attack, you can attempt to knock the target prone. The target must make a Strength saving throw (DC = 8 + your Strength modifier + your proficiency bonus) or be knocked prone. A Huge or larger creature makes the saving throw at advantage. On a failure, it is knocked prone. You can do this a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus, and regain all uses when you finish a long rest.
3684 Forced Cover
3685 Also at 9th level, you know how to keep a body between yourself and a threat. While you are grappling a Medium or Small creature, you have half cover against attacks from other creatures. If an attack misses you due to the +2 bonus to your armor class offered by half cover, you can use your reaction to force the attack to hit the creature you are grappling.
3686 I'll Hold 'Em For You
3687 By 13th level, you are used to working with others to beat down the opposition. A creature you are grappling has disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than you, and when an ally makes a melee attack against a creature you are grappling, you can use your reaction to grant that ally advantage on the attack roll.
3688 Brutalize
3689 At 17th level, your attacks are most fearsome against vulnerable opponents. Your Sneak Attacks deal an additional 3d6 damage against creatures that are grappled or prone.
3691 Inquisitive
3692 As an archetypal Inquisitive, you excel at rooting out secrets and unravelling mysteries. You rely on your sharp eye for detail, but also on your finely honed ability to read the words and deeds of other creatures to determine their true intent. You excel at defeating creatures that hide among and prey upon ordinary folk, and your mastery of lore and your sharp eye make you well equipped to expose and end hidden evils.
3693 Ear for Deceit
3694 When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you develop a keen ear for picking out lies. Whenever you make a Wisdom (Insight) check to determine whether a creature is lying, treat a roll of 7 or lower on the d20 as an 8.
3695 Eye for Detail
3696 Starting at 3rd level, you can use a bonus action to make a Wisdom (Perception) check to spot a hidden creature or object or to make an Intelligence (Investigation) check to uncover or decipher clues.
3697 Insightful Fighting
3698 At 3rd level, you gain the ability to decipher an opponent's tactics and develop a counter to them. As a bonus action, you make a Wisdom (Insight) check against a creature you can see that isn't incapacitated, contested by the target's Charisma (Deception) check. If you succeed, you can use your Sneak Attack against that target even if you don't have advantage on the attack roll, but not if you have disadvantage on it.
3699 This benefit lasts for 1 minute or until you successfully use this feature against a different target.
3700 Steady Eye
3701 At 9th level, you gain advantage on any Wisdom (Perception) or Intelligence (Investigation) check if you move no more than half your speed on the same turn.
3702 Unerring Eye
3703 At 13th level, your senses are almost impossible to foil. As an action, you sense the presence of illusions, shapechangers not in their original form, and other magic designed to deceive the senses within 30 feet of you, provided you aren't blinded or deafened. You sense that an effect is attempting to trick you, but you gain no insight into what is hidden or into its true nature.
3704 You can use this feature a number of times equal to your Wisdom modifier (minimum of once), and you regain all expended uses of it when you finish a long rest.
3705 Eye for Weakness
3706 At 17th level, you learn to exploit a creature's weaknesses by carefully studying its tactics and movement. While your Insightful Fighting feature applies to a creature, your Sneak Attack damage against that creature increases by 3d6.
3708 Mastermind
3709 Master of Intrigue
3710 When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you gain proficiency with the Deception skill. You also learn two languages of your choice.
3711 Additionally, you can unerringly mimic the speech patterns and accent of a creature that you hear speak for at least 1 minute, enabling you to pass yourself off as a native speaker of a particular land, provided that you know the language.
3712 Master of Tactics
3713 Starting at 3rd level, you can use the Help action as a bonus action. Additionally, when you use the Help action to aid an ally in attacking a creature, the target of that attack can be within 30 feet of you, rather than 5 feet of you, if the target can see or hear you.
3714 Insightful Manipulator
3715 Starting at 9th level, if you spend at least 1 minute observing or interacting with another creature outside combat, you can learn certain information about its capabilities compared to your own. The DM tells you if the creature is your equal, superior, or inferior in regard to two of the following characteristics of your choice: Intelligence score, Wisdom score, Charisma score, Class levels (if any). At the DM's option, you might also realize you know a piece of the creature's history or one of its personality traits, if it has any.
3716 Misdirection
3717 Beginning at 13th level, you can sometimes cause another creature to suffer an attack meant for you. When you are targeted by an attack while a creature within 5 feet of you is granting you cover against that attack, you can use your reaction to have the attack target that creature instead of you.
3718 Soul of Deceit
3719 Starting at 17th level, your thoughts can't be read by telepathy or other means, unless you allow it. You can present false thoughts by making a Charisma (Deception) check contested by the mind reader's Wisdom (Insight) check.
3720 Additionally, no matter what you say, magic that would determine if you are telling the truth indicates you are being truthful if you so choose, and you can't be compelled to tell the truth by magic.
3722 The Outrider
3723 Light Cavalry
3724 Starting at 3rd level, you gain proficiency with scimitars and Animal Handling, and you can add double your proficiency bonus to any ability check you make in Animal Handling. If you are not wearing medium or heavy armor and are mounted on a creature that is not wearing medium or heavy barding, your mount can use your Stealth modifier for any Dexterity (Stealth) check it makes, and when you use your Cunning Action to Disengage, your mount gains the benefit of the Disengage action as well.
3725 Harrying Strike
3726 Also at 3rd level, you are skilled at striking multiple enemies quickly. When you deal Sneak Attack damage to a creature on your turn, you can immediately move up to half your speed without provoking opportunity attacks and make a weapon attack against another creature. If you are mounted, your mount can move half its speed instead, and neither you nor your mount provoke opportunity attacks. On a hit, the creature takes the weapon's damage and half the number of Sneak Attack dice for your level (round up). You can use this ability a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus and regain all uses when you finish a long rest. When you reach 9th level in this class, if you roll initiative and have no uses of this ability remaining, you regain one use.
3727 Forerunner
3728 By 9th level, you are as light on your feet as you are in the saddle. Your movement speed increases by 10 feet. If you are mounted, your mount's movement speed increases by 10 feet if you are not wearing medium or heavy armor and the mount is not wearing medium or heavy barding.
3729 Stunt Riding
3730 Also at 9th level, any mount you are riding gains the benefit of your Evasion feature, and if your mount is hit by an attack, you can use your reaction to half the damage it takes.
3731 Beleaguer
3732 By 13th level, you can create openings in enemies for your allies to exploit. When you use your Harrying Strike, the creature you dealt your Sneak Attack damage to is left open to attack. The next time a creature other than you makes an attack against it before the start of your next turn, they have advantage on the attack roll. If the additional attack you made as part of the Harrying Strike hit, the target suffers the same vulnerability.
3733 Overrun
3734 At 17th level, you are a master of keeping your enemies off balance. When you use your Harrying Strike, the creature you dealt your Sneak Attack damage to takes an additional 3d6 damage and must make a Strength saving throw (DC = 8 + your Dexterity modifier + your proficiency bonus) if it is Large or smaller. On a failure, it is knocked prone. If you are mounted and dealt the Sneak Attack with a melee attack, they have disadvantage on the saving throw.
3736 Scout
3737 Skirmisher
3738 Starting at 3rd level, you are difficult to pin down during a fight. You can move up to half your speed as a reaction when an enemy ends its turn within 5 feet of you. This movement doesn’t provoke opportunity attacks.
3739 Survivalist
3740 When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you gain proficiency in the Nature and Survival skills if you don't already have it. Your proficiency bonus is doubled for any ability check you make that uses either of those proficiencies.
3741 Superior Mobility
3742 At 9th level, your walking speed increases by 10 feet. If you have a climbing or swimming speed, this increase applies to that speed as well.
3743 Ambush Master
3744 Starting at 13th level, you excel at leading ambushes and acting first in a fight.
3745 You have advantage on initiative rolls. In addition, the first creature you hit during the first round of a combat becomes easier for you and others to strike; attack rolls against that target have advantage until the start of your next turn.
3746 Sudden Strike
3747 Starting at 17th level, you can strike with deadly speed. If you take the Attack action on your turn, you can make one additional attack as a bonus action. This attack can benefit from your Sneak Attack even if you have already used it this turn, but you can't use your Sneak Attack against the same target more than once in a turn.
3749 Swashbuckler
3750 Fancy Footwork
3751 When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you learn how to land a strike and then slip away without reprisal. During your turn, if you make a melee attack against a creature, that creature can't make opportunity attacks against you for the rest of your turn.
3752 Rakish Audacity
3753 Starting at 3rd level, your confidence propels you into battle. You can give yourself a bonus to your initiative rolls equal to your Charisma modifier.
3754 You also gain an additional way to use your Sneak Attack; you don't need advantage on the attack roll to use your Sneak Attack against a creature if you are within 5 feet of it, no other creatures are within 5 feet of you, and you don't have disadvantage on the attack roll. All the other rules for Sneak Attack still apply to you.
3755 Panache
3756 At 9th level, your charm becomes extraordinarily beguiling. As an action, you can make a Charisma (Persuasion) check contested by a creature's Wisdom (Insight) check. The creature must be able to hear you, and the two of you must share a language.
3757 If you succeed on the check and the creature is hostile to you, it has disadvantage on attack rolls against targets other than you and can't make opportunity attacks against targets other than you. This effect lasts for 1 minute, until one of your companions attacks the target or affects it with a spell, or until you and the target are more than 60 feet apart.
3758 If you succeed on the check and the creature isn't hostile to you, it is charmed by you for 1 minute. While charmed, it regards you as a friendly acquaintance. This effect ends immediately if you or your companions do anything harmful to it.
3759 Elegant Maneuver
3760 Starting at 13th level, you can use a bonus action on your turn to gain advantage on the next Dexterity (Acrobatics) or Strength (Athletics) check you make during the same turn.
3761 Master Duelist
3762 Beginning at 17th level, your mastery of the blade lets you turn failure into success in combat. If you miss with an attack roll, you can roll it again with advantage. Once you do so, you can't use this feature again until you finish a short or long rest.
3764 Thief
3765 Fast Hands
3766 Starting at 3rd level, you can use the bonus action granted by your Cunning Action to make a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) check, use Sleight of hand to disarm a trap or open a lock, or take the Use an Object action.
3767 Second-Story Work
3768 When you choose this archetype at 3rd level, you gain the ability to climb faster than normal; climbing no longer costs you extra movement. In addition, when you make a running jump, the distance you cover increases by a number of feet equal to your Dexterity modifier.
3769 Supreme Sneak
3770 Starting at 9th level, you have advantage on a Dexterity (Stealth) check if you move no more than half your speed on the same turn.
3771 Use Magic Device
3772 By 13th level, you have learned enough about the workings of magic that you can improvise the use of items even when they are not intended for you. You ignore all class, race, and level requirements on the use of magic items.
3773 Thief's Reflexes
3774 When you reach 17th level, you have become adept at laying ambushes and quickly escaping danger. You can take two turns during the first round of any combat. You take your first turn at your normal initiative and your second turn at your initiative minus 10. You can't use this feature when you are surprised.
