6616 6
6617 Mind Over Matter
6618 6
6619 6
6620 9
6621 1d10
6622 N = Noetics
6623 PP = Psychic Powers
6624 PR = Psychic Reservoir
6625 PD = Psi Dice Size
6627 Hit Dice: 1d6 + your Constitution modifier
6628 Armor: Light armor, medium armor, shields
6629 Weapons: Simple weapons, short swords
6630 Saving Throws: Constitution, Intelligence
6631 Skills: Choose three from Animal Handling, Athletics, Insight, Investigation, Perception, and Stealth
6632 Equipment
6633 Dagger, backpack, 4 candles, chalk, clothes (common)
6634 Mind Strike
6635 Starting at first level you gain the ability to force the power of your mind into that of others. At level 1 you choose one of the following options. You may use this ability as a bonus action on a creature or object within 60ft.
6636 Ballistic: You hurl a small or smaller unattended object within range at a target within 60ft of the object with a psionic attack roll, dealing 1d6+Int damage that may be bludgeoning, slashing, or piercing depending on the object.
6637 Telepathic: The target must make an Intelligence Save or take 1d6+Int Psychic Damage. Concentration checks against this damage are made with Disadvantage.
6638 Telekinetic: The target must make a Strength Save or take 1d6+Int Force Damage and you move the target 5 feet in a direction of your choosing.
6639 Thymokinetic: Make a ranged Psionic Attack roll to deal 1d6+Int Energy Damage. When you choose this option you must also choose between Cold, Fire, and Lightning. Fire can light flammable objects on fire. Cold reduces the target's movement speed by 10ft if they take damage. Lightning causes your target to become Rattled.
6640 Psionics
6641 At 1st level you have learned to tap into your reservoir of psychic energy to manifest psionic powers. Psionic Ability Intelligence is your psionic ability for your psionic powers. You use your Intelligence whenever a power refers to your psionic ability. In addition, you use your Intelligence modifier when setting the saving throw DC for a psionic power you manifest and when making an attack roll with one.
6642 Psionics save DC = 8 + your proficiency bonus + Intelligence modifier
6643 Psionics attack modifier = your proficiency bonus + Intelligence modifier
6644 Learning and Using Powers
6645 You begin play with one Power. A power is a psionic ability that can be used any number of times without ever being exhausted. You gain additional powers at levels 2 and 5 and then every four levels thereafter which cannot be altered without intensive training or mystical intervention. Powers have ranges, targets, and some powers require known disciplines to learn. To learn a new power which has two or three required disciplines you must already know one power from each discipline. A power you know tied to two disciplines, such as Astral Projection, counts as one power from each of its disciplines for the purposes of learning new powers. In this case Telepathy and Telekinesis.
6646 Manifest: You use your psionics by taking the Manifest action. When you do so, you choose a Power to manifest as well as any Augments you wish to apply to the effect. You then assign your Psi Dice to your Augments and your power takes effect. Once that happens, you roll your psi dice to resolve the action.
6647 Concentration: Most powers manifest instantaneously but some have a duration measured in minutes. Many such powers require concentration to maintain the power for the full duration.
6648 Psychic Reservoir
6649 You gain access to a reservoir of psychic energy. The Psychic Reservoir column of the Esper table shows the number of Psionic Dice you possess based on your level. You have two Psi Dice at level 1, each 1d4. You gain more and larger dice as you level up. When you take a Short Rest you may trade Hit Dice to recover Psi Dice on a 1 to 1 basis rather than using that Hit Die to recover HP. When you take a long rest you recover all of your Psi Dice.
6650 Using Psi Dice
6651 Each power you possess has Augments which allow you to modify the way your power works, such as altering the range or function of that power. To activate an Augment, you must assign one of your Psi Dice to that Augment, and a Psi Die cannot be assigned to multiple Augments. Some Augments are particularly powerful, and require two, or even three, Psi Dice to be assigned to them for use. After you manifest your power, roll any Psi Dice assigned to the power's Augments.
6652 On a roll of 1 or 2 you remove that Psi Die from your reservoir. If you roll the highest number on the die, you add a Psi Dice to your reservoir up to your current maximum. It is possible to both lose and gain Psi Dice on a single power. When this happens, you lose Psi Dice first, then gain Psi Dice, allowing you to recover dice lost on the power you just manifested.
6653 Basic Augments
6654 You always have access to the following Augments in addition to any augment specific to a power. You can use any of these Augments on a given power.
6655 Range: +60ft per Psi Dice (Unless the target is Self)
6656 Harm: +1d8 per Psi Dice (Assuming the power deals damage)
6657 Restoration: +1d8 per Psi Dice (Assuming the power heals damage)
6658 Split: The power targets an additional creature or object per Psi Dice
6660 Archetype
6661 At first level you gain access to an Esper Archetype which will grant you additional unique abilities at levels 3, 7, and 15. Five Esper Archetypes can be found after this class entry, the Chirurgeon, the Mystic,
6662 Noetics
6663 While an Esper's power comes from within, it is through crossing thresholds of thought that they become empowered. Such knowledge is known as a Noetic and can be found in meditation, lucid dreaming, and visions. You gain your first Noetic at 2nd level, and additional Noetics as you gain Esper levels. Whenever you gain a level you may trade a Noetic you know for a new one you meet the prerequisites to learn.
6664 Psychic Focus
6665 Also at level 2 you gain the ability to focus on one of the four most basic psionic disciplines. Gaining Your Psychic Focus requires 1 action of careful thought and requires concentration. It grants the following benefits: Once per turn when you roll a 2 on a Psi Dice used with your chosen discipline you may reroll that die as a Reaction. You must take the result of the reroll. Once per long rest when manifesting your chosen discipline you may treat all Psi Dice used to augment that power as having rolled a 3 instead of rolling.
6666 In addition, you gain a benefit specific to your chosen Discipline:
6667 Empathic: You gain Expertise on all Insight and Persuasion checks.
6668 Kinesthetic: You gain Expertise on all Acrobatics and Athletic checks.
6669 Telepathic: You gain Telepathy with a 120ft range. A creature does not have to share a Language to respond to your telepathy, but must be able to speak at least one language.
6670 Telekinetic: Your melee weapon Reach increases by 15ft and all melee weapon attacks become psionic  melee weapon attacks as you telekinetically manipulate them.
6672 Some abilities allow you to Expend your psychic focus. When you do so, you may not recover Your psychic focus until you take a short or long rest.
6673 Psychometry
6674 At 4th level you gain the ability to glean information about people, places, or objects in your vicinity, typically these impressions are faint, but if you touch the target. you gain more information. You may choose People, Places, or Things when you gain this ability, and may change it at the end of a long rest.
6675 People: You are more aware of important events that have happened in a person's life, and can make contact with spirits related to them. You gain Expertise ou Insight and Perception checks to gain information about a person. You may also use Speak with Dead as a Ritual with no components to ask questions of a spirit related to a being you are in physical contact with about their life and choices. This spirit can be any deceased relative, close friend, or dire enemy and negates the need for a corpse to target.
6676 Places: You are aware of important events that have happened in a location. When you make a DC 15 Investigation check to search an area, you can recognize if any of the following conditions are met in the area searched: Any creature from another plane has passed through the area within the last hour. Any creature has died in the area within the last 100 years and how long ago it was. Any creature has experienced intense emotional trauma in the area within the last 24 hours. Any supernatural effects are at play in the area (e.g. a magical or psionic illusion, aura, or ward)
6677 Things: You learn a great deal about the people who owned an object. By holding an object you can spend an action to learn if it was used to harm anyone within the past week, or if it was present during a time of emotional turmoil. You may commune with the object over the course of 10 minutes to witness a specific event, or series of events, that the object was present for in the last five years. During these 10 minutes you are unable to see the world around you, though you may still hear and speak normally, even narrating events as they play out in the vision.
6678 Attack Modes
6679 At 5th level choose two of the following improvements to your Mind Strike feature. You gain another improvement from this list at 11th level and again at 16th level. When you gain a level in this class, you can choose one of the attack modes you know and replace it with another attack mode from this list that you could learn at that level. You may apply any two attack modes you know when you use your Mind Strike.
6680 Boggle Mind
6681 Your mind strikes are aimed to disorient and afflict your target. If your victim takes damage from your mind strike they become Frightened until the start of your next turn, if they fail a Wisdom save. Once a creature has been frightened in this way it becomes unable to take Reactions until the start of your next turn, on any additional use of this Attack Mode.
6682 Choking Grasp
6683 Prerequisite: Telekinetic Mind Strike, Concentration
6684 Your telekinetic strength increases, allowing you to suspend a target in midair. When targeting a Large or smaller creature, you may now choose "up" as the direction to move your target. When you do so, your target becomes grappled and unable to breathe until the end of your next turn if they fail an Intelligence save.
6685 Drain Psyche
6686 Prerequisite: Telepathic Mind Strike
6687 You've learned how to draw energy through a connection made with a victim, like a psychic vampire. When you deal damage with your mind strike you can steal a single hit dice from them, up to your current limit. If the target also loses concentration, you can steal two. The hit dice you gain are d6’s, regardless of what your target's hit dice are.
6688 Ego Whip
6689 Lashing out at your enemies, you can strike multiple minds at once. You may now use the Split basic augment on your mind strikes.
6690 Explosive Fury
6691 Prerequisite: Thymokinetic Mind Strike
6692 Your emotions burst outward from your target, catching others in your thymokinetic blast. Your mind strike takes the form of a 15ft cube centered on your target.
6693 Fling For
6694 Prerequisite: Ballistic Mind Strike
6695 Rather than only hurling objects you can also hurl people. When you use your mind strike you may instead choose a medium size or smaller creature to throw at another creature or object within 15ft of your target if they fail an Intelligence save. On a successful hit, the struck creature takes 1d8+int bludgeoning damage while the flung target takes bludgeoning damage equal to your Intelligence modifier, and both creatures become adjacent to each other. On a miss, the flung target is still placed adjacent to the other creature or object but neither takes damage.
6696 Lash Out
6697 Reacting to pain and frustration sometimes means lashing out at those who hurt you. When struck by a ranged attack by a target you can see within 60ft. you can use your reaction to make a Mind Strike against that target. This mind strike cannot use any other Attack Modes.
6698 Mind Probe
6699 Like a thief you rob those you harm with eager abandon. When a creature takes damage from your mind strike you may glean a single piece of information from their mind. This can be a short phrase, such as a proper name or a password, or a set of numbers such as a code. You can use this attack mode once per short or long rest and must have a specific piece of information in mind when you use this ability.
6700 Noetic Lange
6701 The reach of your mind cannot be underestimated. You may now use the Range basic augment on mind strikes. This Attack Form cannot be combined with Psychic Scream or Psychic Shockwave.
6702 Psionic Blast
6703 The power of your mind is overwhelming. You may reroll the damage of your Mind Strike but must take the second roll.
6704 Psychic Scream
6705 Unleash your anger, pain, or fear on your foes. Your mind strike takes the form of a 20ft cone with a Wisdom save for half damage. If you are currently Frightened the effect ends on you, but targets who fail their saving throw against your Psychic Scream become Frightened until the start of your next turn.
6706 Psychic Shockwave
6707 Surrounded by foes, you unleash a blast of psionic force that presses them all back. Your mind strike takes the form of a 10ft radius burst centered on your square. Any creature in the area must make a Constitution save. On a failure they are pushed directly away from you up to 15ft in addition to taking any damage or concentration checks. On a success they take half of any damage and are pushed 5ft away. The raw force of your Psychic Shockwave takes a toll, however, and it cannot be combined with any other Attack Mode.
6708 Intellect Fortress
6709 At 7th level your mental prowess expands to increase your resilience. You gain Resistance to Psychic Damage and your Thoughts can no longer be read against your will.
6710 ID Insinuation
6711 At 9th level your ability to read other people's interests and motivations grows substantially. When you roll a Deception, Intimidation, or Persuasion check which relies on base desires (such as Anger, Greed, Lust, Hunger, and Sleep) you may reroll the check and take the second result, even if it is worse, unless your target is resistant or immune to Psychic Damage.
6712 Psychic Crush
6713 At 10th level your Mind Strike improves to deal an additional 1d6 of damage.
6714 Thought Shield
6715 At 13th level, your Intellect Fortress applies to all allies within 10ft of you, granting them resistance to Psychic damage and Immunity to Mind-Reading effects.
6716 Tower of Iron Will
6717 At 14th level you gain proficiency in Wisdom Saving Throws.
6718 Twin Mind
6719 At 17th level you gain the ability to split your focus between your conscious and subconscious mind. When you manifest a power which requires concentration while you are already concentrating, you can shunt the manifested power into your subconscious mind, allowing you to concentrate on both effects. If your concentration is broken, you lose both effects.
6720 The Speed of Thought
6721 At 18th level you can take one additional bonus action on your turn and one additional reaction in the same round. You can use this feature once between short or long rests.
6722 Mind over Matter
6723 At 20th level you become the epitome of the adage Mind Over Matter. You can, as an action taken even while dying, restore your full hit points, hit dice, and refill your Psychic Reservoir as if you had taken a long rest. Once you use this ability, you may not do so again until you take a Long Rest.
6724 Noetics
6725 Alter Aura
6726 By changing your own aura you can create a sense of welcome or unease in other people. As an action you shift your personal aura to present as either appealing or revolting and gain the following effects for up to 10 minutes based on your concentration.
6727 Appealing: Regardless of your physical appearance you develop a form of sheer personal magnetism that resonates in other people. The sheer power of which can briefly awe those in your presence and cause them to re-evaluate you as a person. You become the center of attention fairly quickly, and creatures within 20ft of you have disadvantage on perception checks to notice other people.
6728 Revolting: Regardless of your physical appearance you become a person to be reviled and disdained, with people going out of their way not to notice you in the way they might overlook those in poverty. You gain expertise on Stealth checks. This revulsion is so strong that people associated with you also gain the Stealth expertise.
6729 Aura Sight
6730 You are more aware than others of people's aura and understand the significance thereof. While you maintain a Telepathic or Empathic focus you can observe a creature within 30ft to gain the following information: Their current emotional state as if you had rolled a natural 20 on Insight. Whether they are able to use Psionics or cast Spells. How many Psi Dice they have available, or the highest spell slot. Learning each piece of information requires one action spent reading a creature's aura.
6731 Clairvoyance
6732 Prerequisite: Level 4
6733 Remote viewing is a rare gift that grants unique benefits. By spending 1 minute focusing your mind you are able to see beyond your own body. Using this noetic creates an invisible and intangible eye which has a fly speed of 120 ft. at your command and allows you to see both the world around you and what the eye sees as overlaid images. This lasts for up to 10 minutes.
6734 Alternatively, if you have an object owned by a person in your possession you may spend 1 minute focusing to look through that creature's eyes by expending your psychic focus. This use lasts 5 rounds before the connection is broken.
6735 Danger Sense
6736 You are preternaturally aware of potential dangers and respond accordingly. You may always use your Intelligence modifier when rolling Initiative Checks, and can no longer be surprised. In addition, you can expend your psychic focus when one or more of your allies would be surprised to immediately end that condition for your allies.
6737 Dreamwalker
6738 Your exceptional ability to lucid dream allows you to step through the pseudo-reality of the dream world into the dreams of others. By touching a slumbering creature as an action, you can step into its dream world and perceive its dreams. While there, you can speak to the creature or otherwise interact with it as if it were conscious. Everything you do in the dream both you and the creature will remember upon waking. If you expend your psychic focus, you can bring up to 4 additional creatures into the dream space with you, so long as they are in physical contact with you.
6739 Ectoplasmic Form
6740 Prerequisite: Level 12
6741 By expending your psychic focus you can temporarily take on a spectral or ghostly form. As an action on your turn, you become incorporeal and gain resistance to all damage types. You cannot make physical attacks or interact with your environment directly while in this form, but you can use your psionic powers, noetics, and mind strikes normally. This form lasts up to 1 minute with concentration. If you are inside an object when you revert to your normal form you are immediately shunted into the nearest unoccupied space and take 10d10 force damage.
6742 Excise Influence
6743 Prerequisite: Level 3
6744 By expending your psychic focus you can cast out unwanted presences. When you or another creature within 10 ft. of you is possessed, dominated, or otherwise controlled by another being you can attempt to force the unwanted influence out. The target entity must make a Charisma save against your Psionics DC. On a failed save, possessors are cast out and the domination effects end, and the possessor or dominator cannot attempt to control another again for 1 hour. If you spend 10 minutes preparing with the victim beforehand, you can make the Intelligence Check with advantage.
6745 Inertial Barrier
6746 Prerequisite: Level 3
6747 By expending your psychic focus you can attempt to protect yourself and others by attempting to form air itself into a barrier. Choose a 15ft line within your reach. You create a one-way barrier on this line which is 10ft tall and provides half cover against ranged attacks and area of effect abilities, but does not block line of sight. This barrier lasts so long as you spend your action maintaining it and do not move from your current position.
6748 Limit Break
6749 Prerequisite: Level 7
6750 By expending your psychic focus you are able to push yourself beyond your current limits. You may take an action to Limit Break on your turn. When you do so, you may gain up to 2 temporary Psi Dice, each 1d4, at the cost of 2 Hit Dice each. These Psi Dice last up to 1 minute and can be used normally. You may use this Noetic once per long rest.
6751 Mind Link
6752 Prerequisite: Level 7
6753 Through careful probing and connection you create a semi-permanent link between yourself and another person. You become deeply telepathically linked and can communicate over any distance, see through each other's eyes for up to 10 rounds per short rest, and are aware of any injuries or pain the other is going through. Creating this link requires 10 minutes in physical contact with your target and can only be broken by your decision, which requires no action and immediately breaks the bond.
6755 Psionic Stride
6756 By bundling kinesthetic power in your legs and releasing it, explosively, you're able to clear massive distances. You gain expertise on any check related to Jumping, and can spend a bonus action to refocus your mind into your legs. Doing so doubles your jumping distances for up to 1 minute as long as you concentrate.
6757 Read Minds
6758 By focusing on a creature of intelligence 3 or higher within 30ft you can attempt to read its mind as an action. On the first round you pick up any surface thoughts but do not alert the creature to your intrusion. On the second round and any following round you may attempt to locate a specific thought or memory, which causes the target to make a Wisdom save. On a successful save the target becomes aware you are in their mind and the connection is broken, but on a failed save you locate the specific thought or memory. You may use this Noetic once per short or long rest.
6759 Suspend Animation
6760 Prerequisite: Level 3
6761 When you are reduced to 0 hit points you may take a special reaction to expend your psychic focus. If you do so, you immediately become stabilized but appear to be utterly dead to all onlookers. You gain fatigue, normally, but immediately begin taking a short rest. At the end of which you can expend hit dice normally. If you choose, this short rest can be 1 hour, 8 hours, or 24 hours long. You may also enter into this state of your own volition at any time by spending 1 minute of preparation. While you are in this state you still breathe, and your heart still beats, but so slowly as to be imperceptible. You also require no food or water and use no supply until you wake.
6762 Telekinetic Mastery
6763 Prerequisite: Level 5, Telekinesis Power
6764 Your skill with Telekinesis is something to be admired. You may now use the Telekinesis power as a bonus action to move and manipulate objects, but not deal damage to creatures or objects.
6766 Esper Archetypes
6767 Chirurgeon
6768 Medicalist and Healer
6769 Chirurgery
6770 At 1st level, you gain access to the Chirurgery power, which does not count against your number of powers known and you do not need to meet its prerequisites.
6771 Field Aid
6772 At 3rd level, you can grant or force rerolls on any hit dice expended within 30ft of you as a reaction. When you use this ability the target, or targets, must take the results of the second roll. You may do this to a number of hit dice equal to or less than your proficiency bonus. This effect can be applied to any hit dice roll including but not limited to short rests, the Chirurgery power, and spells which affect hit dice.
6773 Gift of Life
6774 Starting at 6th level, you can transfer hit dice between different characters in order to provide healing power. As a 1 point augment to the Chirurgery power you may transfer a number of your own hit dice equal to or less than your proficiency bonus to your target or targets. In addition, you may transfer hit dice between willing characters during a short rest.
6775 HEALING RESERVOIR
6776 At 15th level, you may ignore the loss of up to 2 dice when used to augment the Chirurgery power each time you use it.
6778 Medium
6779 Spirit-Speaker and Diviner
6780 Out of body Experience
6781 At 1st level, you gain access to the Astral Projection power, which does not count against your number of powers known.
6782 Gift of Prophecy
6783 At 3rd level, you may use your psychic power to directly aid your allies, or befoul your foes. As an action, you can temporarily lose access to one of your Psi Dice to instead lend it to your ally or apply it to your foe. During the next minute, your ally can roll your Psi Dice to gain a bonus on an attack or save. When you use this ability on a foe, you instead roll the Psi Dice to apply a penalty to an attack or save. On a roll of a 1 or 2 the dice is lost. Otherwise you regain it at the start of your next turn.
6784 Psychometric Mastery
6785 Starting at 6th level, you no longer choose between People, Places, and Things when using your Psychometry and instead gain all benefits at all times.
6786 Grand Vision
6787 At 15th level, you gain the ability to perceive a grand vision of reality. This vision conveys upon you important knowledge of the future which is uncannily accurate. You may choose any combination of three of the following options:
6788 Great Success: You may alter any d20 roll within 90ft into a Natural 20 as a reaction
6789 Terrible Failure: You may alter any d20 roll within 90ft into a Natural 1 as a reaction
6790 Revelation: Important information is chosen and revealed by the Narrator.
6791 Death: A creature at 0hp automatically stabilizes, but appears to be dead to all observers.
6793 You may only have a Grand Vision during a Long Rest at a Safe Haven. And must wait at least 7 days before having another. Each chosen option persists until you have another vision, or it is triggered and used.
6795 Mindhammer
6796 Mind Strikes and Storms
6797 Mind Strike Mastery
6798 At 1st level, the Mindhammer stretches the most basic powers of their mind to be ready for anything. Choose two Mind Strike options in addition to the one granted at level 1. While you can only use one Mind Strike per turn, you now have the ability to choose between your three selections. You may choose multiple variations of Thymokinesis as a part of this feature.
6799 Hammerstrike
6800 At 3rd level you may choose two of the Attack Modes from the following list: Boggle Mind, Ego Whip, Mind Probe, Noetic Lance, Psionic Blast
6801 Psionic Tornado
6802 At 6th level your ability to use your Mind Strike grows intensely and is combined with your telekinetic prowess. You can unleash a wave of power so intense it blasts your enemies back. As an action you expend your psychic focus to create a line of force 15ft wide and 60ft long using a chosen mind strike. All creatures within the area must make a Dexterity save, for ballistic or thymokinetic psionic tornadoes, or a Wisdom save, for telekinetic and telepathic psionic tornadoes. On a failure they take damage appropriate to your chosen mind strike and are thrown up to 15ft away from your line's area. On a successful saving throw they take half damage and are pushed out of the line's area to the nearest safe square. You may apply only the boggle mind or psionic blast attack modes to this special mind strike.
6803 Psychic Storm
6804 At 15th level your power becomes truly terrifying to behold through the use of the psychic storm. Once you've started the storm it continues until you choose to stop spending actions to maintain it, or fall unconscious. You are immune to the effects of your Psychic Storm.
6805 As an action you become the center of a powerful display of telekinetic force. This storm becomes more powerful each turn you spend on it, growing outward to terrible effect.
6806 - This storm begins as a 15ft cube centered on your square. Creatures and unattended objects within the area are battered with telekinetic force for 6d6 Force damage and pushed up to 15ft in a direction of your choosing. This cube is treated as difficult terrain and strong winds, it also creates disadvantage on all perception checks.
6807 - On the second round the storm expands to a 35ft cube centered on your square. In addition to the 6d6 force damage creatures and objects in the area are battered with objects torn free for an additional 2d6 bludgeoning damage.
6808 - On the third round, and all following rounds, the storm expands to a 55ft cube centered on your square. In addition to the 6d6 force damage the bludgeoning damage increases to 4d6, and creatures within the storm take a further 3d6 of psychic damage each round.
6809 Creatures in the area of your storm can attempt to make a Dexterity saving throw each round to halve any damage the storm deals.
6810 Each round after the third costs you one Psi Die from your reservoir as if you had rolled a 1.
6811 Once you have created a Psychic Storm you cannot do so again until you've completed a long rest in a haven.
6813 Psychic Warrior
6814 Martial Mentalist
6815 Psychokinetic Combat
6816 As you become a Psychic Warrior at 1st level you gain access to the Awakened Mind combat tradition. You gain an exertion pool and 2 maneuvers immediately, and gain additional maneuvers as you level up.
6817 You gain an additional maneuver at levels 5, 11, 16, and 19. At level 5 you may use 2nd degree maneuvers. At level 9 you may learn 3rd degree maneuvers. At level 13 you may use 4th degree maneuvers. And at level 19 you may learn 5th degree maneuvers.
6818 Mental Martial Traning
6819 At 3rd level, you gain proficiency in four martial weapons of your choice, and gain an additional 1hp for each Esper level you possess. In addition you may use your Int modifier in place of your Dex modifier for melee and ranged attack rolls and AC calculation.
6820 Extra Attack
6821 Starting at 6th level, you can attack twice instead of once when you take the Attack Action.
6822 Mind and Body
6823 At 15th level, you can exchange Exertion and Psi Dice. When you have no Exertion you may take an action to sacrifice one or more Psi Dice for more Exertion on a 1 to 1 ratio, or sacrifice one or more Exertion for more Psi Dice on a 2 to 1 ratio.  You may only perform this special action once per long rest.
6825 Thymokineticist
6826 Emotional Elementalist
6827 Energy Manipulation
6828 As a 1st level Thymokineticist you gain the Energy Manipulation power, which does not count against your number of powers known, and you do not need to meet its prerequisites.
6829 Enfettered Emotions
6830 At 3rd level you become capable of entering a state of raw emotional response. By spending a bonus action on your turn you can become Unfettered.
6831 While unfettered you gain the following benefits and penalty:
6832 - You have advantage on Charisma checks and Charisma saving throws.
6833 - Every round you remain unfettered you gain a number of temporary hit points equal to your proficiency bonus. These do not stack with themselves, or with other temporary hit points. These hit points dissipate when you're no longer unfettered.
6834 - You gain resistance to Psychic damage. If you have resistance to psychic damage you instead gain immunity.
6835 - You cannot add or remove dice from your reservoir.
6836 - If you are affected by a spell or ability that allows you to make a Wisdom saving throw to end the effect on your turn, you may expend your bonus action on your turn to end the effect without rolling a saving throw.
6837 - While Unfettered you have disadvantage on Wisdom saving throws and Insight checks.
6838 Unfettered lasts for up to 1 minute, though it ends if you fall unconscious or choose to end it as a bonus action on your turn. You can enter the Unfettered state once per long rest, but regain use of this ability after removing 2 psi dice from your reservoir or expending your psychic focus.
6839 Emotional Control
6840 When you gain 6th level your understanding of your own emotions and the emotional resonance of others improves. By spending 1 minute within 30ft of a creature you learn:
6841 - The creature's current emotional state and the general cause of that state, such as whether it relates to hunger, a relationship, or lost opportunity.
6842 - Whether the creature has a higher or lower Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma than you, choose one.
6843 - Whether the creature is more Deceptive, Insightful, Intimidating, or Persuasive. Which skill has the highest bonus, specifically.
6844 In addition, you gain a one time benefit against that creature in which you can impose disadvantage on a Wisdom saving throw by referencing the basis of their emotional state as a reaction. You do not need to be the one that initiates the Wisdom save. Once you have used this ability on a specific creature you can never do so, again.
6845 Unfettered Influence
6846 At 15th level your raw emotions are so powerful that they can flow out from you into others. Once per long rest when you activate your Unfettered state, you may choose up to 4 other creatures to gain the same benefits as you.
6847 This state, however, is more emotionally taxing, and lasts for a maximum of 5 rounds before you exit the Unfettered state. When you end the Unfettered state, whether by falling unconscious, running out of time, or spending a bonus action, the state ends for all affected creatures.
6849 Psionic Powers
6850 Body Manipulation
6851 Tier: 1
6852 Discipline: Kinesthetic
6853 Range: Self
6854 Target: Self
6856 A powerful kinesthetic esper can alter their body to survive adverse environments, extend their limbs, even restore their body. You manipulate your body when you activate this power by extending your limbs outward to increase your reach to ten feet, treat your space as large, or contort yourself to pass through cramped spaces for a creature of your size at full speed. Uses of this power require Concentration.
6857 Augments:
6858 Constrict: If you are grappling a target when you use this power, you may add +1d8 damage to the basic melee damage of your grapple per augment.
6859 Endure Elements: For one round you gain resistance to fire and cold, and ignore the effects of extreme heat and cold for an additional 10 minutes.
6860 Restoration: You may expend a single hit dice and recover hit points and/or power points as if you took a short rest.
6861 Agments, 2 dice:
6862 Lung Control: You may hold your breath for up to 10 minutes with no adverse effects.
6863 Clawing Hands: Your fingernails grow stronger, granting you a climb speed of 30ft. In addition, your unarmed strike and basic melee damage become slashing damage.
6864 Crumple: As a reaction you may use this power to negate any falling damage or damage caused by other impacts involving your body hitting a stationary object or being hit by a moving vehicle.
6868 Empathic Interference
6869 Tier 1
6870 Discipline: Empathic
6871 Range: 30ft
6872 Target: 1 Creature.
6874 You may read your target's current emotional state with perfect accuracy.  You may also attempt to interfere with the target's emotions to make them more positive, causing them to regard you or another creature in a more friendly manner, granting Advantage to the next charisma based skill check used against them. Targets may make a wisdom save, but are unaware of your influence unless they roll a total of 20 or higher.
6876 And finally you may influence your target to regard a creature in a negative manner, imposing Disadvantage on the next charisma skill check that person makes against your target. Should any charisma skill check result in a natural 1, the target becomes aware of the emotional manipulation. Targets may make a wisdom save, but are unaware of your influence unless they roll a total of 20 or higher.
6877 Augments:
6878 Terrorize: By heightening your target's emotional state you cause them to become frightened for up to 1 minute and take 1d6 psychic damage per augment on a failed saving throw. On success they take half damage and are not frightened. Frightened creatures make a save at the end of each of their turns to end the effect.
6879 Charming Banter: If the target fails its saving throw against this power the effects last for 1 minute, and the target refuses to do you or your allies harm.
6880 Augments, 2 dice:
6881 Grapple: A creature that fails a wisdom save against this power is also grappled so long as you maintain concentration, though it may attempt to end the grapple with wisdom saves on its turn.
6882 Manipulation: By expertly twisting your target's emotions you can make them perform a single task they would be unwilling to perform for a friend. This task can take up to 5 minutes, but the target is aware of the manipulation when the effect ends unless they rolled a natural 1 on their saving throw.
6883 Augments, 3 dice:
6884 Domination: By taking direct control over a creature's emotional state, you are able to bend them to whatever your heart desires. You may issue commands to your target on your turn and it will do its best to fulfil them. You may also directly take control over the creature, expending your action, bonus action, movement, and reaction to control each of its actions during the turn.
6886 Telekinesis
6887 Tier 1
6888 Discipline: Telekinetic
6889 Range: 30ft
6890 Target: 1 Creature or Object
6892 You use telekinesis to strike a creature or manipulate an object you can see within range.
6893 As an attack this deals 1d8 + your esper level points of Bludgeoning damage with a Strength Save for half damage. If you choose to move an object you can manipulate it as if holding it in one hand, and it may weigh up to your Intelligence score in pounds. Using the power in this way is a concentration effect.
6894 Augments:
6895 Weight Limit: +50lbs per augment spent
6896 Augments, 2 dice:
6897 Manipulate: A creature that fails its Strength save against this discipline is pushed up to 15ft in a direction of your choice.
6898 Augments, 3 dice:
6899 Flight: You, or another target within range, are lifted off the ground and carried a short distance. You gain a flight speed of 40ft for 1 minute. This manifestation requires concentration.
6900 Lift: A creature that fails its Strength save against this discipline is instead lifted into the air for one turn, up to 15ft. While suspended in this way the target cannot move from its current space.
6908 Telepathy
6909 Tier 1
6910 Discipline: Telepathic
6911 Range: 30ft
6912 Target: 1 Creature.
6914 You may use telepathic contact to assail or communicate with a creature within range. When you assault the target they must make a Wisdom save or take 1d10 + your esper level points of Psychic damage.
6916 When you attempt to communicate you're able to transfer meaning to and from your target, allowing for the sharing of words and emotion, regardless of any language barrier. Though creatures without language, such as a beast, will be incapable of providing words in return and may not understand words that you share.
6917 Augments:
6918 Learning: You may attempt to subtly take information from your target by listening in to their active thoughts.
6919 Augments, 2 dice:
6920 Overwhelm: Roll 10d4 and compare the value to the target's current hit points. If the value equals or exceeds the target's hit points they immediately fall into natural sleep.
6921 Invade: You may attempt to steal a memory from your target. Doing so causes your target to make a Wisdom Save. On a failure you are able to select one memory, generally an hour or less, and gain it for yourself. At your discretion, your target loses access to that memory for 24 hours.
6922 Augments, 3 dice:
6923 Traumatic Interference: You assail your target's mind in a manner that is so traumatic and painful that your target gains a Short Term Mental Stress Effect of your choosing for the next 1 minute.
6925 Astral Projection
6926 Tier: 2
6927 Disciplines: Telekinetic, Telepathic
6928 Range: –
6929 Target: Self
6931 By focusing your sense of self outward into the wider cosmos you are able to generate an astral construct of your mortal form.
6933 Your astral self does not breathe, and can exist in hard vacuum without issue. It can move 20ft each round as if it had a fly speed. While your astral self is active, you cannot see, hear, feel, or take any actions based on your living body.
6935 If your astral self is damaged by any attack, it immediately disperses and you are forced back to your body, taking any damage your astral self took from the attack.
6937 If your body is killed, or dies of hunger or thirst or asphyxiation while you are away, your astral self dies with it. Your astral self can manifest psionic powers that you know, but it cannot directly interact with objects or creatures in its environment.
6939 Manifesting this power takes 1 minute, and it lasts up to 24 hours. You may end this manifestation at any time. This power does not require Concentration.
6940 Augments:
6941 Resist Dispersion: When your astral self would be destroyed by damage, you attempt a death saving throw as a reaction. On a success, your astral self is not dispersed. On a failure, your astral self is not dispersed, but you gain 1 level of Exhaustion. You must roll your Psi Die each time you use this augment during the duration.
6943 Chirurgery
6944 Tier 2
6945 Discipline: Kinesthetic, Telepathic
6946 Range: 90ft
6947 Target: 1 Creature.
6949 By extending your mind out into another being you can affect repairs to their mind and body through your understanding of mortal systems, or disable such systems to horrifying results.
6950 When you manifest this power on a target within range, that target can immediately expend 1 hit die as if they were taking a short rest and immediately gain HP and/or Psionic Dice as normal.
6952 You may also choose to target an enemy creature within range and force them to expend 1 hit die, taking damage equal to the value rolled on a failed Intelligence Saving throw.
6954 If your target has no hit dice available, in either case, this power has no effect. When targeting animals and other monsters, treat the number of dice that determine their hit points as hit dice.
6955 Augments:
6956 Smell/Blindness/Deafness/Numbness: Your target loses access to one of their senses, temporarily. This lasts for up to 1 minute per power point you expend, though your target may spend an action to roll a saving throw on any round, ending the effect on a successful save.
6957 Deep Healing: The target expends an additional Hit Dice per augment.
6958 Repaired Systems: Remove one disease or condition from your target for every HD they spend as part of this power's manifestation. Choose from smell, blinded, deafened, paralyzed, or poisoned. At the Narrator's discretion, some diseases may not be cured with this power.
6959 Augments, 2 dice:
6960 Rest and Rejuvenate: Your target may remove one of the following effects for every hit die they spend as part of this power's manifestation: One level of Fatigue or Exhaustion, any reduction to a single ability score, or any effect that has reduced their hit point maximum.
6961 Augments, 3 dice:
6962 Restore Activity: You may attempt to immediately expend all of your currently dead target's remaining hit dice in an attempt to bring them back to life. Your target can be dead for no more than 1 minute when you use your power in this way, and regains hit points as if they expended a single hit dice.  If your target has half or less of their normal maximum hit dice when you manifest this power, they must make a constitution save against your Psionics DC. On a failure, they instead regain only 1 hit point and remain unconscious until they've completed a long rest.
6967 Energy Manipulation
6968 Tier 2
6969 Disciplines: Empathic, Telekinetic
6970 Range: 90ft
6971 Target: 1 Creature, Object, or Energy Source.
6973 By tapping into your own emotional understanding, and reaching out with your telekinetic skill, you are able to generate or manipulate certain elemental or energetic effects that are sympathetic with your emotions.
6974 You may use your power to create one of the following effects:
6975 Empower: Increase the damage of an energy source by 1d8 + Esper Level for up to 1 minute (concentration).
6976 Ignite: Ignite an unattended flammable object no larger than a 5ft cube.
6977 Freeze: Create up to 25 cubic feet of ice per round.
6978 Reduce: Reduce the damage of an energy source by 1d8 + Esper Level for up to 1 minute (concentration).
6979 Augments:
6980 Expand: Double the affected area per augment. Each further augment creates a multiplier.
6981 Augments, 2 dice:
6982 Overcharge: Overcharge a weapon that deals cold, electric, or fire damage, increasing its damage by your Esper level for up to 1 minute (concentration).
6983 Recharge: Restore charge to a target object with energy. Small objects are fully recharged; larger objects gain ½ charge.
6984 Augments, 3 dice:
6985 Destroy Source: Snuff out an energy source completely. Batteries or power cells are ruined.
6986 Explosive Release: Letting out an enormous emotional blast you create a powerful energy burst at a targeted creature or object within range.
6987 Choose whether this burst is a line 90ft long, a cone 30ft long, or a sphere 20ft in diameter.
6988 All creatures within the area must make a Dexterity saving throw or take 3d8 + Esper level damage.  You may choose bludgeoning, cold, fire, lightning, piercing, or slashing damage as the damage type.
6992 Psychoportation
6993 Tier 2
6994 Disciplines: Kinesthetic, Telekinetic
6995 Range: 90ft
6996 Target: Self or 1 Creature.
6998 By using your knowledge of the self and your innate ability to manipulate objects across space, you have developed a way to transfer matter directly from one point to another without crossing the intervening space.
7000 As an action you can teleport yourself or one willing creature within range to another point within range.  If you target an unwilling creature with this power they must make an immediate Constitution saving throw.  On a success they remain where they are, and are unharmed.  On a failure they take 1d8 + your Esper level points of force damage as you teleport tiny fragments of the target's body without bringing the entire thing.
7001 Augments:
7002 Targeting: You may attempt to teleport an object within range. The object must be medium sized or smaller. Using this augment twice increases the size to large.
7003 Augments, 2 dice:
7004 Juxtapose: You may target two creatures and if they are willing, swap their positions. If one or both are unwilling, the Constitution save remains, but on a failure the target takes the damage and is moved.
7005 Slide: You are able to delay a teleporting creature's return, causing them to cease to exist for up to 5 rounds. If you use this ability on an unwilling target, the Constitution save instead becomes a Wisdom save with damage on a successful save and sliding on a failed save.
7006 Augments, 3 dice:
7007 Gateway: Rather than simply moving a creature from one location to another, you are able to open a doorway, briefly, that any number of creatures may pass through. This gateway lasts so long as you concentrate on it, but each round after the first you gain 1 level of Exhaustion holding it open.
7008 Memory Path: You may teleport to a location ignoring range completely. Consult the following table:
7011 Familiarity
7012 Off Target
7013 On Target
7014 Very familiar
7015 1-23
7016 24-100
7017 Seen casually
7018 1-43
7019 44-100
7020 Viewed once
7021 1-63
7022 64-100
7023 Description
7024 1-83
7025 84-100
7026 Concept
7027 1-93
7028 94-100
7030 If you end “Off Target” you and your targets arrive some distance away in a random direction. The further you travel, the further away you are likely to arrive. You arrive off target by a number of miles equal to 1d100 percent of the total distance of your trip. If you tried to travel 1,000 miles and roll a 24 on the d100, you land 24 percent off target and arrive 240 miles away from your intended destination in a random direction. Roll 1d8 to randomly determine the direction: 1—north, 2—northeast, 3—east, 4—southeast, 5—south, 6—southwest, 7—west, 8—northwest.
7032 Warp Perception
7033 Tier 2
7034 Disciplines: Empathy, Telepathic
7035 Range: 90ft
7036 Target: A number of Creatures equal to your Psionic Ability Modifier.
7037 Duration: 1 minute (Concentration)
7039 Perception is reality, and you have learned to alter the perceptions of entire groups of creatures at the same time, shifting their understanding of reality in one of the following ways.
7040 When you manifest this power, each target may make a Wisdom saving throw against the effect.
7041 Addition: Your targets perceive twice as much of something. If they are looking on a group of creatures, that group appears to have twice as many people in it, with any incongruities in space accounted for by distortion of the shared space.
7042 Subtraction: You remove a single entity or object from your target's perception. Any investigation or perception check to locate the object, no matter how large or obvious, is made at disadvantage, and the subtracted creature or object may make stealth checks without cover or concealment.
7043 Creation: Your targets perceive an entity or object taking up to a 10ft cube of space. This entity or object cannot move from that location, though it can move within that space if it is smaller than 10ft on a side. If you choose, the creation may also make noises such as words or music, regardless of how inappropriate that would be for your creation.
7045 Any target which interacts with the addition or creation function of this power cannot use expertise on any skill check related to the illusion.
7046 This effect lasts for up to 1 minute so long as you maintain concentration.
7047 Augments:
7048 Duration: +1 minute per Augment.
7049 Swap: Your targets perceive two entities or objects as being in opposite locations. For example you may cause them to perceive you as being one of their allies, and that ally as you. Directly interacting with either swapped entity or object triggers an immediate Wisdom saving throw to end the effect.
7050 Augments, 2 dice:
7051 Additional Effect: You may simultaneously use two separate effects, such as Subtraction and Creation, rather than only one.
7052 Deletion: When you Subtract a creature or object from perception your targets become incapable of perceiving that creature or object whatsoever. Any perception or investigation check to locate the creature or object automatically fails and the creature or object cannot be attacked or interacted with by the targets. If a creature deleted in this way attacks a target that cannot perceive it, this power immediately ends for that target, alone.
7053 Expansion: Increase the range of this power by a factor of 10 for every 2 Augments, as well as the space a Creation can occupy.
7055 Planeswalking
7056 Tier: 3
7057 Disciplines: Empathic, Telekinetic, Telepathic
7058 Range: –
7059 Target: Self
7061 When you use this power as an action on your turn, you can immediately project your spirit to the Astral Plane. Your body remains behind but will be teleported to your location if you leave the Astral Plane and enter another plane of existence.
7062 As a bonus action on your turn, you can end this effect and return to your body.
7064 Augments:
7065 Shifting Steps: You take one creature with you. Each time you use this augment, you can choose one additional creature.
7066 Augments, 5 dice:
7067 Rend the Veil: You tear open a hole into the Astral Plane from whichever plane you are on. The rift remains open for 1 minute, allowing any creature of Large size or smaller to move between the planes. If you are on the Astral Plane, you can instead open a portal to the Material Plane.
7069 Synchronic combat
7070 Tier 3
7071 Disciplines: Empathic, Kinesthetic, Telepathic
7072 Range: 30ft
7073 Target: One Ally within Range.
7075 You and one ally of your choice become mentally linked for up to 1 minute, so long as you can maintain concentration.  While linked, and able to see threats to your ally, you may share saving throws.  When either of you is subjected to a saving throw, you both roll and use the higher result.  This does not stack with advantage, but works with disadvantage.  You may also grant your ally aid on one attack as if you had taken the Help action as a reaction.  Once you have shared your saving throw or aided on an attack, this power ends. Cannot be used with the Split augment.
7076 Augments:
7077 Castle: Instantly change places with your ally within 60ft. Ends the power.
7078 Share Movement: Add together your movement and your ally’s movement and divide it in 5ft increments amongst yourselves. Roll Psi Die each turn you use this.
7079 Augments, 2:
7080 Share Attacks: Forgo your action to grant your ally an additional action. Ends the power.
7081 Unity: Share hit points and temporary hit points. When one of you takes damage or healing, you may split the value in half using your reaction.
7085 Warging
7086 Tier 3
7087 Disciplines: Empathic, Telekinetic, Telepathic
7088 Range: 120ft
7089 Target: One Living Creature
7091 You perceive the world as the creature you are in contact with, essentially becoming the same creature.  On a failed Wisdom save, you can no longer perceive the world through your own senses, but instead through the target’s.
7092 The target is aware of a ‘presence’ in their mind.
7093 Lasts up to 10 minutes (concentration). Range becomes unlimited after initial connection.
7094 Augments:
7095 Control Movement: Control the target’s movement until the start of your next turn.
7096 Imperceptible: Your presence is not perceived if used every round. Ends if any other augment is used.
7097 Slow Down: Alter your target’s initiative by up to 6 turns.
7098 Augments, 2 dice:
7099 Duration: Increase duration by 1 hour per use.
7100 Take Action: Control the creature’s action on its turn.

7287 T = Talents
7288 D = Disciplines (1/2 rounded up from path)
7289 PP = Psi Points
7290 PL = Psi Limit
7292 Hit Dice:1d8 + your Constitution modifier
7293 Armour: Light Armour
7294 Weapons: Simple Weapons, Scimitar*
7295 Saving Throws: Intelligence, Wisdom
7296 Skills: Choose two from Arcana, History, Insight, Medicine, Nature, Perception, Religion
7297 Equipment
7298 a quarterstaff or (b) a scimitar
7299 a sling and 20 bullets or (b) any simple weapon
7300 scholar’s pack or (b) an explorer’s pack, leather armour
7301 Psionic Ability
7302 Intelligence is the key ability for psionic disciplines. You use your Intelligence modifier when setting the saving throw DC for a psionic talent or discipline, or when making an attack roll with one.
7303 Discipline Save DC= 8 + your proficiency bonus + your Intelligence modifier
7304 Discipline Attack modifier= your proficiency bonus + your Intelligence modifier
7305 Mystic Paths
7306 A mystic strives to become a master of the powers of the mind, but the path to such mastery varies. At first level, you commit yourself to a particular path, whether through desire, as a consequence of the instruction you have received, or because this is an expression of natural talent.
7307 There are three such paths, the Path of the Savant, the Path of the Nomad and the Path of the Shaper. Savants consider themselves the purest form of Mystic, and focus on influencing the minds of others; Nomads are wanderers, using their minds to travel and explore; Shapers focus their powers on manipulation of the four elements and the material world. Each path gives you features when you choose it at 1st level and additional features at 3rd, 6th, and 14th level.
7308 In addition, each path provides a pool of 5 talents and 6 disciplines from which you can select, with at least half of your disciplines being drawn from your chosen path. More information on talents and disciplines is provided under Psionics on page 4.

7328 Telepathy
7329 You can engage at will in mental communication with any creature within 120 feet who knows at least one language (you do not have to know this language), or is telepathic themselves. When doing so, you can – as an action – act as if you had cast Detect Thoughts.
7330 Psychic Focus
7331 From 2nd level, you can focus your mind to draw ongoing benefits from your pool of psychic talent. In addition to a primary benefit, you gain a secondary benefit if the active focus type matches your mystic path (see table). Initiating or switching focus requires a bonus action. You can only have one type of focus active at any one time, but this remains active until you are incapacitated or select a different focus type.
7332 Focus Type
7333 Primary benefit
7334 Secondary benefit
7335 Savant
7336 Advantage on Initiative
7337 Resistance to Psychic Damage
7338 Nomad
7339 Darkvision, +60 feet
7340 Normal vision in natural & magical darkness to 30 ft
7341 Shaper
7342 Advantage on cold & fire
7343 Resistance to Force Damage
7345 Strength of Mind
7346 Even the simplest psionic technique requires a deep understanding of how psionic energy can augment mind and body. This allows you to alter your defences to better deal with threats. From 4th level, if unarmoured and without a shield, you add your Intelligence modifier to your Armour Class.
7347 Potent Psionics
7348 From 8th level, you infuse your manifestations with greater potency. For talents and disciplines from your chosen Mystic Path, you increase the save DC and attack modifer by 1. In addition, you add your Intelligence modifier to any damage you deliver via a psionic talent.
7349 Consumptive Power
7350 At 10th Level, you gain the ability to sacrifice physical durability for psionic power. Once per short rest, you can manifest a power by expending hit points instead of psi points, at a rate of 2 hit points per psi-point required. This hit-point reduction cannot be healed, but restores at the end of a long rest.
7351 Harness Subconscious
7352 From 12th level, you can manifest a power without expending psi points, once per long rest. At 14th, 16th and 18th level, this can be achieved two, three and four times per long rest, respectively.
7353 Master of Telepathy
7354 You become a master of mental communication at 16th level, and can use Telepathy to (a) communicate with up to 6 creatures at the same time, and over any distance; (b) create a permanent mental link with a single creature; and/or (c) initiate, or become a node for, a Convocation (below).
7355 Dreamscape Traveller
7356 Travelling the land of dreams is a powerful but sometimes unreliable means of getting from one place to another. Once asleep, the mystic of 18th level or greater fashions a dream that involves travelling from his present location to his intended destination, awaking wherever his nocturnal wandering carried him.
7357 Distance is not a factor, but the more strongly you can visualise your destination, the better the chances of a successful voyage. You can even take others with you, although this is more demanding than travelling alone (5 psi points per companion).
7358 A dream journey takes around eight hours. When you (and any companions) reach your destination, you wake. In the place where you lay sleeping, your body and any equipment carried or worn fades away. At the same time, you fade into being at your new location, still in sleeping posture, but fully awake.
7359 Note that a DM may choose to play out the journey as an adventure, in which case note that this power in itself does not guarantee that you will reach the intended destination. A dream traveller killed during the journey wakes the the journey’s origin, unharmed but possibly shaken by the nightmare they experienced.
7360 Psionic Body
7361 At 20th level, the mystic’s mastery of psionic power causes the mind to transcend the body. You no longer age, but can still accrue experience(s). Your physical form is infused with psionic energy resulting in multiple benefits:
7362 Immunity to exhaustion, and to disease & poison (damage & condition).
7363 Resistance to bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing damage.
7364 Discorporation on death. Instead of dying, roll d20: on a 10 or higher, you discorporate, your body and worn/carried equipment disappearing. You reappear at a spot of your choosing 1d3 days later on the plane of existence where you died, having gained the benefits of a long rest.
7365 Psionics
7366 Psionics are powers of the mind that can influence others, the material world, or the user of those powers themselves. Psionics are not magic, but rather the manifestation of internal strength and a understanding of the nature of the universe. As such, magic and psionics do not intersect, and using psionic powers is not spell-casting.
7367 Psionic manifestation requires no verbal, somatic or material components, but such manifestation can be detected by a flash in the eyes of the mystic and, amongst less experienced mystics, by gestures that serve to help focus when manifesting powers or talents.
7368 Psionic Talents
7369 These are minor psionic effects and actions that are akin to a wizard’s cantrips. The first of these that you master must be from your Mystic Path, but otherwise you can select talents as you desire, subject to the maximum number listed in the Mystic table.
7370 Psionic Disciplines
7371 A psionic discipline is a rigid set of mental exercises that allows a mystic to manifest particular set of powers (three per discipline). The Disciplines Known column of the Mystic table shows the total number known at each level; when that number goes up for you, choose a new discipline. The minimum number that you must select from your Mystic Path is indicated in parentheses. Once learnt, disciplines cannot be unlearnt by normal means.
7372 Psi Points
7373 You have an internal reservoir of energy that can be devoted to psionic powers that you know. This energy is represented by psi points, the number of which is dependent on your level. The Mystic table displays the maximum number for each level. Each power within a discipline describes effects you can create by expending a certain number of psi points. Note that you cannot drop below 0, nor exceed the maximum. Psionic talents require no psi points.
7374 Psi Limits
7375 Although you have access to a potent amount of psionic energy, it takes training and practice to channel that energy. Learning a discipline is not enough: it needs to be mastered. The degree of mastery is represented by the Psi Limit, as shown in the Mystic table.
7376 This limit is the maximum number of psi-points that you can spend to manifest a power within a discipline. For example, as a 3rd-level mystic, you can spend no more than 3 psi points on a power each time you use it, no matter how many psi points you have available. Powers that require more than your limit, in a discipline you know, are those your mind seeks still to master.
7377 Exceptions to this are detailed in individual powers.
7378 Resting
7379 Psionic activity is draining, represented by the loss of psi points. These are restored (to your maximum) when you finish a long rest. During a short rest, however, you can choose to expend Hit Dice to recover lost psi-points in place of hit points, trading physical for mental vitality.
7380 Where an ability or power is useable a limited number of times per short or long rest, you must complete at least the stated rest in order to regain the ability to use that power (one hour of a long rest counts as a short rest for this purpose).
7381 Psionic Convocation
7382 A convocation is a literal sharing of minds, with its establisher as the central node. To call a convocation, you must perform a prolonged meditative ritual that depends on the minds to be joined. This can only be attempted once per long rest.
7383 Telepathic bond. If non-telepathic individuals are included, the convocation takes the form of a Telepathic Bond. Up to eight willing minds can be joined, who must be present at, and participants in, the ritual. Subsequently, these minds are able to communicate telepathically for as long as the mystic maintains concentration, or until 1 hour has elapsed. Performing this form of convocation costs 30 psi-points.
7384 Convergence. A convocation that emerges from the joining of psionic minds, that need only be in telepathic contact when the ritual is performed. At its conclusion, those so joined – in addition to continued telepathic communication with one another – share a common pool of psi points and access to one another’s talents and powers. An individual’s psi-limit still applies to their own manifestations, but otherwise any member of the convocation can manifest any power known to another, and all powers manifested draw on the common pool.
7385 This connection comes with risks, however. Any psychic damage received by any member ripples through the convergence – all connected individuals receive half of the original damage, without a save, and ignoring any resistance to psychic damage. Similarly, if a member of a convergence is killed, all surviving members of the convergence take 1 point of psychic damage per mystic level of the deceased.
7386 Mystics with Master of Telepathy form nodes in the convergence, which lasts as long as one of these nodes maintains concentration on the convergence, or until 24 hours have elapsed. Members of the convergence may withdraw (leave) at any time, and the convergence collapses when the last nodal individual withdraws. An individual must withdraw in order to take a long rest.
7387 When the convergence is discontinued, psi points that remain in the pool are evenly divided amongst all the participants. Fractions are rounded down, and no individual can exceed their usual maximum (extra points are lost). Performing this form of the convocation ritual costs the establisher 60 psi points.
