7413 Path of the Savant
7414 Empathy
7415 A savant is more attuned to telepathy than other mystics, and this ability passively extends Telepathy to non-verbal, non-linguistic creatures. Thus, you are able to communicate telepathically with any creature that has a mind, although such communication may (for Intelligence less than 4 – some of these creatures have minds) be more of emotion and desire, than linguistic.
7416 In addition, while concentrating, you gain proficiency (or expertise if already proficient) in the Insight skill.
7417 Object Reading
7418 Powerful emotions leave a psychic residue. Objects and locations retain a ‘shadow memory’ of the events that generated these emotions, and by concentrating for a period of ten minutes, a savant can recover this memory and reconstruct the events, although this may be hazy or partial, and particular details are at the DM’s discretion.
7419 Psionic Surge
7420 Once per short rest, a savant of 6th Level or above can – as a bonus action, and at a cost of 5 psi points – expend additional psychic energy to force a target to suffer disadvantage on a saving throw against a particular discipline or talent manifested by the savant in that turn.
7421 Ectoplasmic Form
7422 Ectoplasm is the material from which dreams are formed, and is susceptible to manipulation by psychic power. At 14th level, the savant gains the ability to become a ghostly figure of ectoplasm. This is a demanding ability, and can only be attempted once per long rest. The ectoplasmic form lasts for 1 hour, or until you use an action to revert to your material form.
7423 While in this form, you have resistance to all except psychic damage. You move at half speed but can pass through objects and creatures while moving. You cannot make physical attacks (any attempt fails), or cast any spell – if you have that ability – with either a somatic or material component. You also cannot willingly end movement in a space occupied by another object if that would require you to intersect with that object.
7424 Path of the Nomad
7425 Dimension Fade
7426 A nomad is more attuned to the dimensions of space and time, as well as higher dimensions, than other mystics. With this ability you partially shift your body into a higher dimension and, while concentrating, gain proficiency (or expertise if already proficient) in the Stealth skill. Additionally, whenever you hide, observers suffer disadvantage on their attempts to spot you.
7427 Memory of Movement
7428 At 3rd level, a nomad gains the ability to use psionics to recall her steps. When you are hit by an attack you can, as reaction, teleport to an unoccupied space that you occupied since the start of your last turn, causing the attack to miss. This ability can only be used once per short rest.
7429 Superior teleport
7430 At 6th level, a nomad gains a superior talent for teleportation. When manifesting a power from the Nomadic Step discipline, you can increase your distance travelled by up to 50% at your discretion.
7431 Effortless Journey
7432 Starting at 14th level, a nomad is capable of moving by means of creating local dimensional distortions. Once per turn, you can trade up to 30 feet of movement to travel that distance by teleportation. The destination location must be both visible and unoccupied.
7434 Path of the Shaper
7435 Fabricate
7436 While concentrating, a shaper gains proficiency (or expertise if already proficient) in crafting tools of choice, and/or can use ectoplasm to mend, repair, and or patch damage to normal objects that amounts to no more than 2 sq. feet (of damage) per mystic level. Such repairs dissipate if and when concentration on this ability ends.
7437 Ectoplasmic Servant
7438 A shaper of 3rd Level or greater can – as an action – form ectoplasm into the shape of small creature, up to 8” tall. The shaper can determine the nature of this shape, but it must be elemental or material (earth, air, fire, water, wood, metal, stone, clay etc.). However, the creature’s form is solid, and it does not gain any specific properties associated with the chosen element or material.
7439 At any time, you may have only one servant. The construct can act autonomously, but remains under your telepathic command. If the servant is killed, it returns to ghostlike ectoplasm before dispersing. Creating a new servant requires a period of focus and can be accomplished only once per short rest.
7440 Ectoplasmic Servant; Tiny construct; Armour Class; 14 (natural armour); Hit Points; 1 + your Intelligence modifier + your Mystic level; Speed; 20 ft., fly/swim 40 ft (if shaped appropriately, e.g. wings); STR(4 (-3)); DEX(16 (+3)); CON(12 (+1)); INT(10 (+0)); WIS(9 (-1)); CHA(6 (-2)); Damage Immunities; poison; Condition Immunities; exhaustion, poisoned, diseased, charmed, frightened; Senses; darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 10+PB; Languages; Those that you,as its master, understand; Proficiency Bonus; equal to your PB; Immutable Form. The ectoplasmic servant is immune to any spell or effect that would alter its form.; Stealth. The ectoplasmic servant has advantage on stealth checks when hiding or sneaking; Actions; Natural Weapon (according to form). Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5ft., one target. Hit: Your Intelligence modifier in psychic damage
7442 Astral Warrior
7443 From 6th Level, a shaper can – as an action – form ectoplasm into either a humanoid form (5-6’ tall), or an equivalently-sized beast, the nature of which is as per the description for Ectoplasmic Servant.
7444 This warrior responds to the telepathic commands of its creator, but is otherwise predisposed to fight for &/or guard its creator, which it will do so in the absence of other instruction. Thus, it is an ideal protector for a resting mystic. If the warrior is killed, it returns to ghostlike ectoplasm before dispersing. Crafting a new warrior can be accomplished only once per short rest. At any time, the shaper may have only a single Astral Warrior, in addition to an Ectoplasmic Servant.
7445 Astral Warrior; Medium construct; Armour Class; 16 (natural armour); Hit Points; 10 + d10/mystic level-1, + your Intelligence modifier; Speed; 30 ft., fly/swim 30 ft (if shaped appropriately, e.g. wings); STR(16 (+3)); DEX(14 (+2)); CON(14 (+2)); INT(10 (+0)); WIS(9 (-1)); CHA(8 (-1)); Damage Immunities; poison, bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing from nonmagical weapons that aren’t adamantine; Condition Immunities; exhaustion, poisoned, diseased, charmed, frightened; Senses; darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 10+PB; Languages; Those that you, as its master, understand; Proficiency Bonus; equal to your PB; Immutable Form. The astral warrior is immune to any spell or effect that would alter its form.; Magic Resistance. The astral warrior has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects. This is a consequence of the nature of its existence, not a magical effect.; Actions; Natural Weapon (according to form). Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5ft., one target. Hit: 8(d10 + 3) physical damage (slashing, bludgeoning or piercing according to form, as chosen by the mystic at creation), + mystic’s Intelligence modifier in psychic damage.; Extra Attack. At Mystic Level 12, the Astral Warrior gains an extra attack per turn.
7446 Crystal Form
7447 Powerful shapers, from 14th Level, can – as an action – shield their bodies with a crystalline construct that covers their skin and clothing. This is a demanding ability, and can only be attempted once per long rest.
7448 The crystal shell grants +3 AC, resistance to slashing, piercing and bludgeoning, and immunity to critical hits , but incurs disadvantage to Stealth checks as its facets tend to reflect light. The form lasts for 1 hour, or until the shaper uses an action to end it.
7449 Psionic Talents
7450 Animate Shadow
7451 As an action, you can animate a shadow cast by a creature or object within 120 feet. You make the shadow seem to have life of its own, and it can even move away from the creature or object that cast it. The animated shadow can never be more than two-dimensional and must remain flatly cast along a surface.
7452 This talent could be used, for example, to entertain a crowd, or divert a guard’s attention.
7453 Beacon
7454 As a bonus action, you cause bright light to radiate from your body in a 20 ft radius and dim light for an additional 20 feet. The light can be coloured as you like. The light lasts for 1 hour, and you can extinguish it earlier as a bonus action.
7455 Blind Spot
7456 As an action, you erase your image from the mind of one creature you can see within 120 feet of you; the target must succeed on a Wisdom saving throw, or you are invisible to it until the end of your next turn.
7457 Daydream
7458 As an action, you can cause a creature’s mind to wander. The target must be within 60 feet and visible to you and have at least 4 points in intelligence. If this creature fails an Intelligence save, attack roles against it are made at advantage, and it suffers disadvantage on Perception and Investigation skill checks, and on Dexterity saves, until the end of your next turn.
7459 Delusion
7460 As an action, you plant a false belief in the mind of one creature that you can see within 60 feet. Only the target of this talent perceives the sound or image you create.
7461 A sound can range from a whisper to a scream; it can be your voice, someone else’s voice, a creature’s roar, a musical instrument, or any other sound you pick. It lasts for 1 minute. An object must fit within a 5 ft cube and cannot move or be reflective. The image cannot create any effect that influences a sense other than sight. The image lasts for 1 minute, and disappears if the creature attempt to touch it.
7462 Ego Whip
7463 As an action, you target one creature you can see within 120 feet. If the target fails an Intelligence save, it is left feeling inferior and worthless. Until the end of your next turn, it suffers a 1d4 reduction on Intelligence, Wisdom and Charisma saving throws, and can take only the Dodge, Disengage, or Hide actions. Speed is halved and the target will not approach any risk or challenge, real or imagined. Highly egotistical creatures, such as dragons, beholders and mind flayers, suffer the maximum penalty if they fail their save.
7464 Eye of the Falcon
7465 As an action, you enhance your vision, magnifying distant objects, until the end of your next turn. This effectively triples the distance at which you can discern detail in objects.
7466 This increases the range of any targeted talent or power that you know (read range in yards instead of feet) for its duration, and negates the disadvantage associated with using a ranged weapon at long range. Unfortunately, it also makes it more difficult to see objects nearby, and so you suffer disadvantage on mêlée attacks for the duration.
7467 Feather Step
7468 As a bonus action, you alter your density and weight to improve your mobility. For the rest of your turn, your walking speed increases by 10 feet, and the first time you stand up this turn, you do so without expending any of your movement if your speed is greater than 0.
7469 Mind Thrust
7470 As an action, you target one creature you can see within 120 feet with a stab of psychic energy. If the target fails an Intelligence save, they take 1d10 psychic damage.
7471 The talent’s damage increases when you reach 5th level (2d10), 11th level (3d10), and 17th level (4d10). This damage can be declared non-lethal at ranges up to 60 feet.
7472 Mystic Charm
7473 As an action, you beguile one humanoid you can see within 120 feet of you. The target must succeed on a Charisma saving throw or be charmed by you until the end of your next turn.
7474 Minor Telekinesis
7475 You can use your action to manipulate or move one object within 30 feet of you. The object can’t weigh more than 10 pounds, and you can’t affect an object being worn or carried by another creature.
7476 This talent allows you, for example, to open an unlocked door, or pour out a beer stein. If the object is loose, you can move it up to 30 feet in any direction. The object falls to the ground at the end of your turn if you leave it suspended in mid-air.
7477 Moment of Prescience
7478 As a bonus action, you can gain a flash of insight into the immediate future. This grants +2 AC for the remainder of that round.
7479 Planar Energy
7480 As an action, you expose one or more creatures (or objects) to a momentary bust of another plane’s energy (acid, cold, fire, lightning, thunder, or necrotic (desiccation) at your discretion).
7481 Target one location you can see within 90 feet. All creatures who intersect the 1 ft x 3 ft rift (typically, one medium-sized target) must save vs Dexterity or take 1d8 damage, increasing to 2d8 at 5th level, 3d8 at 11th, and 4d8 at 17th level. Objects that intersect the rift are damaged equivalently, according to their nature.
7482 Psychic Grasp
7483 As an action, you try to grasp one creature you can see within 120 feet, with a hand crafted from telekinetic energy. The target must succeed on a Strength save or take 1d6 force damage. If it takes any of this damage and is large or smaller, you can move it up to 10 feet in a straight line in a direction of your choice. You can’t lift the target off the ground unless it is already airborne or underwater.
7484 The talent’s damage increases to 2d6 at 5th level, 3d6 at 11th, and 4d6 at 17th level.
7485 Psychic Hammer
7486 As an action, you target one visible creature or object within 60 feet with telekinetic force. You make a melee (discipline) attack to determine if this hits. If successful, the target takes 1d6 force damage and – if large or smaller – must make a Strength save or be knocked prone.
7487 The talent’s damage increases to 2d6 at 5th level, 3d6 at 11th, and 4d6 at 17th level. This damage can be non-lethal at ranges up to 30 feet.
7489 Disciplines - Path of the Savant
7490 Auric Sight
7491 Assess Foe (2 psi)
7492 As a bonus action, you analyse the aura of one creature you see. You learn its current hit point and psi point totals, immunities, resistances, and vulnerabilities.
7493 View Aura (3 psi, Ctn)
7494 As an action, you study one creature’s aura. Until your concentration ends (or 1 hour elapses), and while you can see the target, you learn if it is under the effect of any magical or psionic effects, its current hit point and psi point totals, and its basic emotional state. While this effect lasts, you have advantage on Wisdom (Insight) and Charisma checks you make against it.
7495 Perceive the Unseen (5 psi)
7496 As a bonus action, you gain the ability to see the auras even of invisible or hidden creatures. Thus, you can see the location and nature of all creatures, including those hidden or invisible (regardless of lighting conditions) within 240’ for a duration of 10 minutes.
7497 Inquisition
7498 Forceful Query (2 psi)
7499 As an action, you ask a question of one creature that can see and hear you within 30 feet. The question must be phrased so that it can be answered as ‘yes’ or ‘no’, otherwise this ability fails. If this creature fails a Wisdom saving throw, it replies truthfully. Immunity to being charmed protects a creature from this power.
7500 Probe (5 psi, Ctn)
7501 While you concentrate on this ability, you extract clear memories from one creature’s mind. The creature must remain within 30 feet of you, and you must be able to see it. If you reach the ability’s full duration (1 hour), the target must make three Intelligence saving throws. You learn information from the creature based on the number of saving throws it fails. Three fails reveals key memories from the past 48 hours; two fails, from the past 24 hours, and one fail, from the past 12 hours.
7502 Hallucinations (3-9 psi, Ctn)
7503 As an action, choose one creature you are aware of within 60 feet. If this creature fails an Intelligence save, it perceives a terrifying monster adjacent to it until your concentration ends (or 1 minute elapses). During this time, the target takes 2d4 psychic damage at the start of each of its turns and is effectively Stunned, lost in its horrific vision. You can increase the per-turn damage by 1d4 for every psi point above 3 spent on the ability. Further, and as an action each turn, you can elect to be present within the hallucination and make offers to end the terror (gaining advantage on a Persuasion check).
7504 The target repeats its saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. A creature losing more than 75% of its hit points as a result of this power must make a Wisdom saving throw or suffer from residual short-term madness for 1d10 minutes after the power ends.
7505 Powers in this this discipline have no effect on skeletons, zombies or constructs; or on oozes or plants below 3 intelligence.
7506 Inthraller
7507 Attraction (2-9 psi)
7508 As an action, you exert an enthralling aura to a range of 60 feet. The nearest creature to you, plus an additional creature for every extra psi point expended, must make an Intelligence saving throw or regard you as a friend and ally for 1 minute, granting you advantage on any Persuasion or Deception checks. This Attraction effect ends immediately if you or your allies attempt a hostile act towards any of the creatures so affected.
7509 Charm (3-9 psi, Ctn)
7510 As an action, you exert an aura that inspires awe. One creature, plus one for every two psi points expended above 3 (i.e. 1 – 4 creatures), within 60’ must succeed with an Intelligence saving throw or be charmed by you until your concentration ends (or 1 hour has elapsed). While charmed, the target obeys all your verbal and/or telepathic commands to the best of its ability, without doing anything obviously self-destructive.
7511 The charmed target will only attack creatures that it sees attack you after it was charmed, or that it was hostile towards previously. If a charmed creature is threatened or attacked by you or your allies, it repeats its saving throw; injuring the creature automatically removes the Charm effect.
7512 This power has no effect on any creature specifically immune to being charmed.
7513 Dominate (7 psi, Ctn)
7514 As an action, you target one creature you can see within 60 feet. If this target fails an Intelligence saving throw, you control its actions and movement on its turns until your concentration ends (or 10 minutes have elapsed). The Dominate effect can also be ended by conditions as specified under Charm. A creature that is immune to being charmed is similarly immune to this power.
7515 Powers in this discipline have no effect on undead or constructs; or on oozes or plants below 3 intelligence.
7516 Metapsionics
7517 Psionic Vampirism (1-9 psi Ctn)
7518 Using an action, you target one creature within 120 feet. If that creature is awake and fails an Intelligence save, you drain twice the number of psi points you spend on this power and add those to your current total (this cannot exceed your maximum). If the target has no psi points, you drain 1 point of Intelligence for a net gain of 5 psi points. This does not require concentration.
7519 If the target is sleeping or unconscious, the drain can continue for as long as you maintain concentration (you can’t move faster than walking, or outside the range of the power, to do this). The target receives no save in the first round, but does make an Intelligence save on each of its turns subsequently. A successful save ends the drain and blocks further attempts. The drain also ends when the target reaches 0 in psi-points or Intelligence.
7520 Excess psi-points beyond your total must be spent immediately (i.e. on your next turn) on a power that does not require concentration or otherwise be lost to the ether. Such ‘spillage’ is potentially detectable by certain creatures, and could draw unwanted attention.
7521 A creature drained to 0 psi points cannot manifest any psionic powers (a long rest restores drained points), while a creature drained to 0 Intelligence is rendered, or remains, unconscious. An extended rest, or Greater Restoration, is required to regain lost ability points.
7522 Negate Psionics (5 psi)
7523 As an action, you can use this power to end ongoing powers manifested on a creature or object, or to end ongoing power (or at least its effects) within an area. A power so negated ends as if its duration had expired. You automatically negate any power that you manifest yourself but you cannot negate the consequences of a power with an instantaneous duration (e.g. psychic damage from Mind Thrust could not be removed by Negate Psionics). One object, creature, or area is the target of the power. You make a negation check against the power (d20 + your mystic level, opposed by an equivalent roll by the manifester of the power) for each ongoing power until one is negated. If the check is tied, the negation fails. DM Note: if multiple powers are active, at your discretion, the player can nominate a specific power, or you can select one at random.
7524 Synaptic Static (7 psi, Ctn)
7525 As an action, you call into being an area of synaptic static, centred on – but excluding – yourself. This aura has a radius of 120 feet, and lasts for as long as you concentrate (or 1 minute has elapsed).
7526 Any creature within the synaptic static that attempts to use psionic powers (including mind flayers and other psionic monsters) must make an opposed Intelligence check (d20 + Modifier + Proficiency Bonus), on which they suffer disadvantage. If they fail, they expend psi-points but fail to manifest the power. Ongoing psionic effects are not removed, but a power maintained by concentration (that was manifested before entering the area of static) has to make the same opposed check as above, or the concentration is broken.
7527 Psychic Assault
7528 Id Insinuation (4-9 psi)
7529 As an action, target one creature you can see within 120 feet. If this creature fails an Intelligence save, it takes 1d8 psychic damage per psi point expended, and goes into a fury as its id runs momentarily rampant. On its next turn, it will not move from its current location, but Attack a random target within its reach, or Dodge if there is nothing to attack.
7530 Mind Flame (6-9 psi)
7531 As an action, you unleash devastating psychic energy in a 40 ft cone. Each creature in that area must make an Intelligence saving throw, or take 1d8 psychic damage per psi point expended and be Staggered (disadvantage on attack rolls, ability checks & saves, no reactions) until the end of its next turn.
7532 Psychic Crush (7-9 psi)
7533 As an action, you create an implosion of psychic energy a a point up to 90 feet from you. Each creature within 20 feet of that point takes 1d8 psychic damage per psi point expended, or half as much damage if they succeed with an Intelligence saving throw
7534 Towering Will
7535 Psionic Parry (1-7 psi)
7536 As a reaction, you gain a +1 bonus to an Intelligence, Wisdom, or Charisma saving throw for each psi point you spend on this power. You can use this power after rolling the die but before suffering the results.
7537 Psychic Riposte (2-8 psi)
7538 As a reaction, you impose disadvantage on an attack that would deal you psychic damage (or, gain advantage on your save). If you take damage, the attacker must save vs your DC or suffer psychic damage to the amount of d10 + d10 per 2 psi-points expended.
7539 Intellect Fortress (5 psi)
7540 As an action, you create a field of protective psychic energy. For a duration of 10 minutes, you, and those you select within 30 feet, gain resistance to psychic damage, immunity to Charm, and advantage on Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma saving throws. This field moves with you – any you select to benefit from this power must remain within the field to do so.
7541 Disciplines - Path of the Nomad
7542 Discordance
7543 Dimensional Screen (2 psi, Ctn)
7544 As a bonus action, you distort and wrap a dimensional fracture around your body. While you maintain concentration, or until 1 minute has elapsed, you gain resistance to force damage, and attack rolls against you have disadvantage. Further, if a creature misses you with a mêlée attack, you can use your reaction to force that creature to redirect their attack against a randomly chosen new target within its melee range.
7545 Unfortunately, the nature of this power means that you also suffer disadvantage on attack rolls (other than for your psionic talents and powers).
7546 Dimensional Shear (5-9 psi)
7547 As an action you target a single creature and rip apart the fabric of the dimension in which they exist. Such tears last but for a moment, but the target must make a Constitution save or suffer force damage of d6 + d6 per psi point expended. Objects can be similarly targeted.
7548 Planar Schism (7-9 psi, Ctn)
7549 As an action, you create multiple dimensional fractures within a 20-foot cube, up to 120 feet from your location. Through each fracture, the material of the current plane of existence is randomly exchanged with that of another. This creates a space that is highly inhospitable to life. While you maintain concentration (or until 1 min. elapses), any creature within this area must make a Dexterity save each turn, or suffer 4d4 from each of three randomly determined energy types: acid, cold, fire, lightning, thunder, or necrotic (desiccation). Expending 8 psi points increases damage to 5d4 per type; 9 psi points, to 6d4 per type. Successful saves result in half damage. Objects in area will be similarly damaged.
7550 Nomadic Reach
7551 Wrench (3 psi, Ctn)
7552 As an action, you target a creature within 90 feet. On a failed Constitution save, it is wrenched wholly into the Prime Material. Thus, this power only affects creatures which exist simultaneously on the Prime Material and another plane of existence. This includes most undead and summoned beings from other planes (excluding deities) such as elementals, demons and devils.
7553 A creature so wrenched suffers multiple impediments for as long as you maintain concentration (or until 1 minute has elapsed): its armour class is reduced by your intelligence bonus, it become vulnerable to normal weapons, and it suffers disadvantage on saving throws.
7554 Baleful Transposition (5 psi)
7555 As an action, choose one creature you can see within 120 yards (360 feet). If that creature fails a Wisdom saving throw, you and that creature teleport, swapping places. This ability fails and is wasted if either of you cannot fit into the destination space.
7556 Nomad’s Gate (7 psi, Ctn)
7557 As an action, you create an area of dim grey light within 5 feet of you that is recognisable as a gateway without frame or door. This portal is ~18 feet tall and ~10 feet wide, about the dimensions of a castle gate.
7558 Simultaneously, you create an identical shape at any point of your choice within 1 mile that you have viewed within the past 24 hours. Such viewing may be in person or through Clairvoyance, or extracted vis Probe. The distance between the gates increases to up 10 miles for a mystic following the Path of the Nomad. Until your concentration ends (or 1 hour elapses), anyone entering one of these gateways immediately teleports to the other, stepping out into an unoccupied space in front of the gate. The teleportation fails (although the gate remains) if there is no space for the creature to step into.
7559 Nomadic Sight
7560 Know Location (1-2 psi)
7561 This power is useful to mystics who frequently travel by teleportation or magical gateways. As an action, this power reveals general information about your current location, usually no more detailed than the response that might come from a simple farmer. For example, you might understand that you are “a few miles north of Waterdeep … as the crow flies.” You can query this understanding once per use of the power by expending a second psi-point. This provides additional but less specific information. For example, if you come to understand that you are “in the house of Kilgore the Taxidermist”, you might determine that this is “in Chendl, in the Kingdom of Furyondy”.
7562 Clairvoyance (4-9 psi, Ctn)
7563 Clairvoyance allows the mystic to see images from a distant area. You can pick any location that you can visualise, whether that be from your memory, or from a memory extracted from another’s mind. A detailed description, or an image, may also suffice if sufficiently accurate. This power will then allow you to see anything that you could, were you standing in that spot. Clairvoyance does not replace your normal eyesight: the distant scene is superimposed. Thus, most mystics close their eyes to avoid the confusion of images.
7564 Clairvoyance does not, it itself, enhance your vision, and once the scrying spot is chosen, it is fixed (although you can scan as if turning your head). For a new location, you need to use this power again. The more distant the viewed area is, the more difficult it is to use this power:
7565 Costs of Clairvoyance
7566 Scrying Distance (up to)
7567 Psi-Points
7568 10 miles
7569 4
7570 100 miles
7571 5
7572 500 miles
7573 6
7574 1000 miles
7575 7
7576 5000 miles
7577 8
7578 10000 miles
7579 9
7581 You are able to view the distant location for up to 10 minutes, as long as you maintain concentration. Your clairvoyant presence is undetectable by normal means, and cannot be dispelled or kept away by any form of magic.
7582 True Sight (7 psi)
7583 As an action, you gain truesight with a radius of 120 feet, which lasts for a duration of 1 hour.
7584 Nomad’s Step
7585 Dimension Door (2-9 psi)
7586 As a bonus action, and if you have yet to move on your turn, you open a dimensional fracture (that vaguely resembles a portal or empty doorway) and step, dive or otherwise enter this fracture. You instantly appear via a paired fracture up to 60 feet from your initial position per psi point spent, in an unoccupied space that you can see or otherwise visualise. The fracture only exists as you travel through it; another individual cannot use it unless you are carrying them.
7587 This transition does not provoke an opportunity attack, but is disorientating, and in consequence your movement is reduced to 0 for the remainder of that round. Note that you may also manifest this power as a reaction, e.g. to dodge a landslide, or to circumvent falls (and their associated damage).
7588 Transposition (3 psi)
7589 As a bonus action, and if you have yet to move on your turn, you and a willing ally (whom you can see within 60 feet) teleport, swapping places. This does not provoke opportunity attacks, but movement for both parties is reduced to 0 until their next turn. This ability fails and is wasted if either of you can’t fit in the destination space.
7590 Teleport (5-9 psi)
7591 The signature power for a nomad, and many mystics, Teleport allows instantaneous transportation of the individual to any location with which they are familiar. That familiarity need only extend to sufficient mental visualisation. There is no requirement for you to have visited the location yourself; you could, for example, extract the visualisation from another creature’s mind.
7592 As an action, and at the cost of all movement for that turn, you simply vanish from your current location and appear at the destination. This does not provoke an opportunity attack. Your clothing and worn equipment accompany you, at your discretion (e.g., if you are bound with ropes, you can leave those ropes at your point of origin).
7593 Note that you travel to the destination that you visualise, and if that destination has changed, you may suffer consequences. For example, visualising the top floor of a tower that has since been demolished will result in a rapid and potentially deadly fall – without warning – on arrival. Mystics who master this discipline often take great pains to ensure the accuracy of the information with which they form the visualisation. They often pursue the discipline of Nomadic Sight in consequence.
7594 The costs of teleportation are provided in the table below. The base cost determines the maximum distance possible accord to your psi-limit; the encumbered cost represents an additional penalty for the extra effort and is an exception to the psi-limit rule:
7595 Teleportation costs (psi points)
7596 Distance (up to)
7597 Base Cost
7598 Cost if Encumbered
7599 1 mile
7600 5
7601 15
7602 10 miles
7603 6
7604 18
7605 100 miles
7606 7
7607 21
7608 500 miles
7609 8
7610 24
7611 1000 miles
7612 9
7613 27
7615 Note that if you attempt to teleport when heavily encumbered, items that are so encumbering will not teleport with you, and you may arrive with little more than your clothing and other items worn about your person.
7616 You may teleport other individuals or creatures along with you, up to a number determined by your Intelligence bonus (but subject to your psi-limit). These others must be within 5 feet of you, or of each other if one is within 5 feet of you. There is no requirement that these individuals or creatures be willing.
7617 You must pay an additional cost for each individual or creature of medium or smaller size as indicated in the table above; large creatures incur the ‘encumbered’ cost. Attempts to teleport a large, encumbered, creature, or one of greater size, will automatically fail.
7618 Example: Anslon the Brave wishes to teleport 37 miles to a Temple of Eldath that he once visited, along with the body of a fallen comrade, and his pet cat. He must pay the cost for three individuals, 21 psi points (7 per creature), or 35 if he were also encumbered. Anslon needs to be at least 9th level to meet the psi-limit requirement for journeys of this distance.
7619 Obscurance
7620 Shades (1-7 psi, Ctn)
7621 As an action, you twist dimensions such that a space (sphere with a 10 ft radius per psi point expended) becomes shadowy and distorted. This is centered on any spot you can see within 60 feet. Light produced by spells of 2nd level or lower is suppressed in this area, and creatures within are at disadvantage, appearing as indistinct shades to one another, as well as to observers without the area. Only truesight or Perceive the Unseen can bypass the Shades effects, which last until you end your concentration, or 10 mins have elapsed.
7622 Step from Sight (5 psi)
7623 As a bonus action, you cloak yourself from sight, turning invisible and remaining so until 1 minute elapses, or until immediately after you target, damage, or otherwise affect any creature with an attack, a spell, or ability.
7624 Alternatively, and as an action, you can include one additional creature for every additional psi point you spend on this ability (to a maximum set by your Intelligence bonus). These creatures must be willing, visible to you and within 60 feet. Each targeted creature turns invisible, subject to the same restrictions.
7625 Walk in Shadows (7 psi, Ctn)
7626 As an action, you transform yourself, your clothing and equipment ‘about your person’ into living shadow. You blend perfectly into any other shadows.
7627 While in shadow-form, you cannot be detected by normal or enhanced senses (including darkvision); only by psionic or magical means. You gain immunity to all damage except for radiant – to which you become vulnerable – and psychic. You cannot interact physically with any object, including launching physical attacks. You lose the ability to speak, and spells cannot be cast (unless they have no components) but your psionic abilities, talents and powers remain functional.
7628 You can only travel through darkness and shadow. In dim light, your movement rate is halved, while in total darkness it is doubled. Under both conditions, you ignore difficult terrain and can pass spaces as narrow as one inch wide without effort.
7629 Areas of bright light are impassable: you cannot enter such areas, and are instantly displaced from them if they are created (this movement does not require any action from you and does not impact the amount of movement you can take). Failing to save against a Light spell (or equivalent), or holding a source of light, instantly ends the effects of this power. Otherwise, you can maintain this form, through concentration, for up to 1 hour.
7630 Planar Adept
7631 Planar Lore (2 psi, Ctn)
7632 Some mystics can glean information from creatures dwelling in other planes and dimensions. This power provides a means by which you can learn deeply-hidden secrets or unravel mysteries, but you must ask very specific questions as your informant is unlikely to volunteer information, and may even seek to distort or confuse the truth. You may ask one question on each of your turns. The power lasts for as long as you maintain concentration, or until 3 minutes have elapsed.
7633 DM’s Note: use secret Charisma (Persuasion) checks as a guide to the success of any line of questioning, but feel free to provide information to reward clever questions, or to advance plots and/or individual character goals.
7634 Banishment (4 psi, Ctn)
7635 As an action, you attempt to send one creature within 60’ that you can see from its current plane of existence. This creature must succeed on a Intelligence saving throw, or be so banished.
7636 If the target is native to their current plane of existence, they are banished to a pocket dimension, a featureless area with a benign environment, and whilst there, are incapacitated. The target remains banished for as long as you maintain concentration, or until 1 minute has elapsed. When the creature returns, it reappears in the space it left (or otherwise the nearest possible unoccupied space), no longer incapacitated.
7637 If the target is native to a different plane of existence, it is instead banished to its home plane for as long as you maintain concentration. If you concentrate for 1 minute, the creature does not return.
7638 Summon Planar Creature (5 psi)
7639 This power allows the mystic to reach into another plane, and use an action to grab whatever they happen to find there and teleport it to their plane. The identity and nature of the summoned creature is, therefore, unpredictable, although more powerful mystics have a greater chance of wrangling a more powerful creature. You can choose to reach into either good, neutral or evil-aligned planes, in order to influence the type of creature you grab, and if the creature is intelligent, you can attempt – once it arrives – to persuade or intimidate it into providing assistance.
7640 However, the creature is as likely to fly into a murderous rage when it discovers its plight and be hostile to any creatures in its vicinity – unless you have some other means of exerting control. Thus you can have it appear up to 120 yards from you in your line of sight. The summoned creature will rmain for up to an hour.
7641 Disciplines– Path of the Shaper
7642 Adaptation
7643 Acclimate (1 psi)
7644 As an action, you – or a creature you touch – ignore the effects of extreme heat or cold (but not cold or fire damage) for the next hour.
7645 Cat Fall (2 psi)
7646 As a reaction, you ignore falling damage when falling from 30 feet. or less, and take half damage from falls over greater distances.
7647 Bestial Form (2 psi + )
7648 As an action, you alter your physical form to gain one or more of the following effects. Each effect has its own psi point cost. Add them together to determine the total cost. The individual effects, but not the total, are subject to the psi-limit. Unless you end it – a bonus action – this transformation lasts for 1 hour.
7649 Amphibious (2 psi). You gain gills and can breathe both air & water.
7650 Clawed (3 psi). Your forearms resemble those of a bear, with long razor-sharp claws. With these claws you can make a proficient mêlée attack which deals 2d4 + Strength bonus in slashing damage, along with a bonus attack for 1d4 slashing damage.
7651 Climbing (2 psi). You grow tiny, hooked claws and gain a climbing speed equal to your walking speed.
7652 Keen Senses (2 psi). Your eyes and ears become more sensitive, gaining you advantage on Perception checks. Your head takes on a bestial appearance.
7653 Predator Senses (3 psi). You gain a keen sense of smell and an instinct to detect prey. You can are aware of invisible creatures and objects within 10 feet, even if blinded. Your head takes on a bestial appearance.
7654 Swimming (2 psi). Webbing grows between your fingers while your legs become a powerful tail, akin to that of a dolphin; you gain a swimming speed equal to your walking speed.
7655 Tough Hide (2 psi). Your gain a tough leathery hide; raising your base AC to 12. At your discretion, this hide is covered in fur.
7656 Winged (5 psi). Wings sprout from your back. You gain a flying speed equal to your walking speed.
7657 Anslon the Brave is confronted by a band of goblins. He adopts a bestial form, transforming his limbs, head, and hide, to gain slashing attacks (claws), an instinct for prey (predator senses) and improved defense (tough hide). He expends 8 psi, and somewhat resembles a werewolf in hybrid form.
7658 Elementalism
7659 Shape Element (1 psi)
7660 The mystic is able to shape each of the four elements to his will, either instantaneously (taking an action) or for up to 1 hour. This delivers equivalent effects to the Control Flames, Gust, Mold Earth, & Shape Water cantrips – you pick which specific element you wish to shape when manifesting the power.
7661 Adaptive Shield (3 psi)
7662 When you take acid, cold, fire, lightning, or thunder damage, you can use your reaction to gain resistance to damage of that type – including the triggering damage – until the end of your next turn.
7663 Elemental Form (7 psi, Ctn)
7664 With this power, you are able to take an action to transform your body, clothing and personal equipment into a limbed, bipedal form composed of one of the four fundamental elements: earth, air, fire or water, as if a creature from the respective plane. This form lasts while you maintain concentration, or until 1 hour has elapsed.
7665 While in elemental form, you gain the resistances, immunities, and vulnerabilities of a creature composed of that element, but retains your limbs, your senses and your mind.
7666 Resistances & Immunities
7667 All forms grant resistance to bludgeoning, piercing, and slashing from nonmagical weapons; immunity to poison; immunity to the paralysed, petrified & poisoned conditions.
7668 Air, Fire and Water forms are immune to being grappled or restrained.
7669 Fire is additionally immune to fire damage.
7670 Water is additionally resistant to acid damage,
7671 Air is additionally resistant to damage from lightning and thunder.
7673 Vulnerabilities
7674 The Earth form is vulnerable to thunder damage
7675 Air, Fire and Water forms are vulnerable to cold damage.
7676 Note that while these vulnerabilities can be mitigated using Adaptive Shield, this presupposes that psi points are available, and that you have not otherwise used your reaction in that turn.
7677 Illumination
7678 Brilliance (2 psi, Ctn)
7679 As an action, an object you touch radiates light in a 30-foot radius and dim light for an additional 10 feet.
7680 Alternatively, a creature you touch radiates light in the same manner if it fails a Dexterity saving throw. While lit in this manner, the creature cannot hide, and attack rolls against it gain advantage. The effects of this power last until your concentration ends, or 1 minute elapses.
7681 Radiant Beam (5 psi)
7682 As an action, you project a beam of light at one creature you can see within 60 feet. On a failed Dexterity save, this creature takes 6d6 radiant damage and is blinded until the end of your next turn.
7683 Enduring Invisibility (7 psi, Ctn)
7684 As an action, you turn invisible and remain so until your concentration ends, or 10 minute elapse. You become momentarily visible if you attack (including casting a spell or using a psionic ability that targets an enemy) but immediately become invisible again.
7685 Becoming visible in this manner makes you vulnerable to reactions from your opponent, and if you haven’t moved from the location where you became visible, they can continue to attack you, albeit at disasdvantage.
7686 Molecularism
7687 Innervate (2-8 psi)
7688 As an action, you deliver a touch that inflicts a surge of wracking pain. This requires a successful mêlée (discipline) attack roll, and delivers 1d8 psychic damage per psi point expended. In addition, the target suffers disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks until the end of the next round. This can be non-lethal damage.
7689 Undead, constructs, and oozes are immune to this power.
7690 Molecular Agitation (1-9 Psi, Ctn)
7691 Molecular agitation enables the mystic to excite the molecules of a substance within 90 feet: paper ignites, wood smoulders, skin blisters, etc. This takes an action to initiate, and may be used, for example, to start a campfire, light a candle or dispose of an unwanted document. If used against a creature, this causes 1 point of fire damage.
7692 If you concentrate, and remains within range, this power lasts up to 7 rounds, at a cost of 1 psi-point per round. Both duration and cost are bound by the psi-limit. In the second round, this power inflicts 1d4 damage; in the third and fourth rounds, 1d6 damage; in the fifth, sixth and seventh rounds, the damage rises to 1d8, while 1d10 in inflicted in each of rounds eight and nine. Targets wearing metal armour, or who are otherwise metallic, take double damage. The target makes a Constitution save each round to reduce damage for that round by half. Negate Psionics, Cryokinesis, or breaking the mystic’s concentration will interrupt this power.
7693 Cryokinesis (1-9 Psi, Ctn)
7694 This power is the literal opposite of molecular agitation, as the mystic calms molecules to induce an increasing chill, freezing an object or creature within 90 feet. For example you could frost the leaves of a tree, or freeze it to its core, or you could craft a solid pathway of ice across a flowing river. If you concentrate and remain within range, this power lasts up to 7 rounds at a cost of 1 psi-point per round. Both duration and cost are bound by the psi-limit.
7695 This power inflicts 1 point of cold damage in the first round, 1d4 damage in the second; in the third and fourth rounds, 1d6 damage; in the fifth, sixth and seventh rounds, the damage rises to 1d8, while 1d10 is inflicted in each of rounds eight and nine. Targets wearing metal armour, or who are otherwise metallic, take double damage. The target makes a Constitution save each round to reduce damage for that round by half. Negate Psionics, Molecular Agitation, or breaking the mystic’s concentration will interrupt this power.
7696 Psychokinesis
7697 Levitate (1-5 psi, Ctn)
7698 Levitation allows the mystic to float; it is the use of telekinesis on oneself. As an action, you rise vertically, up to a height of 20 feet per psi point expended, to a maximum of 100 feet, at a rate of 20 feet per round, and remain suspended at your desired height while you maintain concentration (or until 10 mins elapse).
7699 You can use your per turn movement to change your height within the maximum, but levitation is not flight and you can move horizontally only by pushing or pulling against a fixed object or surface within reach (such as a wall or a ceiling). If you push yourself from a fixed surface you will drift at 10 feet / round until you strike a fixed object, or the power ends. If you are aloft when the power ends, you float gently to the ground.
7700 Telekinesis (3 psi, Ctn)
7701 You gain the ability to move or manipulate creatures or objects by thought. As an action, this power delivers equivalent effects to the 5th level wizard spell of the same name for as long as you maintain concentration (or 10 minutes has elapsed).
7702 Telekinetic Barrier (5 psi, Ctn)
7703 A telekinetic barrier is an immobile field of mental force that resembles the wizard spell Wall of Force.
7704 As an action, you create an invisible barrier up to 60 feet in length and 10 feet in height (or any other vertical surface of 600 square feet or less) at a range of up to 60 feet. You may instead create a dome with a 10 ft radius centered upon yourself.
7705 No physical object or any form of energy can penetrate the barrier, although it is possible to circumvent the barrier by using abilities such as Teleport or Dimension Door, while disintegration will destroy the barrier entirely.
7706 The telekinetic barrier is immobile once created, and if you move more than 60 feet from it, it collapses. Otherwise, the barrier lasts as long as you maintain concentration, or 10 minutes have elapsed.
7707 Shaping
7708 Weave Ectoplasm (1-7 psi)
7709 You draw together ectoplasm to create a nonmagical, unattended object of either vegetable or mineral in nature, no larger than a 3 ft cube. The object must be of a form and material that you have seen in some manner. For every additional psi-point expended, you can increase the maximum volume by a 3 ft cube, or the duration by an additional hour or minute according to the material (below). The weaving requires one full turn per 3 ft cube created.
7714 Duration of Weave Ectoplasm
7715 Material
7716 Base Duration
7717 Vegetable
7718 24 hours
7719 Stone/Crystal/Base Metal
7720 12 hours
7721 Precious metal
7722 1 hour
7723 Gemstones
7724 10 mins
7725 Adamantine/Mithral
7726 1 min
7727 Elemental Apparition (4 psi,Ctn)
7730 As an action, you shape and manipulate an existing element to assume the form of an elemental creature. For example, you can cause an apparent Fire Elemental to surge from a large (lit) brazier or bonfire, a Water Elemental from a pond or river, an Earth Elemental from bare earth or rock (not from worked stone), or an Air Elemental from the very winds themselves. (note that a very small source may result in a similarly small apparition, further constraining the CR value, below)
7731 This apparition appears in an unoccupied space you can see within 120 feet, adjacent to its source, and persists until your concentration ends (or until 1 hour has elapsed). While this apparition shares the properties of a summoned elemental (of CR equal to half of one less than your mystic level), it is but the element animated into a form under your mental control (it is not ‘summoned’; is not sentient). When this power ends, the apparition returns to its raw element.
7732 Crystallise (7 psi, Ctn)
7733 You attempt to turn one creature that you can see within 60’ into solid crystal. Initiating the process takes an acition. If the target fails a Constitution saving throw, it is Restrained as its body begins to harden. A creature so restrained must make another Constitution saving throw at the end of each of its turns. If it successfully saves three times, the crystallisation effect dissipates; if it fails three times (including the initial fail), it is turned to a solid crystalline form and subjected to the Petrified condition for as long as concentration is maintained. Success and failure do not need to be consecutive; keep track of both until the target collects three of a kind.
7735 If you maintain concentration for 10 rounds (1 minute) on an effected creature (3 failed saves), the crystallisation is permanent, although this can be countered by Negate Psionics or magical abilities that can change the form of a target, e.g. Polymorph (for which reversion to the target’s true form would not be limited by that spell’s duration).
7736 The crystalline form is robust. In addition to the effects of the petrified condition, it has AC16 and immunity to piercing damage. However, should the creature be physically damaged while in crystalline form, it suffers from similar damage &/or deformities should it revert to its original state.
7787 House rules
7788 Rules written in red are not used. Rules with a blue header are used (and red, if on Athas).
7789 Athasian pricing
7790 Metal is scarce on Athas and they use ceramic pieces and eighth bits (0,125 CP) as currency. Ceramic pieces hold the same value as copper pieces. All prices listed in SP are priced with the equivalent number in CP. A bagpipe costing 30 SP under ordinary circumstances will cost 30 CP on Athas. The exception to this rule is armour and weapons which have the metal quality. These items retain their value in SP also on Athas. A Breastplate valued at 400 SP, would still cost 400 SP on Athas. This means that relative to other items without the “metal” quality, it would cost the equivalent of 40 000 CP.
7791 No tool proficiencies
7792 All tool proficiencies are removed from the game. Some of these are replaced by ordinary skills:
7793 Thief’s tools  Sleight of hand
7794 Musical instrument  Performance
7795 Disguise kit  Deception
7796 Forgery  Sleight of hand
7797 Other proficiencies are replaced by the Craft skill (see below). Those who feel the need for a Carpentry or Gaming Dice skill can fuck off.
7798 The adventuring day:
7799 The normal adventuring day can be split into 3 eight-hour periods. During each period you can perform one of the following activities (amongst others); work, tend, overland travel, research/information gathering, long rest (including sleep), crafting, hunting/foraging. Long rests, crafting and hunting/foraging follow their own rules. If you spend an eight-hour period researching or gathering information under the right circumstances, you gain Advantage on the appropriate skill roll. If using the work activity in a populated area, you are able to use one of your skills to earn your ability modifier for that skill, in SP (GP). If using the tend activity you can grant a creature a save versus an ongoing condition providing the creature was originally granted a saving throw. You can only perform one of each such task each 24-hour period. If you attempt to push this, spending time doing the same activity (resting is exempt from this) for another eight-hour period, or if resting is not at least one of the activities you perform during the day, you must succeed on a DC 14 constitution save or gain a level of exhaustion.
7800 New skill (craft)
7801 There are five different Craft skills depending on what it lets you create: armour, weapons, herbal concoctions, alchemical substances and poison. Each can be taken as long as you have access to it through your class, through character generation as a background skill or through selecting a feat that grants a skill. Whenever the GM asks for a check that relates to one of these craft skills, you gain advantage on that roll. For instance, you might get advantage on an Intelligence (History) check to learn the origin of a set of ancient armour, if you have the craft: armour skill. Knowing a craft skill also lets you craft related items from the following list:
7802 Armour: any armour.
7803 Weapon: any weapon or arrow.
7804 Herbal: any herbalist concoctions.
7805 Alchemy: any alchemical substance.
7806 Poison: any poison.
7807 Crafting:
7808 Crafting follows either one set of rules for crafting poisons, alchemical substances and herbalist concoctions, or one for making armour and weapons. For armour and weapons divide the price of the item you wish to create, by your Proficiency Bonus (rounding up), and spend the amount in raw materials (SP). Then take that amount and multiply by 8 for the number of hours it takes to forge. Armour and weapons require a stationary forge and workshop to craft, and do not expire after 24 hours. For example: you can create a short sword if you have the Craft (Weapons) skill. If you have a proficiency bonus of +3, you can spend 4 SP in material costs (10 SP / 3, rounding up), and 32 hours in the forge (4 * 8). It would normally take four days to forge the short sword. Crafting herbal concoctions, alchemist supplies or poisons (*) requires access to related items from the Adventuring gear table (see equipment). To create an item, find the price on the equipment table and divide that by your proficiency bonus. You have to spend that number of doses from your kit (usually each kit contains 10 doses), and the same number of hours to create the item. For example: you can craft an herbal concoction of “allnight” if you have the Craft (Herbal) skill and access to a herbalism kit. If your proficiency bonus is +3 you can create it by spending (18/3 = 6) 6 hours crafting and removing 6 doses from your kit. You could create two, but this would take 12 hours and 12 doses, with the risk of gaining Exhaustion. Created poisons, concoctions and supplies are fickle and only lasts 24 hours until they expire but they can be fortified by spending twice the amount of doses when creating them. Crafting is considered a strenuous activity, which means you can spend one eight-hour period for each 24-hour period crafting, without risking exhaustion.
7809 * As an optional rule for crafting poisons you can follow the rules for pricing according to the potency of the poison (see below).
7810 Create poisons (optional): If you are proficient in the Craft (Poison) skill, you can create a poison, choosing an Effect, a way of Intake and a Save DC). When you have decided upon an effect calculate the cost by multiplying (X1*X2*X3), rounding up. A poison causing 6d6 damage on injury, with a save DC of 14 would have a full price of 41 SP (3*3*4,5=41, rounded up). You can then create that poison according to the ordinary rules for crafting.

7866 [1] An ingested poison will be able to affect up to 5 people per dose as long as they all ingest the poison.
7867 [2] An inhaled poison will cover a 10*10 feet area and affect all within.
7868 [3] Once applied to a weapon or piece of ammunition, the poison retains its potency for 1 minute or until you hit with the weapon or ammunition.
7869 [4] An item coated in poison will remain poisonous until the poison is removed or 24 hours.
7870 [5] The effect lasts for a number of minutes equal to the save DC.
7871 [6] A poison causing damage will cause half damage on a successful save.
7873 Action points
7874 There are no inspiration points, though the rules for Advantage from other sources remain. Instead, at the start of every session you create a pool of Action Points equal to the number of players. During that session, whenever someone makes an attack roll, saving throw, or ability check, they can take one or more points from the pool and add 1d6 per point removed, to the roll.
7875 Exhausting beat-down
7876 Any time you are dropped to 0 HP you gain a level of Exhaustion. This counts as any other Exhaustion level, this means that if you somehow manage to drop to 0 HP six times you are dead.
7877 Thirst and starvation
7878 Characters that go without water for 1 day will start feeling the effects. For each day after the first you gain a level of Exhaustion. If you are wearing medium or heavy armour or carrying anything weight equal to your Constitution score you gain two levels. Drinking water during a long rest will remove any such Exhaustion levels.
7879 Characters that go without food for days equal to con modifier * 2 (min 1) will start feeling the effects. Unless they roll a constitution save DC 8 + 2 per day or gain a level of Exhaustion. If you are wearing medium or heavy armour you have Disadvantage on the saving throw. Eating food during a long rest will remove any such Exhaustion levels.
7880 Hit point progression
7881 When reaching a new level take the HD of the class you are taking a level in and roll a die equal to half the HD (D6 becomes D3, D8 becomes D4 etc.). Then add half the value of you max HD for the class. A character gaining a level in a class with D8 HD, would roll 1d4 + 4.
7882 Piecemeal armour
7883 Piecemeal armour grant only a fraction of the bonus of a full suit of armour. Take the AC -10 and divide it according to which piece is used and add these together. You always round down to calculate the AC and up to calculate the weight.
7884 Body part
7885 AC bonus
7886 Torso
7887 0,5
7888 One arm
7889 0,125
7890 One Leg
7891 0,125
7892 Head
7893 0
7895 Example: full chain mail armour has AC 16. This is divided into torso = 6 * 0,5, two arms = 6 * (0,125*2), two legs = 6 * (0,125*2) and head = 0. Finding a chain mail arm and two legs would give AC = 10 + ((6 * 0,125 )+  (6 * (0,125*2))) = 10 + 2,25, rounded down to 12. Do the same with the weight: the torso of a chain mail would weigh 55*0,5 ≈ 28, one arm would weigh 55*0,125 ≈ 7).
7896 Any piecemeal armour has all the properties of its worst part.
7897 Inferior materials
7898 Inferior materials can only be used for certain melee weapons, depending on the material. Weapons made of inferior material can be broken according to the rules below. A magical weapon of any material can never be broken in combat. Inferior materials cost less than ordinary, use the corresponding price modification (mod.) to calculate the price of each item.
7899 Steel can be replaced by natural materials like animals exoskeleton, shell and scales. Creating armour from such creatures can be cheaper, but the armour is inferior.

7920 Medicine skill
7921 You can use your skill to heal others of their wounds. If you succeed on a Medicine skill roll versus DC 15, you grant the target 2d4+2 HP. You can only do this once per long rest per creature.
7922 E11
7923 Class levels cap at 11th level. Thereafter you can buy a feat for every 15 000 exp that you earn. You can never have more than 1 more of either cultural or general feats, than the other (unless you gain a cultural feat from your race).
7924 Encumbrance
7925 Unencumbered: 0-4 times your strength: no modifiers
7926 Encumbered: 5-9 times your strength: speed is reduced by 10’.
7927 Heavily encumbered: 10-14 timers your strength: speed reduced by 20’, disadvantage on ability checks, attack rolls and saving throws relying on strength, dexterity or constitution.
7928 Immobile: 15+ times your strength. As above, and speed is 0
7929 Foraging
7930 Foraging includes every activity to gather food. Any check made to forage is made during an 8 hour interval and is considered strenuous activity. When foraging, a character makes a Wisdom (Survival) check, with the DC determined by the abundance of food and water in the region.
7931 Lush (rich forest) DC 10
7932 Abundant (forest) DC 15
7933 Limited (hills) DC 20
7934 Very limited (plains and mountains) DC 25
7935 Nearly depleted (desert) DC 30
7936 Depleted (salt flats and silt) DC – (no food or water available).
7937 * Foraging in heavy snow increases the DC by 10
7938 If you succeed on a Survival skill check you gain 1 pound of food (enough to sustain a person for 1 day). For every 3 which you beat the DC by, you gain another pound of food. A foraging character finds nothing on a failed check. Using hunting equipment, requires you to remain in the same area during the eight-hour period. If multiple characters forage, it is treated as taking the Help action. For example: Rollo has a Survival skill bonus of +9 and is foraging in hilly terrain (Limited, DC 20). He uses a bear trap which reduces the DC by 8, to a total DC of 12. Rollo rolls a 20 (11+9) on his skill check, and since he beats the check by 8, he finds 1+2 pounds of food. The next day Rollo’s friend Mollo decides to aid in the search for food. He has a lower Survival Skill bonus and so the player decided that he will aid Rollo, granting him advantage on the roll.
7939 You always find enough water to cover your needs for the day, unless foraging in very limited or nearly depleted terrain. In such cases you find 1 galleon of water per pound of food you manage to forage.

8067 Ammunition
8068 The ammunition does additional damage equal to your Wisdom bonus; moreover, your target’s next attack is made with disadvantage. At the end of the battle, you can recover your used arrows if circumstances allow it, unless you rolled a 1, in which case, that arrow is lost or broken beyond recovery
8069 Shield

8071 When you roll a critical hit on a melee attack roll while fighting with this shield in hand, instead of applying the critical hit effects to your foe, you may choose instead to automatically block the next attack that would’ve otherwise successfully struck you during this fight. No matter what your attacker rolls, treat it as though they failed to roll enough to hit your Armour Class.
8072 You gain a +1 bonus to AC when you wield this shield. This bonus is in addition to the shield’s normal bonus to AC. In addition, it cannot be destroyed by mischance or the attacks of enemies, nor can it be disarmed during combat

You must take a short or long rest before you can use this ability again.

8084 Wondrous

If successful, the refuge is within two day’s march; if you roll 20 or above, it is mere hours away. 8094 Sigil (of the Sorcerer Regent)
8095 Every templar receives a sigil that is the sign of their rank and station. The form of the sigil is unique to each city state but is always unmistakable for what it is. You gain an authority score equal to your Proficiency Bonus + you Charisma modifier. You gain the authority to take one of the actions described below against so long as your authority scores is equal to are higher than the social standing score from the list below: higher social rankings of individuals:

8109 Requisition: You can draw upon the resources of your city, gaining the use of slaves or soldiers and overriding the wishes of their owner. You can requisition slaves with HD equal to your authority score times 3, or soldiers equal to your authority score. Example: a templar with an authority score of 5 could requisition the aid of up to 15 HD worth of slaves, or 5 HD worth of soldiers. Your authority does not extend to confiscating items for personal use. You can intrude on citizens of social standing equal to your authority score - 2.
8111 Accuse: You may have a citizen imprisoned indefinitely, awaiting the gathering of evidence against them. You may only imprison one suspect in such a manner at a time. You can Accuse citizens of social standing equal to your Proficiency authority score - 4.
8112 Judge: You may pass judgment. This includes setting fines, prison sentences, death sentences or anything else you wish, within the laws of your city-state. You can Judge citizens of social standing equal to your authority score - 6.
8113 Special: Anyone caught wearing such a sigil without actually being part of the Templarate, will most likely face summary execution or enslavement.
8123 Obsidian Sphere
8124 (Esper) The sphere will retain its levitating abilities even if loaded with weight. The sphere will remain levitating even when carrying up to 100 times the weight limit for the Telekinesis power. This means that an un-augmented use of the Telekinesis power by a psionicist with an Intelligence modifier of +2, would make them able to lift a rock weighing 200 pounds. If the user spends a psi-dice augmenting their use of the power, this would be increased to 5200 pounds ((2*100) + (50*100) = 5 200). The energy stored within the orb allows for the item to be lifted for up to 12 hours. The most common use for such orbs is to build them into decks of silt-skimmers.(Esper) The sphere will retain its levitating abilities even if loaded with weight. If the user spends a psi-dice augmenting their use of the power, this would be increased to 5200 pounds ((2*100) + (50*100) = 5 200). The energy stored within the orb allows for the item to be lifted for up to 12 hours. The most common use for such orbs is to build them into decks of silt-skimmers.The obsidian sphere allows a psionicist to infuse it with their Telekinesis power making the sphere hover inches above the surface. The sphere can retain the power for far longer than the Telekinesis power would usually allow and enables him to lift far more weight.
8125 (Esper) The sphere will retain its levitating abilities even if loaded with weight. The sphere will remain levitating even when carrying up to 100 times the weight limit for the Telekinesis power. 8126 (Esper) The sphere will retain its levitating abilities even if loaded with weight. The sphere will remain levitating even when carrying up to 100 times the weight limit for the Telekinesis power. This means that an un-augmented use of the Telekinesis power by a psionicist with an Intelligence modifier of +2, would make them able to lift a rock weighing 200 pounds. If the user spends a psi-dice augmenting their use of the power, this would be increased to 5200 pounds ((2*100) + (50*100) = 5 200). The energy stored within the orb allows for the item to be lifted for up to 12 hours. The most common use for such orbs is to build them into decks of silt-skimmers.
8127 Using the Telekinesis power on the orb itself allows you to retain concentration on the object for up to 10 hours, after which the levitation will slowly dissipate until it falls to the ground after another hour. They can also lift a total amount equal to 10 tons per Psi point spent (effectively allowing for augmentation of the Telekinesis power). The sphere will retain its levitating abilities as long as you don’t exceed the ships tonnage. The most common use for such orbs is to build them into the decks of silt-skimmers. Stability is ensured by binding the entire structure with thin threads made of a metallic material which is connected to the orb. This causes the levitating properties to be dispersed across the ship, ensuring balance. Copper is the most common material, since it’s cheaper (see equipment and skimmers (below)).
8128 Psion Stone:
8129 Smoothly polished, rumoured to derive from the gizzard stones of Athasian drakes and are about the size of a duck’s egg. The stones themselves are able to store psychic energy. It can hold up to half the attuned Psionicists maximum psi points. The attuned mystic can store or retrieve psi points during a short (or long) rest. Retrieved points can only be used to recoup those previously expended, whilst storing psi points drains these from the mystic’s mind.
8130 Skimmers:
8131 Though the most common silt ships are those that have massive wheels, which allow them to roll along mudbanks beneath the silt, many would prefer skimmers if they had the means. A silt skimmer needs a psionicist who masters the Telekinesis power (see Obsidian orb above).
8132 Mind Worms:
8133 Used to control beasts, like Driks, Mekillots and Giant Beetles. Mind worms allows or a fragment of a psionicists mind to be transferred into another creature by having it eat its way into the brain of said creature. The psinoicist must allow the worm to first leech some of his power, usually through the temple, granting them a level of Exhaustion. This extraction takes a minimum of one hour. The psionicists must possess some sort of power which control minds, such as the Inthraller discipline. The worm then detaches from the psinoicist, and the psionicist will feel that his mind is split between themselves and the worm. Then they must allow the worm to dig into an opening in the creatures skull, like the ear or nose. This process usually takes several days, during which handlers must actively soothe the animals as the process is extremely irritating. The creature is allowed a Wisdom save against the save difficulty of the psinoicist psionic power. If the creature fails its save a bond is created which cannot be broken except for by other psionic powers or magic. While it is active the psionicist can give the creature orders by taking an Action. If the psionicist remains out of sight for the creature its mind will deteriorate, effectively granting it a level of Exhaustion for each full day which passes from the last time the psionicist was in its presence. The psinocist can control a number of creatures up to his Intelligence bonus at the same time.

8232 Beast (First Magnitude):
8233 Effects:
8234 Base: you detect the presence of creatures of a particular type of animal or beast within 30 feet of you. Use the range increment enhancement to increase the range.
8235 Enhance (voice): you can speak to animals or beasts of a particular type, that you can sense with the base effect of this spell (4)
8236 Enhance (befriend): you convince an animal or beast of CR no higher than your proficiency bonus to aid you. Choose one of the following: the creature will stand guard for 24 hours and defend an area. You can have it relay a message of no more than 25 words to a target you designate. The creature must reach the target within 24 hours, or the effect ends. Alternatively, the creature will scout for the duration of the spell. The creature will fight on your side for the duration. The creature is allowed a Charisma save to ignore your order, with Advantage if it is currently threatened (6)
8237 Enhance (strong): you increase the CR of the Beast (2 per +1 CR)

8293 Charm (First Magnitude):
8294 Effects:
8295 Base: a target humanoid creature which fails a Wisdom save will regard you indifferently.
8296 Enhance (beast): as base, but include Beast type (3)
8297 Enhance (creature): as base, but include any creature type (6)
8298 Enhance (friendly): as base, but the creatures regard you as friendly (4)
8299 Enhance (thrall): turn into a thrall. A target creature is dominated by your will (18)

8343 Emotion (First Magnitude):
8344 Effects:
8345 Base: You influence the emotions of a target, making them become Frightened of you, if they fail a Wisdom saving throw
8346 Enhance (calm): instead of inducing fear, you can remove the frightened condition (6)
8347 Enhance (confusion): you cause distress and confusion instead of fear. If affected, each round at the start of their round the target must roll a d10 and act according to the following table* (4):
8348 D10
8349 Action
8350 1
8351 Attack you with melee or ranged weapons or move closer if attack not possible.
8352 2
8353 Choose from any other action in this list
8354 3-4
8355 Do nothing but babble incoherently.
8356 5-6
8357 Flee from you at top possible speed.
8358 7-10
8359 Attack nearest creature.
8360 * Any confused creature that is attacked automatically attacks its attackers on its next turn, as long as it is still confused when its turn comes.

8418 Enchant (First Magnitude):
8419 Effects:
8420 Base: a creature with no more than 3 HD have disadvantage on perception checks. This effect is broken if the creature takes any damage. Unless otherwise noted creatures are affected from closest to furthest away from the initial source. Creatures with higher HD than you are able to affect are ignored.
8421 Enhance (sleep): the creature falls asleep as long as it does not succeed on a Wisdom save. The creature can be awakened by vigorous movement, shaking or being dealt damage (5)
8422 Enhance (HD): the spell affects creatures of one higher HD (3)
8423 Enhance (stun): the creature is stunned (3)

8463 Figment (First Magnitude):
8464 Effects:
8465 Base: you create an illusion of a humanoid which will always remain within 5 feet of you. The illusion copies your exact movements but makes no sound. Alternatively, the illusion can be of a simple object no larger than 1 cubic foot. The illusion disappears if interacted with
8466 Enhance (duplicate): create another duplicate of the illusion (2)
8467 Enhance (independent): the illusions actions are unscripted, meaning it acts on its own (6)
8468 Enhance (senses): the illusion is audible (2), olfactory (2) and/or thermal (2)
8469 Enhance (size): the illusion increases in size to a creature of larger size category (6 per), monstrous (3) or by 1 cubic foot (1)
8470 Enhance (chameleon): illusion covers yourself, and can replicate the area surrounding you, granting you +10 bonus on any checks to hide and allowing you to hide in plain sight (6).
8471 Enhance (script): you create an illusory script on any surface, which only a creature specified by you can read. You need to share at least 1 language in which to convey the message (4)

8520 Force (First Magnitude):
8521 Effects:
8522 Base: you cover yourself in an invisible forcefield, adding a +1 bonus to you AC.
8523 Base (detonate): Alternatively you create a hammer of force. It can be used as a melee weapon, or a ranged weapon with a base range of 10 feet. The hammer deals 1d4 + your Strength modifier in damage.
8524 Base (force weapon): You create a ball of invisible force that immediately detonates causing 1d6 damage. Save versus dexterity for half.
8525 Enhance (protection): per +1 bonus to your AC (2)
8526 Enhance (shut/knock): you force a lid, door or other form of portal to open or shut. The base DC to open a shut portal is equal to you spell save DC (2, +2 per +1 DC)

8708 Spirit (First Magnitude):
8709 Effects:
8710 Base: You can ask a single question of a corpse which will be answered by “yes,” “no,” or other brief responses. The corpse always answers honestly. If the corpse has been subject to this spell within the past week, the spell fails. You can cast this spell effect on a corpse that has been deceased for any amount of time, but the body must be mostly intact to be able to respond. A damaged corpse may be able to give a partial answer or partially correct answer, but it must at least have a mouth in order to speak at all. The corpse can decide not to answer your question.
8711 Enhance (question): add another question (4)
8712 Enhance (converse): you can ask questions with more complex answers (6)
8713 Enhance (spirit): instead of using a corpse, you contact the spirit of a deceased creature (20)

8746 Enhance (duplicate): you summon another creature of the same CR (8)

8768 Second Magnitude Spells
8769 Air (Second Magnitude):
8770 Effects:
8771 Base: you create a brief blast of air. The force of the wind is strong enough to extinguishes candles, torches, and similar unprotected flames. Any larger flames will flicker.
8772 Enhance (breathe): instead of disturbing the air around you, you can choose to avoid the need to breathe entirely (6).
8773 Enhance (strong): Small flames are extinguished. Creatures of size tiny or smaller, within 5 ft. must succeed on a Strength saving throw or be moved to the outside of the range. A sustained gust grants disadvantage on any ranged attacks (4).
8774 Enhance (debris): the wind makes ranged attacks impossible. You can extinguish all natural fires. The wind itself cause 1d4 damage (4)
8775 Enhance (storm): the wind effects creatures of +1 size category (4 per)

8805 Energy (Second Magnitude):
8806 Effects:
8807 Base: you create energy of a particular type; cold, fire, lightning or thunder. The energy deals 1d6 damage. The target is allowed a Dexterity save for half damage.
8808 Enhance: the energy takes on more, and even contradictory types. You can add an energy type from the list above. This will bypass any resistance or immunities to one of the effects, and creatures who are vulnerable to one of the types are considered vulnerable to the whole effect (6)

8847 Undeath (Second Magnitude):
8848 Effects:
8849 Base: you animate the bones of creatures with the power of undeath, making it move at your command. The total HD of the creatures cannot exceed your proficiency bonus. The undead remain under your control for the duration, after which they remain undead, but you do not retain any control over them.
8850 Enhance: you can animate zombies (-)
8851 Enhance: you can animate a dead creature into a shadow, ghouls or spectre (5)
8852 Enhance: you can animate a dead creature into a ghast, poltergeist, mummy or, wight (8)
8853 Enhance: you can animate a dead creature into a ghost, revenant or wraith (12)
8854 Enhance: you can raise additional creatures with HD equal to your proficiency bonus (4 per increment).
8855 Enhance: you gain control of mindless undead with max HD according to the rules above (4)
