8885 Earth (Second Magnitude):
8886 Effects:
8887 Base: your skin becomes earth-like, the ground you touch soften; wet earth becomes thick mud, dry earth becomes loose sand or dirt, and stone becomes soft clay easily mouldable or chopped. You cannot affect magical, enchanted, dressed, or worked stone. The area is considered difficult terrain unless the target succeeds on a Strength saving throw.
8888 Enhance (mud): the effect goes deeper, and the earth becomes even softer. A creature caught within the area have Disadvantage on any melee attacks, and it’s movement is reduced to 5 feet. Even on a successful Strength save, the area is still considered difficult terrain (4)
8889 Enhance (spikes): instead of softening, you can mould the earth, potentially into spikes or similar, dealing 1d4 damage. The ground becomes difficult terrain (6).
8890 Enhance (submerge): you Submerge yourself into sand, earth or rock, retaining your movement while underground (5).

8930 Fire (Second Magnitude):
8931 Effects:
8932 Base: you extinguish any flames on your body, or that you touch.
8933 Base: alternatively, you create fire equal to candlelight. The fire causes 1d4 damage. Save versus Dexterity for half.
8934 Enhance: you cause existing fire, or a flame which you create to flash in blinding light. Any creature within 5 feet, which fails its constitution saving throw is blinded for 1d4 rounds (6)
8935 Enhance: You cause existing fire to billow smoke in a 15 foot radius. The area is considered heavily obscured. You can take this effect more than one, each time adding 15 feet to the radius (2)
8936 Spells:

8974 Water (Second Magnitude):
8975 Effects:
8976 Base: You draw wholesome, drinkable water, just like clean rainwater, from the atmosphere around you, into your body. The amount of water is equal to 1 gallon, or the amount a medium creature need to sustain itself for a day. If you augment the base effect, you can manifest the water somewhere in your surroundings (depending on the enhancing effects). You cannot create water within another creature.
8977 Enhance: instead of water, you create mist in a 15’ radius which is covered in light obscurement. You can augment this effect as though you’d augment a sphere (1)
8978 Enhance: you can walk on water (8)
8979 Enhance: you drain a creature of water. Unless it succeeds on a constitution saving throw, it takes a level of exhaustion (5 per level).
8980 Enhance: if using the Base effect to create water, you can make it permanent (6)
8981 Spells:
8982 Create WaterArcana: DC 13; Range: touch; Effect: target; Duration: Instantaneous; Saving Throw: none.You draw drinkable water from the atmosphere around you and manifest it within an item you touch. The amount of water is equal to 1 gallon, or the amount a medium creature need to sustain itself for a day. Instead of vanishing when the duration runs out, the water remains until used.Math: DC 4 base, touch (+3), water permanence (+6).Create WaterArcana: DC 13; Range: touch; Effect: target; Duration: Instantaneous; Saving Throw: none.You draw drinkable water from the atmosphere around you and manifest it within an item you touch. The amount of water is equal to 1 gallon, or the amount a medium creature need to sustain itself for a day. Instead of vanishing when the duration runs out, the water remains until used.Math: DC 4 base, touch (+3), water permanence (+6).

9022 Third Magnitude Spells
9023 Time (Third Magnitude):
9024 Effects:
9025 Base: You hasten yourself and gain either +1 bonus on attack rolls, AC and Dexterity saves or increase your movement by 10’. Save versus Intelligence.
9026 Base: You slow yourself and take either a –1 penalty on attack rolls, AC, and Dexterity saves or decrease your movement by 10’. Save versus Intelligence.
9027 Enhance: choose one or more of the following: gain +1 bonus on attack rolls, a +1 bonus to AC, +1 bonus to Dexterity saves, +10’ movement (4)
9028 Enhance: choose one or more of the following: gain -1 penalty on attack rolls, a -1 penalty to AC, -1 penalty to Dexterity saves (4)
9029 Enhance: This replaces the base effect. Make yourself suspended in animation, unless you succeed on an Intelligence save (8)
9030 Enhance: This replaces the base effect. You step out of time, seemingly stopping time. While the effect is in place you can only take actions affecting yourself, but you could cast area of effect spells coming into effect at the end of the duration (24)

9069 Shape (Third Magnitude):
9070 Effects:
9071 Base: you change your appearance. You decide what you look like, including your height, weight, facial features, sound of your voice, hair length, coloration, and distinguishing characteristics, if any. Any creature within 10’, who have cause to suspect there might be wizardry involved is allowed to roll an Investigation check against your spell DC to notice something is off. If you change into a specific individual, creatures familiar with that individual is ranted an automatic Investigation check, possibly with Advantage if they are very closely connected with the individual. Your clothes and items carried do not change. If you use augmentation to cast this spell on another unwilling creature, that creature is allowed a Constitution save.
9072 Enhance: you turn into a specific creature of Beast type. The new form must have a CR equal or lower than your Proficiency bonus. Your game statistics are replaced by the statistics of the chosen creature, though you retain your alignment and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores. You also retain all of your skill and saving throw proficiencies, in addition to gaining those of the creature. You gain the creatures Strength, Dexterity and Constitution scores of the new creature. You also assume the hit points and Hit Dice of the new form, but any damage inflicted on you new form remains when the duration expires. You retain the benefit of any features from your class, race, or other source and can use them, provided that your new form is physically capable of doing so (4).
9073 Enhance: as enhance (Beast) but for Dragon type (10).
9074 Enhance: as enhance (Beast) but for Giant type (8).
9075 Enhance: as enhance (Beast) but for Monstrosity type (6).
9076 Enhance: you enlarge or reduce your size category by 1 increment. Reduce you Strength score by 2 and increase your dexterity score by 2 for each category you reduce, or reverse for enlarge (4)
9077 Spells:

9115 Life (Third Magnitude):
9116 Effects:
9117 Base: you gain 1d4 temporary HP. If using augmentation to increase the duration of this spell, calculate the total cost and divide it by 3, rounding up.
9118 Enhance: you can raise a creature from death. You need the entire corpse in order for this to work. The DC increases according to how long it has been deceased. This effect is always immediate (20, +4 per 100 years which has passed since death).
9119 Enhance: as raise (above), but you can a raise a creature from nothing but a single bone, a nail or strands of hair. This effect is always immediate and permanent (30, +5 per 100 years which has passed since death).
